# Clubhouse redesign: production-ready UI, UX and motion (paste this whole message)

You are the Claude Code session on Nick's Mac working in `helmv3`. Nick (the owner) looked at the nine redesign lanes on staging tonight with real demo data on his iPhone and on desktop. His verdict: "Like a 2 and it needs to be a 10. The whole thing." His bar is Apple. Everything below gets fixed: the look, the UX, the motion, and the numbers. Nothing in this list is optional and nothing is "polish for later".

You do every fix yourself, end to end, one lane at a time, with CI on every PR. Work autonomously; don't stop to ask unless a rule below says to.

## Hard rules (never break)
1. `git checkout main && git pull` first. Every PR branches off fresh `main`. One lane (or one foundation step) per PR. Small PRs.
2. Open each PR **ready for review, not draft**, with the fix already pushed. CI skips drafts, and the Clubhouse quality workflow (`.github/workflows/clubhouse-quality.yml`) does not re-run on later pushes. After any follow-up push run `gh workflow run clubhouse-quality.yml --ref <branch>` and wait for it. Never merge on red or on a skipped check. If something goes red, fix it on that same PR before starting the next one.
3. No deploys, no migrations run on any hosted database (staging or prod; a local Supabase is fine for tests), no feature-flag flips, no prod. Don't change what shot tracking collects.
4. Build only from the app's own components and tokens: `src/clubhouse/ui/*` (Button, Segmented, Avatar, Badge, Surface, Section, States, EmptyShape, Modal, FormSheet, Menu, ContextMenu, WeekStrip, PhoneBar, ScrollRegion, DeltaChip, ScoreMark, LargeTitle, PageHero, Toast, Ledger, SearchField, Select, Tooltip, charts/*) and `src/clubhouse/styles/tokens.css`. No new hand-drawn controls, no new hex colours, no raw px font sizes, no copying CSS from `design-refresh/**/redesign*.css` or the design HTML. If a component is missing something, extend the component (with a test), don't fork it.
5. Owner rules: phone page edge `--ch-page-x-phone` (16px, 20px on ≥414); no accent left-border cards anywhere; text follows iPhone Text Size; keep the smooth scroll; red only for under-par, errors and destructive; U+2212 minus, `E` for even, `—` for no data; 44pt tap targets; contrast ≥4.5:1 text and ≥3:1 large text, icons and edges, in light AND dark.
6. Motion: transform and opacity only, durations and easings from tokens, every transition has a reduced-motion path (180ms fade, `CH_FADE_MS`), page-behind slide 24%. **No first-paint motion** (no fly-ins, draw-ins, count-ups or staggers on load). Motion answers the user's action. That is Nick's call and it settles TEAM-33.
7. **Acceptance = the design.** Nick was promised these pages would look like the design stills in `design-refresh/<lane>/` (`redesign/*.html`, `capture/`, `screenshots/`, plus `04-FIDELITY-SPEC.md`, `06-SCREEN-SPECS.md`, `07-MOTION.md`). A page is done only when the live page, at 390x844 and at 1440x900, matches its design still side by side: layout, spacing, type, colour, hierarchy, empty/loading/error states, and the motion in 07-MOTION.md (within rule 6). Anything that doesn't look premium is a bug even if it isn't listed below. Where a design still draws a one-off control instead of the app's real one, reach the same look with the real `ui/` component (extend it if needed) and say which in the PR. Render each design HTML in the same browser at the same sizes so the comparison is fair.
8. Before calling any fix done, look at it: capture before and after with `npm run clubhouse:shots` in **WebKit at 390x844 and Chromium at 1440x900, light and dark**, and for every phone screen also WebKit at 320x568 and 430x932, at the largest Text Size, and with the keyboard up on any screen with a field (if Xcode's iOS Simulator is on the Mac, check each lane in Simulator Safari on the smallest and largest iPhone, keyboard and home-screen mode included; otherwise simulate the keyboard by shrinking `visualViewport` in the test), on the fixture preview (`/clubhouse-preview/<screen>` with the stress states below) and, where you can sign in, on staging with the demo team. Compare every screen side by side with that lane's design reference in `design-refresh/<lane>/` (the `redesign/`, `capture/` and `screenshots/` folders and `04-FIDELITY-SPEC.md` / `06-SCREEN-SPECS.md`). If it doesn't match the design at both sizes, it isn't done. Put the before/after pairs in the PR body.
9. Every PR runs locally and passes before you push: `npm run test`, `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run clubhouse:check`, `npm run clubhouse:css`, `npm run clubhouse:a11y`, `npm run clubhouse:quality`. Every bug fixed gets a test that would have caught it (unit, DOM or Playwright).
10. Step 0: check which commit the QA site (`helmv3-clubhouse-qa.vercel.app`) is built from. If it isn't current `main`, say so in your first PR body and in your report to Nick. Don't redeploy it yourself.
11. **Merge gate: side-by-side pictures on every PR.** Before a lane PR can merge, its body shows, for every screen it touches, one image per size (390x844 WebKit and 1440x900 Chromium) with three panels: design still | before | after. Show light, and dark where the design has a dark still. Most packages ship `design-refresh/<lane>/capture/capture.mjs`, which takes the design stills (`design`) and the matching app shots (`app`); use it, and `npm run clubhouse:shots` where it has no matching id, plus a small script that joins the PNGs (commit the script, not the images). Team view (`coachhelm-team`), Ask, Quick chat (`coachhelm`), Coach vs player (`coachhelm-lenses`) and Classes/Roster/Home ship no rendered stills yet, only the HTML, so run their `capture.mjs design` first. Nothing is called "done" or "matches the design" in a PR, a commit message or a report without those pictures, and if the after panel doesn't match the design panel, the PR stays open. Nick reviews the pictures, not your words.
12. **Be absurdly critical. Every subpage, every motion, every feature.** Nick's words: "Be absurdly critical", "Every subpage", "Every motion and transition", "Every feature you claimed to build".
    - Every subpage means each route, tab, segment, sheet, modal, menu, popover, drawer, detail view, editor, composer, row expansion and toast, in its empty, loading, error, partial, long-data and one-item states, on phone and desktop. Each one gets its side-by-side, not just the main page. The subpage inventory tables in `lanes/fidelity-*.md` list them; a subpage the design draws that the code doesn't render must be built.
    - Pixel fidelity: a 2px spacing miss, a different radius, a wrong weight, a wrong word or casing, an icon at the wrong size or stroke, a missing pressed or hover state is a bug. Fix it.
    - Every motion and transition in each lane's `07-MOTION.md` is built exactly (within rule 6), on every subpage, with its reduced-motion path. Every tap gets a pressed state. Nothing jump-cuts that the spec animates, and nothing animates that the spec doesn't.
    - Every feature the packages claim (02-PLAN, 05-SCREENS, 06-SCREEN-SPECS, 08-ACCEPTANCE) works end to end on real data and is reachable from the app's navigation. The feature checklist tables in `lanes/fidelity-*.md` mark what isn't. A stub, a fixture-only screen, a dead tap, a TODO or a placeholder is not done. Where a feature needs a held migration or a flag, build it fully behind that flag and list the migration in your report; don't run it.
    - When you finish a lane, re-read its `08-ACCEPTANCE.md` line by line and tick each item in the PR body with the picture that proves it.

## Why it looks vibe coded (fix these causes first; they explain most of the list)
- **SYS-1 Design-page CSS ported, not built.** Lanes copied the design HTML's CSS (private hex palettes, `overflow: visible`, fixed coordinates, fixed px widths, undefined `--ch-hf-*` tokens) instead of composing `src/clubhouse/ui`. Hand-drawn segmented controls, tabs, chips, close buttons, menus and sheets sit next to the real ones, so no two pages feel the same.
- **SYS-2 Data-driven geometry with no clamp, labels at fixed points.** Team hole halos `10 + |v|×22` (r≈96 in a 330-wide drawing), Stats green arcs, Root-cause arms (one dot per hole, up to 300), CVP sparkline and field dots. Circles cover text, labels collide and run off cards.
- **SYS-3 Cards inside cards, and legacy negative margins never reset.** The lane card rules were laid over the older cardless phone pass (`margin-inline: -12px; width: calc(100% + 24px)`), so rows sit 4px from card edges and inner cards run edge to edge with clipped shadows (Home, Ask, Team view rail, Messages).
- **SYS-4 Desktop layouts kept on phone, phone layouts shipped on desktop.** 2×2 grids at 147px columns (Home legs), 5-up chip rows, 300px fixed segmented controls; Plan format shows the phone bar on desktop at full 1100px width.
- **SYS-5 No shared page frame.** Team, Brief and Ask each have a different header, title size and switch position; empty sections stretch to equal heights with 100–200px of dead space; legends describe marks that aren't drawn; empty states are bare.
- **SYS-6 Type tokens inside SVG.** `--ch-type-*` scales with Text Size while the drawing doesn't, so chart text outgrows its padding.
- **SYS-7 The numbers disagree.** The same player shows 2, 3 and 10 rounds and +0.5 vs −6.1 strokes gained on different pages; gains and losses use the same colour; Ask ranks the team as a player and renders a failed chart as a placeholder table.
- **SYS-8 Nothing checked it.** Reviews compared code to the plan on fixtures; nobody looked at real-data-shaped values on a phone. The gate in PR 1 fixes that.

## PR order
**PR 1: Gate (land this first).** Extend `e2e/clubhouse-quality.spec.ts` (and fixtures in `src/clubhouse/preview/`) with stress states for every lane screen: the real staging values (−3.9, +2.5, +1.9, 0.0), long names ("Demo University Golf", "Saturday Spring Preview Tournament", "Wedge Distance Control (80-120 yards)"), zero data, one player, 300 holes, 40 messages. Then add assertions that run at 390x844 (WebKit) and 1440x900, light and dark, and fail the build when:
- an element's box leaves its nearest Surface/card (more than 0.5px), or text is clipped (scrollWidth > clientWidth without ellipsis);
- a Surface is nested inside a Surface;
- phone text is under 12px, or a tap target is under 44pt;
- axe contrast fails;
- a row's content sits less than 12px from its card edge;
- the page edge isn't `--ch-page-x-phone`.

Add `clubhouse:css` rules that fail on new hex colours outside tokens.css, `transition: all`, animated width/height/top/left, `overflow: visible` on chart SVG, and `margin-inline: -` inside `[data-ch-crh='on']` scopes. The gate must be red on today's `main` for TEAM-1, CRH-1, CRH-3 and CRH-4 (prove it in the PR body), then go green as each lane lands. Until a lane has landed, mark its known failures `test.fail` with the bug ID, never `skip`.

**PR 2: Shared primitives.**
- A `clampRadius`/`scaleToBox` helper and an SVG label placer that avoids collisions and keeps labels inside the viewBox (used by Team hole, Stats green, Root-cause arms, CVP charts).
- SVG text in fixed viewBox units, not type tokens.
- One CoachHelm/page header pattern (LargeTitle on phone, the desktop page hero) with the view switch always in the same place.
- Reset the legacy negative-margin rows under the lane scope.
- A Surface "no nested Surface" dev warning.

Tests for each.

**PR 3: Numbers (SYS-7, QA-1/2/3).** One windowed source for rounds and strokes gained across Roster, Team view, Stats and Home; every figure names its window; one sign convention and colour (green gains, amber losses); team excluded from player rankings; failed Ask visuals fall back to the data table or nothing. Add a cross-page consistency test on the demo team fixture.

**PR 4 onward: one lane per PR, worst first.** Team view → Home/Classes/Roster → Ask and Quick chat → Practice (Practice tab, Plan format, plan builder, Documents) → Messages → Root cause → Coach vs player → Stats → final motion check across all lanes. Each lane fixes **every** finding with that lane's prefixes below, including P2s: its own prefix (TEAM, CRH, ASK, QC, PP/PRAC, MSG, RC, CVP, STATS), its `FID-<LANE>-*` and `MOB-<LANE>-*` findings, its QA-* items, its `MOT-*` findings and every row of its subpage inventory, feature checklist and motion coverage table that isn't "matches"/"built". A lane with a lot to build (Practice: the tab, builder, drill bank, templates, print, Run view; Ask: the create flows and artifacts) may split into several small PRs in that order; the lane isn't done until all of them merge. PR 2 also lands every `MOB-SHELL-*` finding (the phone shell, tab bar, safe areas, keyboard handling, viewport and theme-color meta) and the motion fixes that live in shared code (for example MOT-01, the Reduce Motion floor that turns every fade into a cut). The final motion PR walks the whole coverage table on device size and fixes anything left. Where a lane's verdict says rebuild, rebuild that screen's layout from `ui/` components against its design reference, rather than patching CSS.

## Lane verdicts (where each stands vs a 10)
- **Team view: rebuild the hole figure and the right rail.** Clamp and auto-place the hole, rail rows on one Surface, one header with Brief/Ask. Then fix the rest.
- **Home: fix the cascade, then polish.** Reset the legacy rows and drop the nested cards; Team's game goes to one column on phone.
- **Ask and Quick chat: rebuild the answer surface.** Flat thread, one Surface per visual, real table fallback. Then the rail and composer.
- **Practice: rebuild the desktop page and Plan format shell to spec.** The phone bar must not show on desktop. Then fix the known save-after-reload/publish bug (`PlanCard.tsx:171`, `PlanTask.tsx:70`).

- **Root cause: fix the More sheet** (it uses the wrong component), dead taps and the arms cap.
- **Coach vs player: replace the private palette and hand-drawn controls with ui/.** Then fix the charts.
- **Stats: polish.** Clamp the green diagram and fix chart labels. It's the closest to the bar today.
- **Roster/Classes: polish.**
- **Also open from before:** the Ask request id ignores the team (`create-tools.ts:61`). Fix it in the Ask PR.

Build them fully behind their flag against the held N-00 migrations (010000 to 010800) and test them on a local database; don't run those migrations on staging or prod. - An iPhone in landscape gets the phone layout: the phone shell test becomes width ≤820 OR (coarse pointer AND height ≤500), in one shared helper and the matching CSS media query.
- Story viewer: hold to pause with text selection, the loupe and the callout turned off and the board behind locked; swipe down to close, like iOS.
- Ask answers on phone: keep the avatar gutter only where the Ask phone design draws it at 390; below 390 the answer body and its visuals run full width with the mark on the turn header, so charts never collapse at 320 (MOB-ASK-9).
- Rotating a phone keeps the open screen, sheet, composer text and scroll position (follows from the landscape call above).
- Text Size is applied in the early theme script before first paint, so text never jumps.
- `theme-color` and the area above the phone bar are the frame green; the overscroll area below the page matches the page background, in light and dark.

After each PR merges, reply with one line: lane, PR link, bug count fixed, and a before/after pair at 390 and 1440. When all lanes are in, send the full list of PRs and anything you couldn't fix, with the reason. Don't deploy; Nick decides that.

### Team view (34)
- **TEAM-1 Station halo radius is uncapped, so real values draw circles bigger than the hole** [P0] Team board, hole card, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamHole.tsx:51` (`const r = 10 + Math.abs(v ?? 0) * 22`), used at `:77-78` (`.pulse`, `.halo`) Wrong: The radius has no cap. With staging values, putting −3.9 gives r = 95.8 (diameter 191.6, 58% of the 330-wide viewBox); tee +2.5 gives r = 65; approach +1.9 gives r = 51.8. The putting halo's top is at y = −19.8, about 21px above the SVG, over the caption. The tee halo's bottom is at y = 419, 19 units below the 400 viewBox, so it runs about 14px into the chip row. The selected pulse scales ×1.7 to r ≈ 163 and reaches the kicker line. The spec's own radii were 15.4 to 26.2 because the fixture values were all under 0.74 (06 §S-TEAM.hole, "Station halo"). Fix: Clamp it, e.g. `const r = 10 + Math.min(Math.abs(v ?? 0), 1.3) * 14` (max 28.2), or scale against the largest station: `10 + (Math.abs(v) / maxAbs) * 18`. Set the pulse to scale 1.35, not 1.7 (`coachhelm-team.css:300`). With r ≤ 28 every halo fits: tee 354 + 28 = 382 < 400, putting 76 − 28 = 48 > 0.
- **TEAM-2 Station labels sit at fixed points inside the halos** [P0] Team hole, phone (`HOLE.v`) and desktop (`HOLE.h`), both themes. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/hole-geometry.ts:47-50` (`st.*.lx/ly`), `:80-83`; drawn at `TeamHole.tsx:80-85` (`text.sl`, `text.sv` at `ly + 21`) Wrong: Label anchors are constants, so with real halos each label starts inside its own circle. Tee label x 178 vs halo edge 215. Approach label x 146 vs edge 161.8. The putting label (252,58) is 84 units from its centre, inside r 95.8. The short-game station (198,134) and its label start (234,134) are 64 and 86 units from the putting centre, so both sit inside the putting halo. Text on the selected halo fill (amber .34 over feat) is f-ink-2 at 4.36:1 light and 4.20:1 dark, under 4.5 (H8). Desktop does the same: the putting label (664,200) sits inside r 95.8 around (664,120). Fix: Derive the label from the clamped radius, `lx = t.x + r + 8` for `a: 'start'` (desktop: `ly = t.y + r + 14`). Keep `lx/ly` only as the direction. After TEAM-1's clamp, move tee `lx` 178 → 186 (its edge lands exactly on 178).
- **TEAM-3 "Around the green" runs off the card** [P0] Team hole, phone, every Text Size (worst at XXL). File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/hole-geometry.ts:49` (short_game `lx: 234`, `a: 'start'`), `:101` (`STATION_NAME.short_game = 'Around the green'`); `coachhelm-team.css:301-304` (`.stn .sl` 13px) Wrong: The label starts at x 234 and is ≈118 units wide, so it ends near 352. The viewBox is 330, and the only thing holding it is `.feat { overflow:hidden }` at the card edge (about 350 units with the −6 margin and 16 padding). It touches the card edge at default size (seen in the screenshot). At Text Size ×1.12 it is ≈133 wide and loses "en". At ×1.35 it is ≈160 and reads "Around the g". Fix: On the hole use `STATION_SHORT` ("Short game", ≈73 units, ends ≈307), or anchor this one label `end` to the left of the station. TEAM-6 stops it growing with Text Size.
- **TEAM-4 The hole SVG draws outside its box (overflow visible plus negative margin)** [P0] Team hole, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:212-214` (`.feat .hole { margin:6px -6px 0 }`), `:215-220` (`.holesvg { overflow:visible }`), desktop `:1606-1611` (`overflow:visible`) Wrong: The ported page relied on small halos. With `overflow:visible`, anything past the viewBox paints over the header figure, the caption and the station chips. That covers TEAM-1's 21px over the caption, the 14px into the chips, and the pulse up to the kicker. The only clip left is the card's rounded edge. Fix: Set `.holesvg { overflow:hidden }` on both orientations once TEAM-1 and TEAM-2 are in. Keep the −6 margin only if nothing draws past x 0 to 330.
- **TEAM-5 The figure's 3-line caption collides with the hole** [P0] Team hole header, phone 390 and 402, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamBoard.tsx:73-80` (`.hn`, a `<br />` then "This season · N rounds · average of N players"); `coachhelm-team.css:196-211` (`.feat .hn { display:flex; align-items:flex-end; gap:12px }`) Wrong: The caption beside the 40px figure gets ≈261px at 402 and ≈249px at 390 (inner 338 or 326, minus the ≈65px "+0.5", minus the 12 gap). Line 2 is ≈320px at 14px, so it wraps and the caption is 3 lines × 19 = 57px against the 40px figure. With `align-items:flex-end` its first line rides about 17px above the figure. The last line then sits where the halo overshoot from TEAM-1 and TEAM-4 lands. Spec 06 says "2 lines beside the figure". Fix: On phone let the caption take its own row when it can't fit two lines: `.feat .hn { flex-wrap:wrap; align-items:baseline }` and `.hn span { flex:1 1 180px; min-width:0 }`. Or put "average of N players" on its own third line under the figure, not beside it. Add `margin-top:12px` on `.hole`.
- **TEAM-6 SVG labels use the --ch-type-* tokens, so they grow with Text Size while the drawing doesn't** [P1] Team hole, huddle, course trend, finger-print; phone, at any Text Size above default. File: `coachhelm-team.css:301-304` (`.stn .sl` `--ch-type-13-medium`), `:305-309` (`.stn .sv` `--ch-type-20-strong`), `:317-322` (`.scn text` `--ch-type-11-strong`), `:600-607` (`.trend text`, `.trend .lv`), `:862-871` (`.hudsvg .q`, `.axl`, `.tl`), `:884-890` (`.hp text`), `:953-956` (`.fp .lab`) Wrong: `--ch-type-k` multiplies these in SVG user units, so at ×1.35 the station value is 27 units and coin initials are 14.85 units inside an r 11 (22-wide) coin, which overflows the coin. Labels push further into halos and off the card (TEAM-3). Spec: "SVG drawings (the hole, the huddle, the trend) don't scale" (06 line 7). H4 lets SVG chart text stay pinned. Fix: Use fixed sizes in SVG: `.stn .sl { font-size:13px; font-weight:500 }`, `.stn .sv { font-size:19px; font-weight:600 }`, `.scn text { font-size:9.5px; font-weight:600 }`, and the same for `.hudsvg`, `.trend` and `.fp`. Note 11px in `.trend` and `.hudsvg` is also under H12; use 12.
- **TEAM-7 Station tab row wraps "Short game" to two lines and misaligns the row** [P1] Team hole chip row (`.stchips`), phone 390 and 402. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/StationControl.tsx:24-29`; `coachhelm-team.css:330-353` (5 columns, `gap:2px; padding:2px`; `button { padding:8px 4px; font 13 }`), `:1728-1730` Wrong: Each chip's content box is 57px at 402 and 55px at 390 ((326 − 4 − 8) / 5 − 8). "Short game" at 13/500 is ≈72px, so it breaks to 2 lines. That chip grows from ≈55px to 73px. Every other button centres its content vertically, so its label and value sit 9px lower than "Short game"'s, and the row looks broken. "Approach" (≈57px) is on the edge and wraps at Text Size ×1.12. Spec: chip 61.6×64.7, label on one line. Fix: On phone, keep the four spots as a 4-up row (≈71px of content each) and put Course as a full-width row or chip under it. Set the label to `white-space:nowrap` with `font-variation-settings:'wdth' 88` (≈63px fits), and add `align-content:start` so a taller chip can't shift the others. Also match the spec's `gap:3px; padding:3px`.
- **TEAM-8 Gains are drawn as losses on the hole and in the chips** [P1] Team hole and chip row, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: `TeamHole.tsx:77-79` (a halo for any non-null v); `coachhelm-team.css:276-284` (`.halo`/`.core` always amber f-loss), `:347-353` (`.stchips button b { color: var(--ch-tb-f-loss) }`) Wrong: Tee +2.5 and Approach +1.9 are strokes gained (the code's own sign: `worstOf` treats v < 0 as a loss). They draw the second and third biggest amber halos, and their chip values are amber. Around the green 0.0 is amber too. The biggest "leak" circles on the hole are gains. Fix: Draw halos only for v < 0, or add `.stn.is-gain .halo { fill: rgb(143 211 170/.17); stroke: var(--ch-tb-f-gain) }`. Colour the chip `<b>` by sign: f-loss below 0, f-gain above, f-ink-2 at 0.0. Use `changeTone()` from `lib/format.ts`.
- **TEAM-9 Team-average mark lands at the far loss end for a gaining station** [P1] Spot card bars and the player sheet bars, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/SpotCard.tsx:42`; `panels.tsx:280` Wrong: `left = 84 − min(|team|, 1.3) / 1.3 × 84` uses the absolute value, so Tee (+2.5) puts the dashed average at 0%, the left end of the track, which reads as a 1.3-stroke loss. Approach +1.9 does the same. Fix: `left = v < 0 ? 84 − min(|v|,1.3)/1.3×84 : 84 + min(v,0.25)/0.25×16`. Share one helper with `barScale`.
- **TEAM-10 Rank words show on every station, including gains ("+2.5 a round · smallest")** [P1] Spot card header meta, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/SpotCard.tsx:14` (`RANK`), `:78` Wrong: Every station gets a rank word from `stationOrder`. A gain reads "+2.5 a round · smallest" or "+1.9 a round · third", calling a gain a leak. Fix: `${formatSigned(v)} a round${rank === 0 && v < 0 ? ' · biggest leak' : ''}`.
- **TEAM-11 The hollow (early) coin is a dashed dark disc that reads as broken** [P1] Team hole, early players (under 5 rounds), both themes. File: `coachhelm-team.css:323-329` (`.scn.early circle { fill: var(--ch-tb-feat)!important; stroke-dasharray:3 2.4 }`; stroke from `:313-316` is f-ink .55); `TeamHole.tsx:91-92` Wrong: The early coin is a feat-green disc with a 3/2.4 dashed light ring. In a halo it looks like a broken or loading icon, which is the "CB" coin in the screenshot. The coin also lands on the bunker: putting slot (118,98) is inside the bunker ellipse at (110,100) with rx 14. Fix: `.scn.early circle { fill: var(--ch-tb-feat); stroke: currentColor; stroke-dasharray: none; stroke-width:1.5 }` with `color` set to `coinColor(id)` on the `<g>`. Move the putting coin slots off the bunker, e.g. (128,112) and (152,120).
- **TEAM-12 Stations have no 44pt hit target** [P1] Team hole, phone. File: `TeamHole.tsx:61-86` (the `g.stn role="button"` contains only the halo, the core and the text) Wrong: Around the green at 0.0 has r 10, a 20-unit (≈21px) circle. A station with no figure has no circle, so only its text can be tapped. After TEAM-1's clamp, all four halos are at most 56 units wide, and small values are about 30. Fix: Add `<circle className="hit" cx={t.x} cy={t.y} r={22} fill="transparent" />` first in each `.stn`, with `.stn .hit { pointer-events: all }`.
- **TEAM-13 Roster totals are amber even when the player gains** [P1] Roster card, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Cards.tsx:71` (adds `g` when total > 0); `coachhelm-team.css:931-934` (`.rr .tot { color: var(--ch-tb-loss) }`), with no `.rr .tot.g` rule anywhere Wrong: The `g` class has no style, so a +0.5 total is painted loss amber. A null total ("—") is amber too. Fix: Add `.ch-htb .rr .tot.g { color: var(--ch-tb-gain) }`. Use `changeTone(p.total, false)` and give "—" `--ch-tb-ink-3`.
- **TEAM-14 Huddle quadrant label runs off the card with real data, and a coin sits on it** [P1] Huddle card, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Huddle.tsx:195-197` (`x={X(as) + 6}`), `:189-194` (fixed corners) Wrong: With staging values, striking = 2.5 + 1.9 = 4.4 and scoring = −3.9. `huddleRange` widens to −4.3 to 4.7, so X(4.4) = 299 and Y(−3.9) = 211. "Strikes well, leaks scoring" (≈150 units) starts at x 305 of 320 and about 135 units of it are clipped by `.card { overflow:hidden }`. The player's coin (r 13 at 299,211) sits on its first letters, and "Behind on both" (y 212) sits on the dashed average line at y 211. Fix: Anchor the right-hand label `end` at `W − R − 4` when `X(as) > W/2`. Place quadrant labels against the plot edges, not against the average lines. Hide a label when a coin falls inside its box.
- **TEAM-15 Story viewer ignores safe areas: progress bar under the Dynamic Island, buttons under Safari's bar** [P1] "This week" story viewer, phone (iOS Safari). File: `coachhelm-team.css:1244-1256` (`.story { padding:48px var(--ch-tb-edge) 32px }`), `:1826-1829` (`position:fixed`) Wrong: The viewer is full screen and fixed, with a fixed 48px top and 32px bottom. The iPhone 16 Pro top inset is about 62px, and the bottom has a 34px home indicator plus Safari's floating toolbar (visible in the owner's screenshot). So the progress bar and close button sit under the status bar, and "Open the read" / "Next" sit under the toolbar. There is no `env(safe-area-inset-*)` anywhere in the rule. Fix: `padding: calc(env(safe-area-inset-top) + 12px) var(--ch-tb-edge) calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 34px)`.
- **TEAM-16 Close buttons are 34×34 with no extra hit area** [P1] Read, player, course and gone sheets, desktop inspector, story viewer. File: `coachhelm-team.css:1123-1133` (`.xb { width:34px; height:34px }`); used at `panels.tsx:34`, `Stories.tsx:163`, `:307` Wrong: The visible size is the tap size, 34×34. Fix: `.xb { position:relative } .xb::after { content:""; position:absolute; inset:-5px }`. Or use `ui/Button` `ch-iconbtn`.
- **TEAM-17 Phone sheet content is inset 32px, double the spec** [P1] Read, player and course sheets (ui/Modal), phone. File: `coachhelm-team.css:1788-1790` (`.ch-htb-sheet .ch-modal__body { padding-inline:16px }`, keeping `controls.css:470-475`'s 16 top and 20 to 24 bottom) plus `:1101-1105` (`.ins { padding: 8px var(--ch-tb-edge) 40px }`) Wrong: Body padding and `.ins` padding stack. Content sits 32px from each side (cards 326 wide at 390 instead of 358), 24px from the top and 60 to 64px from the bottom. Fix: `.ch-htb-sheet .ch-modal__body { padding:0 }` and let `.ins` own the inset.
- **TEAM-18 Desktop roster: the "Loses most" badge is wider than its column and overlaps the finger-print** [P1] Roster card, desktop 1440, both themes. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Cards.tsx:67`; `coachhelm-team.css:1634-1636` (`grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0,1fr) 112px auto 64px`); Badge is `white-space:nowrap` (`ui.css:291-301`) Wrong: "Loses most: Short game" at 12/600 plus 16 padding is ≈160px in a 112px track. It spills about 48px over the 78px finger-print. Fix: Change the column to `minmax(112px, max-content)`, or shorten the badge to the spot name alone ("Short game") with the header saying "Loses most".
- **TEAM-19 Hidden sheet header still holds a focusable Close and the dialog's label "Details"** [P2] Phone sheets (ui/Modal with `.ch-htb-sheet`). File: `coachhelm-team.css:1781-1787` (visually hides `.ch-modal__head`); `TeamBoard.tsx:380` (`title="Details"`) Wrong: The clipped header keeps the Modal's own Close button in the tab order as a 1px invisible stop, next to the panel's `.xb`. The dialog is labelled "Details" and focus goes to that hidden "Details" heading, not the panel title. Fix: Pass the panel's real title to `Modal`, and add a prop to drop the Modal header instead of clipping it. Or point `initialFocus` at `.insh b`.
- **TEAM-20 Under Reduce Motion the busy spinners stop** [P2] Assign or Undo busy states in the sheets, phone and desktop. File: `coachhelm-team.css:1683-1690` (`.ch-htb * { animation-duration:1ms!important; animation-iteration-count:1!important }`) Wrong: Every animation under `.ch-htb` is forced to one 1ms run, including the shared Button spinner (`ch-spinner-turn`), so a busy button shows a frozen arc. Fix: Exclude the spinner: `.ch-htb :where(*:not(.ch-spinner, .ch-btn__spinner))`, or set `animation-iteration-count` only on named classes.
- **TEAM-21 Course sheet header shows a raw ISO date** [P2] Course sheet (tap a course row), phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/panels.tsx:344` (``line={`${row.rounds} rounds this season · last ${row.last}`}``) Wrong: `row.last` is a `YYYY-MM-DD` string; `CourseCard.tsx:93` formats it with `day()`, but the sheet prints it as is ("last 2026-10-26"). Fix: Export `day` from `CourseCard.tsx` and use `day(row.last)`.
- **TEAM-22 0.0 is painted green in bars and sheets** [P2] Spot card bars, player sheet bars. File: `SpotCard.tsx:36` (`loss = v < 0`), `:45`; `panels.tsx:284` (`v >= 0 ? ' g'`) Wrong: A player at exactly 0.0 (Around the green on staging) gets the gain colour on the value and a gain fill of length 0. Fix: Use `changeTone(v, false)` and `--ch-tb-ink-2` for ''.
- **TEAM-23 Hyphen as minus in story and comparison figures** [P2] Story viewer (focus before and now), read-sheet comparison ladder. File: `Stories.tsx:73`, `:78`, `:81` (`formatFixed(p.from|now|target)`); `panels.tsx:46` (`formatFixed(v, …)`); `formatFixed` is `value.toFixed()` (`lib/format.ts:88-91`) Wrong: A negative strokes-gained focus value prints "-0.4" with U+002D. Fix: Use `formatSigned` for signed figures, or make `formatFixed` swap in `MINUS`.
- **TEAM-24 Huddle's rotated axis label is as long as the plot at default size and spills past it when larger** [P2] Huddle card, phone. File: `Huddle.tsx:201-203` (`.axl` rotated); `coachhelm-team.css:865-868` (`--ch-type-12-medium`) Wrong: "Scoring: short game and putting" is ≈205 units against a plot height of 204 (T 16 to 220). At ×1.12 it is ≈230 and overflows top and bottom, with `.hudsvg { overflow:visible }` (`:842-847`), toward the card header. Fix: Use fixed 11.5 to 12px (see TEAM-6), shorten to "Scoring: short game, putting", and set `overflow:hidden`.
- **TEAM-25 Huddle coins: 28px tap targets, and low-contrast edges in dark** [P2] Huddle card, phone; dark theme. File: `Huddle.tsx:220` (`r={13}`, fill and stroke both `coinColor`); `TeamHole.tsx:11` (`TONE_FILL`) Wrong: The hit target is a 26-unit circle (≈28px). In dark, the sage, mist and heather coins against the card (#191E1A) measure 2.69, 2.66 and 2.53:1, under the 3:1 floor for marks. Fix: Add an invisible r 22 hit circle per coin. In dark, give coins a 1.5 ring in `--ch-tb-f-ink`/`--ch-tb-ink` at 0.5 or lift the tone fills.
- **TEAM-26 Team against Tour grid is 3-up on phone and its cells wrap** [P2] Course tab, phone 390. File: `coachhelm-team.css:684-713` (`.shape` 3 columns; `.sv` 20/600; `.st` 12) Wrong: Cells are ≈95px of content. "Not measured yet" at 20/600 (≈170px) breaks onto 2 to 3 heavy lines, and "a hole · Tour +0.00" (≈115px at 12) wraps. Rows end at different heights. Fix: Show "—" (H14) instead of "Not measured yet" in `.sv`. Allow a 2-up grid under 400px, or give `.st` `white-space:nowrap; text-overflow:ellipsis`.
- **TEAM-27 Spot axis "Tour" label is not under the zero line** [P2] Spot card and player sheet axis, phone 390. File: `coachhelm-team.css:459-468` (`.axis`, `span:last-child { padding-right:40px }`) Wrong: The zero line is at 84% of the track, about x 255 in a 358 card. "Tour" is pushed right by a fixed 40px pad, so its centre is about x 287, roughly 30px off its line. The gap changes with Text Size (`92 × k` name column). Fix: Put the axis in the same grid as `.br` (`92k / 1fr / 52`) and absolutely position "Tour" at `left: 84%` of the track cell with `translateX(-50%)`.
- **TEAM-28 Skeleton doesn't match the loaded frame (layout jump)** [P2] Team board loading, phone and desktop. File: `TeamBoardSkeleton.tsx:35` (`CoachHelmTabs` outside `.sheet`) vs `TeamBoard.tsx:363-365` (tabs inside `.sheet`); `:38` This week 120 tall; `:46-61` desktop has no title or tabs Wrong: On phone the tabs move from full width to the 16px page edge when data lands, and "This week" grows from 120 to about 227. The desktop skeleton has no header row, so everything shifts down by the title and tab height. Fix: Render the tabs inside `.sheet` as the board does. Use the 06 heights. Add the `ch-htb-deskhead` with `LargeTitleSkeleton` on desktop.
- **TEAM-29 Failure notices are missing their icon tile (only This week has it)** [P2] Working on failed, Track record unavailable, hole failed, player with no rounds. File: `Cards.tsx:118-127`, `:170-175`; `TeamBoard.tsx:257-267`; `panels.tsx:250-255` (no `.ni`); compare `Stories.tsx:222-225` Wrong: The same notice is drawn two ways: with the 34px warning tile on This week and as bare text everywhere else, so those read as plain paragraphs. Fix: Make one `Notice` part (or use `ui/States`), with the `.ni` tile, and use it in all five places.
- **TEAM-30 Course "Try again" is a bare inline text button with no height** [P2] Course tab failed state. File: `CourseCard.tsx:124`; `coachhelm-team.css:1768-1771` (`.more.is-inline { min-height:0; padding:0 }`) Wrong: The tap target is the text line, about 19px tall. Fix: Use `ui/Button variant="ghost" size="sm"` as the other notices do.
- **TEAM-31 The same station has two names on one card** [P2] Team hole (label) vs chip row and spot header. File: `hole-geometry.ts:101-102` (`STATION_NAME.short_game = 'Around the green'`, `STATION_SHORT = 'Short game'`); used at `TeamHole.tsx:81`, `StationControl.tsx:29`, `SpotCard.tsx:76` Wrong: The hole says "Around the green" and the chip under it says "Short game" for the same spot. The roster key says "short game". Fix: Use "Short game" on the hole, which also fixes TEAM-3's width, or "Around the green" everywhere with a 2-line-safe chip.
- **TEAM-32 Story tile picture is invented for read and theme stories** [P2] This week tiles, phone and desktop. File: `Stories.tsx:38-44` (fixed bars 70 and 90 wide) Wrong: Any story that isn't a focus draws the same two bars (70, then 90) in gain green, implying a measured improvement that isn't in the data. "Slipping" only swaps the colour. Fix: Draw the round's 18-hole strip for a read story (the data is in `storyRounds`), and a neutral glyph for a theme. Or draw no picture.
- **TEAM-33 First-paint fly-in and trend draw-in conflict with the rubric** [P2] Team hole first paint each session; Course tab trend. File: `team-motion.ts:14-86` (ball flight, stations pop in), `:128-143` (line draw, dots staggered 55ms) Wrong: The lane spec asks for these (M-TEAM-01 to 04, 13 to 15), but RUBRIC H2 fails "chart draw-ins or first-paint staggers (Q-153)". The fly-in also adds `.pre` to stations, which hides the halos and labels for about 2.2s after load. Fix: Ask the owner. If H2 wins, drop `useFlyIn` and `useTrendDraw`.
- **TEAM-34 Dead keyframe references (a drop-in trap)** [P2] CSS only. File: `coachhelm-team.css:531` (`animation:fadein`), `:998` (`popin`), `:1228` (`draw`, with `stroke-dashoffset:24` at `:1226-1227`), `:1237` (`spin`), `:1331` (`storyin`); the keyframes are named `ch-htb-*` (`:838`, `:1000`, `:1230`, `:1333`) Wrong: None of these names exist, so the animations never run. `.assigned path` would draw an invisible check if the class were used, and `.spin` would not turn. They are unused in the TSX today. Fix: Delete the dead rules, or rename them to the `ch-htb-*` keyframes.

### Classes, Roster, Home (35)
- **CRH-1 Latest rounds rows have no inner inset (avatar ~4px from the card edge, score chip flush right)** [P0] Coach Home phone, Latest rounds, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/home.css:3293-3296` (`.ch-hm-rd { width: calc(100% + 24px); margin-inline: -12px; padding-inline: 12px }`), not reset by the lane rule `home.css:4823-4826` (`.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-rd { padding: 8px 16px }`). The card clips at `home.css:4617-4623` (`:is(.ch-hm-sec, .ch-hm-form) { overflow: hidden }`). Markup: `screens/home/HomePhone.tsx:528-560`. Wrong: the lane rule restores 16px padding but leaves the −12px margins and the +24px width. The row starts 12px outside the card and the card clips it, so the avatar sits 16 − 12 = 4px from the card edge. The score well's right edge is also 4px from the card edge. The screenshot shows about 3 to 5pt on both sides. The pressed tint is clipped the same way. Fix: in the lane block, add `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-rd { width: 100%; margin-inline: 0; padding: 10px 16px; border-radius: 0; }`. Make the separators inset: `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-list li + li { box-shadow: none; position: relative }` plus a `::before` hairline at `inset: 0 0 auto 60px` (`--ch-separator`, `--ch-hairline-w`), the way `.ch-dh-rows li + li::before` (`home.css:5121-5127`) already does on desktop.
- **CRH-2 Today card rows on coach Home have the same 4px inset** [P0] Coach Home phone, Today card (any day with events), light and dark. File: `home.css:3264-3266` (`.ch-hm-tl__a { margin-inline: -12px; padding-inline: 12px }`) against the lane rule `home.css:4650-4656` (`padding: 8px 16px` and no margin reset). The rows are rendered by `HomePhone.tsx:303-329` (`Today card`) inside the card at `home.css:4617`. Wrong: this is the same cascade as CRH-1. The time column and the Clash or Next chip start 4px inside the card's clipped edge. The row hairlines (`.ch-hm-tl__r + .ch-hm-tl__r`, `home.css:3089-3091`) also run edge to edge. Fix: add `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-tl__a { margin-inline: 0; border-radius: 0; padding: 10px 16px }` and inset the `.ch-hm-tl__r + .ch-hm-tl__r` rule 16px.
- **CRH-3 "The team's game" stays two columns on the phone, and every line wraps every two words** [P0] Coach Home phone at 390 (and worse at 375, 340 or larger Text Size), light and dark. File: `home.css:4740-4746` (`.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-legs__grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr }`) and `:4747-4753` (cell `padding: 12px 16px`). Markup: `screens/home/TeamLegs.tsx:220-238` (`PeopleLine`, `:185-199`). Wrong: the card is 390 − 32 = 358px wide, so each cell is 179px with a 147px text column. 15px secondary text gives about 18 characters a line. The line "Tyler H. and 2 others gain most · Ethan P. and 2 others lose most" breaks into 4 to 5 lines (screenshot). "No clear change vs previous 10" (13/600) and "Fairways 61% · Tour 66%" both take 2 lines. The `·` separator lands at the start of a line ("·Ethan P."). The two cells in a row end at different heights, so the hairline grid reads ragged. Fix: at ≤820 use one column, `grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr)`. Each leg becomes one row: label and SG figure on one line (`justify-content: space-between`), the diverging bar full width under it, the stat and change on one line, then "gains most" and "loses most" each on their own line (split `PeopleLine` into two spans with `display: block`). Drop the `:nth-child(odd)` right hairline (`:4763-4765`) and use a 16px-inset top hairline between rows. If 2×2 has to stay for the figures, keep only label, figure and bar in the grid and move the stat, change and people lines to a full-width list under it.
- **CRH-4 "This week" is a card nested in a card; the strip runs to the outer card's edges and the event line sits 2px from the edge** [P0] Coach Home phone (also the coach first run with events, `CoachHome.tsx:201`), light and dark. File: `home.css:4972-4980` (`.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-week { background: var(--ch-card-bg); box-shadow: var(--ch-card-shadow); border-radius: var(--ch-radius-lg); padding: 8px 8px 6px }`) inside the section card `home.css:4617-4623`. Note line: `home.css:1501-1505` (`.ch-hm-week__n { padding: 0 2px }`). Section gap: `home.css:3065-3067` (`gap: 6px`). Markup: `HomePhone.tsx:437-465`, `ui/WeekStrip.tsx:93-100`. Wrong: the outer card has no padding, so the inner strip card spans the full 358px. It has its own radius and lifted shadow (`--ch-elevation-lifted` casts about 60px), and the outer `overflow: hidden` cuts that shadow off. The header text sits at 16px while the strip's card edge sits at 0. "Saturday Spring Preview Tournament" sits 2px from the card's left edge and on its bottom edge, because the section has no bottom padding. Fix: in the lane, delete `background`, `box-shadow` and `border-radius` from `.ch-hm-week` and set `padding: 10px 10px 6px`. Give `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-week__n` `padding: 12px 16px 14px` with a top `--ch-separator` hairline inset 16. Set `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-sec { gap: 0 }`. Better still, render This week through `Surface variant="card" padded={false} footerNote={…}` like The team's game, and add the `Calendar` link in `actions`.
- **CRH-5 Coach first-run Home: "Waiting on you" capsules are ivory text on the ivory page (1.01:1)** [P0] Coach Home phone, first run (team with no players or rounds yet but with a join request or a reply waiting), light. File: `screens/home/CoachHome.tsx:200` renders `<CoachQueue phone />` in `.ch-hm-body`, not in the green hero. The lane rules `home.css:4561-4581` paint `.ch-hm-queue__k` in `--ch-on-green-2` and `.ch-hm-queue__a` in `--ch-on-green-plate` (#f4f1e8) on `rgb(255 255 255/.1)`. Wrong: on `--ch-grouped-bg` #f2f0e9 both the key and the capsules measure 1.01:1, so they are invisible. These are the coach's join requests, the first thing a new coach needs to act on. Fix: move `<CoachQueue … phone />` into the first-run `<header className="ch-hm-hero">` after the brief, as `HomePhone.tsx:86` does. Or scope the capsule rules to `.ch-hm-hero .ch-hm-queue*`, so that in the body the queue falls back to the ink capsules at `home.css:4361-4402`.
- **CRH-6 "Since you last looked" is a card inside a card, and its rows are 4px from the inner card edge** [P1] Coach Home phone, a day with events and changes since last visit, light and dark. File: `home.css:5233-5238` (`[data-ch-crh='on'] :is(.ch-hm-later .ch-hm-tl, .ch-hm-since__l) { padding-inline: 16px; background: var(--ch-card-bg); box-shadow: var(--ch-card-shadow); border-radius }`) applied inside the section card from `home.css:4617` (`DayCard.tsx:400-406`, `section.ch-hm-sec.ch-hm-since > LedgerList.ch-hm-since__l`). The rows are `ui.css:818-821` (`.ch-lgr__row.is-link { margin-inline: -12px; width: calc(100% + 24px) }`). Wrong: this is the nested-card pattern again. The list card spans the outer card edge to edge, its lifted shadow is clipped by the outer `overflow: hidden`, and the Ledger rows' −12px margin puts their content 16 − 12 = 4px inside the inner card. Fix: drop `.ch-hm-since__l` from the `:5233` selector, because its parent is already the card. Add `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-since .ch-lgr__row.is-link { margin-inline: 0; width: 100%; padding-inline: 16px; border-radius: 0 }`.
- **CRH-7 Team scoring card: the sparkline runs edge to edge, there is an extra 16px above the title, and a stray rule sits at the bottom** [P1] Coach Home phone, Team scoring, light and dark. File: `home.css:1447-1451` and `:3145-3147` (`.ch-hm-form__svg { width: 100%; margin: 16px 0 16px }`). `home.css:3114-3117` (`.ch-hm-form { padding-top: 16px; border-top: … }`): the lane resets the border at `:4617-4623` but not the padding. `home.css:3148-3152` (`.ch-hm-form__figs { border-bottom: … ledger-rule }`): the lane `:4732-4738` sets only `border-top`. Wrong: the card has no side padding, so the line's first point is about 4px from the left edge and the end dot (r 3.6 at x=316/320) touches the right edge. The "Team scoring" head sits 28px from the card top while every sibling card's head sits 12px down. A parchment ledger rule draws along the card's bottom edge under the figures. Fix: in the lane add `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-form { padding: 0 }`, `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-form__svg { width: calc(100% - 32px); margin: 12px 16px 8px }` and `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-form__figs { border-bottom: 0 }`.
- **CRH-8 Player names in "The team's game" are colour-only links on the phone** [P1] Coach Home phone, The team's game, light and dark. File: `home.css:4466-4472` (`.ch-hm-legs__a { color: var(--ch-green-600); text-decoration: none }`). The lane's `:4760-4762` only removes padding. Desktop has the underline (`home.css:5385-5392`). Wrong: in-text links are marked by green alone, so in greyscale "Tyler H." reads as plain text. Fix: copy the desktop rule into the phone lane block: `text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-thickness: 1px; text-underline-offset: 2px; text-decoration-color: color-mix(in srgb, currentcolor 45%, transparent)`.
- **CRH-9 The team's game name links are about 20px tall tap targets** [P1] Coach Home phone, light and dark. File: `home.css:4760-4762` (`.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-legs__a { padding: 0 }`) cancels the 12px vertical padding from `:4466-4471` that the hit-area `::after` (`:4475-4479`, `inset: -1px …`) relied on. Wrong: the target is one 15px line (about 20px) plus 2px. Adjacent names in a wrapped people line are about 20px apart, so taps land on the wrong player. Fix: keep `padding: 0` for layout but set `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-legs__a::after { inset: -12px min(0px, calc(50% - 22px)) }`. With CRH-3's one-column layout, put each people line on its own row so the 44px areas don't overlap.
- **CRH-10 The team's game notes and notices sit flush on the card edge** [P1] Coach Home phone, The team's game; states "nobody ranked" (CH-2315), up-and-down figures failed (CH-2218), Tour marks failed (CH-2219). Wrong: `padded={false}` removes the body padding. The rows bring their own 16px, but the note and the RefreshNotice blocks don't, so "No player has 3 rounds with hole scores yet…" starts at x=0 of the card. Fix: pass the note as `footerNote` (the CH-2315 sentence replaces the "cost the most" note when `!legs.ranked`). Wrap `<Notices>` in a `div` with `padding: 12px 16px 0`.
- **CRH-11 The week strip's event dots are 2.17:1** [P1] Coach and player Home phone (This week), Hub and Calendar strips (shared `ui/WeekStrip`), light (dark 2.39:1). File: `src/clubhouse/styles/ui.css:2029-2034` (`.ch-wks__d i span { background: var(--ch-ink-300) }`) Wrong: ink-300 #b3afa5 on the card #fffefb measures 2.17:1. In dark, #5c584f on #191e1a measures 2.39:1. The dots are the only sign a day has events (the label is screen-reader only). Fix: `background: var(--ch-green-500)` (or `--ch-text-tertiary`). Keep `--ch-on-green-ink-soft` for `.is-on`.
- **CRH-12 The week strip tile has a fixed 64px height that its own content overflows** [P1] Every WeekStrip (Home phone, Hub, Calendar), worst at larger Text Size, light and dark. File: `ui.css:1964-1971` (`.ch-wks__d { height: 64px; padding: 12px 0 0; gap: 6px }`). Contents: `em` 12px, `b` 17px at `--ch-lead-tight` 1.1, `i` 11px. Wrong: at the default size the content is 12 + 13.2 + 6 + 18.7 + 6 + 11 = 66.9px in a 64px box. At XXL (k = 1.36) it is about 78px. The trophy mark and the dots fall below the today ring, into the strip's bottom padding or the note line. Fix: replace `height: 64px` with `min-height: calc(64 * var(--ch-type-k))` and `padding: 10px 0 9px`, as `.week div` does.
- **CRH-13 Leader cards and the profile's year chip never get their year colour** [P1] Roster phone and desktop (leaders row), player profile head (year chip), light and dark. File: `roster.css:3824-3835` sets `--ch-rs-y` only for `:is(.ch-rs-mix, .ch-rs-mix__lg) [data-year=…]`. `RosterLeaders.tsx:101` puts `data-year` on `.ch-rs-ldr` (outside `.ch-rs-mix`). `RosterProfileCrh.tsx` puts `data-year` on `.ch-rs-ychip`. Fallbacks: `roster.css:2953` and `:2974` (`--ch-chart-flat`), `:3468-3469` and `:3477` (`--ch-accent-ink`). Wrong: every leader card gets the same neutral tint, ring and icon colour whatever the player's year. The year chip is always green, even for a Sophomore whose coin is another colour. Fix: widen the selectors to `[data-ch-crh='on'] [data-year='sr'] { --ch-rs-y: var(--ch-yr-sr) }` (and the same for jr, so and fr), or add `.ch-rs-ldr, .ch-rs-ychip` to the `:is()`.
- **CRH-14 Binders after the first lose their tone ring and lift on the phone, and a pressed binder loses its tone fill** [P1] Classes phone, Your classes deck, light and dark. File: `classes.css:1644-1647` (`.ch-cl.is-phone .ch-cl-card + .ch-cl-card { box-shadow: inset 0 var(--ch-hairline-w) 0 var(--ch-ledger-rule-soft) }`, specificity 0,4,0) beats the lane's `classes.css:3040-3049` (`[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-cl-deck .ch-cl-card { box-shadow: var(--ch-classes-shadow-2) }`, 0,3,0). Also `:1648-1650` (`[data-ch-pressed] { background: var(--ch-ledger-row-press) }`, 0,4,0) beats the lane's `background: var(--ch-cl-t)`. Wrong: the first binder has its tone ring and lift. Every later one shows a 1px parchment rule along its top inside edge and no ring. On press, the coloured binder turns parchment. Fix: in the lane block add `[data-ch-crh='on'].ch-cl.is-phone .ch-cl-card + .ch-cl-card { box-shadow: var(--ch-classes-shadow-2) }` and `[data-ch-crh='on'].ch-cl.is-phone .ch-cl-card[data-ch-pressed] { background: var(--ch-cl-t); transform: scale(.97) }`, or scope the old phone rules to `:not([data-ch-crh='on'])`.
- **CRH-15 Classes day-ribbon axis labels don't sit at their hours** [P1] Classes phone, Today card on a day off ("No classes today", ribbon of the next class day), light and dark. File: `screens/classes/ClassesCrh.tsx:204-209` (four spans "7 AM", "10", "1 PM", "5 PM") with `classes.css:3000-3006` (`display: flex; justify-content: space-between`). Blocks are placed by `ribbonBox` (`ClassesCrh.tsx:182-186`) on a 7 AM to 5 PM scale. Wrong: on that scale 10 AM is at 30% and 1 PM at 60%. `space-between` spreads the four labels evenly: on a 326px ribbon "10" centres at about 34% and "1 PM" at about 64%. A 1:00 PM class block starts visibly left of the "1 PM" label. The labels are at fixed points that don't match the data. Fix: position each label absolutely at its fraction (`left: 30%` and `60%`, `transform: translateX(-50%)`; first `left: 0`, last `right: 0`), with `position: relative` on the axis.
- **CRH-16 The team-event block on the ribbon and the team row dot are 1.82:1** [P1] Classes phone, Today card (ribbon and rows), light. Fix: use `--ch-green-600` (or `--ch-chart-gain`) for the light theme, and keep mint for dark (`html[data-fw-theme='dark']`).
- **CRH-17 Dark mode: a parchment double rule draws across the top of the coach Home green hero** [P1] Coach Home phone, dark. Fix: add `border-top: 0` to `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-hero__date`.
- **CRH-18 Term card "Soon" stays two columns on the phone** [P1] Classes phone, term card, light and dark. File: `classes.css:2872-2879` (`[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-cl-term__soon { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr }`), `ClassesCrh.tsx:113-120` Wrong: the green card is 358 − 32 = 326px, so each column is 157px. Real event labels from `eventLabel` ("Saturday Spring Preview Tournament", "Travel to Demo University Golf Invitational") at 15px wrap to 2 or 3 lines beside a one-line "Term ends". This is the same two-columns-on-phone pattern as CRH-3. Fix: `grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr)` at ≤820 (keep 2 or 4 columns on desktop, `classes.css:3346-3349`). Lay each item out as date then label on one row: `display: flex; gap: 12px`, with the `dt` at a fixed 58×k width like the time column.
- **CRH-19 Player Home (phone) is still the pre-redesign ledger page** [P2] Player Home phone, light and dark. File: `screens/home/PlayerHomePhone.tsx` (no `crh` prop, no `data-ch-crh`). It draws `.ch-hm-sec` sections with double rules and parchment rows (`home.css:3226-3382`, `3390+`). Wrong: the coach's Home is grouped cards on `--ch-grouped-bg`. The player's is cardless parchment with engraved rules, champagne and the old Up next. Roster and Classes (the player's other tabs) are already on the new cards, so Home is the odd one out on a player's phone. Fix: carry the lane's card treatment to `PlayerHomePhone` behind the same flag (reuse `CrhFailedCard`, the `Surface` sections and `TeamLegsPhone`'s pattern), or record it as out of scope in DECISIONS.md.
- **CRH-20 The SG bars grow on every mount, at 280ms** [P2] Coach Home phone and desktop, The team's game. File: `home.css:4806` and `:5340` (`animation: ch-hm-leg-grow var(--ch-dur-release) var(--ch-ease) both`) Wrong: the animation runs whenever the card mounts: returning to Home, a section retry, the day rollover refresh. It is a chart draw-in each time, not "once when Home is first shown". The duration is 280ms against the spec's 360ms. Fix: gate it with a session flag (`data-ch-grown` set after the first paint via `useChSessionState`) and use a 360ms token, or drop it per H2.
- **CRH-21 "Team stats" can be a dead `#` link** [P2] Coach Home phone and desktop, Team scoring head and failed cards. File: `HomePhone.tsx:117` and `:391` (`rebuiltHref('/golf/dashboard/stats') ?? '#'`), `CoachHome.tsx:95` and `:111` Wrong: when Stats or Roster isn't rebuilt for the role, the link renders as `href="#"`. A tap jumps to the top and does nothing. Fix: render the link only when `rebuiltHref` returns a value (as `Rounds` does at `HomePhone.tsx:515`), and make `CrhFailedCard`'s `href` optional.
- **CRH-22 Latest rounds row type, coin and separators are off spec** [P2] Coach Home phone, Latest rounds. File: `HomePhone.tsx:543` (`Avatar size={38}`); `home.css:1551-1564` (name 15/600, meta 13 tertiary); `home.css:3289-3291` (full-width separator); `home.css:2476-2478` (`.ch-hm-score em { font-size: 12px }`, fixed) Wrong: the coin is 38 against the spec's 40. The to-par line is a fixed 12px against the 13 caption. Fix: `Avatar size="md"`; in the lane block `.ch-hm-rd__b b { font: var(--ch-type-row) }`, `.ch-hm-rd__b span { font: var(--ch-type-meta); color: var(--ch-text-secondary) }`, `.ch-hm-score em { font: var(--ch-type-caption) }`; separators as in CRH-1.
- **CRH-23 This week: no Calendar link, and competition days lack the design's green tile** [P2] Coach Home phone, This week. File: `HomePhone.tsx:440-442` (head without a link); `ui/WeekStrip.tsx:110-111` (a trophy icon only) Wrong: the design head has "Calendar" on the right. Competition days are drawn as `.week .major { background: var(--hero) }` with mint text (`redesign.css:471-473`). The app shows a small ink-secondary trophy. Fix: add `<Link className="ch-hm-link" href={CALENDAR}>Calendar</Link>` in the head. Add an `is-major` tile style under `.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-hm-week li.is-major .ch-wks__d` (`--ch-frame` fill, `--ch-hero-mint` letter). The `li` already carries `is-major`.
- **CRH-24 The leaders scroller cuts off the cards' lift shadow** [P2] Roster phone, leaders row, light. File: `roster.css:3018-3028` (`overflow-x: auto; padding: 2px … 6px`) with the cards' `--ch-roster-x3`, which includes `--ch-elevation-lifted` (`tokens.css:570-572`, cast to about 60px below) Wrong: `overflow-x: auto` forces vertical clipping. Only 6px of shadow shows, so the cards look sliced flat at the bottom. Fix: use `--ch-elevation-reading`, or the ring plus `0 1px 2px` only, on `.ch-rs-ldr` on the phone. Alternatively, give the scroller `padding-bottom: 16px; margin-bottom: -10px`.
- **CRH-25 Roster coin initials are 11px** [P2] Roster phone, team card top-five coins and leader coins. Fix: set `--ch-av-type: 12px` (or `calc(12 * min(var(--ch-type-k), 1.2px))` as `:3126` does).
- **CRH-26 Binder week tiles keep the old champagne "today" and grey "team" fills** [P2] Classes phone and desktop, binders, light and dark. Neither is in the design (`.wk` today ringed only). Fix: `[data-ch-crh='on'] .ch-cl-deck .ch-cl-card__week > span:is(.is-today, .is-team) { background: none }`, then restore `.is-on`'s selected tint.
- **CRH-27 Ribbon: a class that is wholly before 7 AM or after 5 PM disappears** [P2] Classes phone, Today card ribbon. - **CRH-29 Coach Home Today time column is 48px with nowrap** [P2] Coach Home phone, Today card, larger Text Size or "All day". File: `home.css:4657-4661` (`width: calc(48 * var(--ch-type-k)); white-space: nowrap`) Wrong: "All day" at 15/600 is about 50px, and 24-hour times with a minute are close to that. - **CRH-30 Player Roster: the sort bar sits above the Coaches card it doesn't sort** [P2] Roster phone, player view. - **CRH-31 Desktop "The team's game" squeezes into four 148px columns at 821-1100** [P2] Coach Home desktop, narrow windows or iPad landscape. - **CRH-32 "Names open that player's game on Stats" shows when names aren't links** [P2] Coach Home phone, The team's game footer. - **CRH-33 The Classes deck is wrapped in `.ch-cl-deck` twice on the phone** [P2] Classes phone. File: `ClassesView.tsx:475` (`<div className="ch-cl-deck">{deck}</div>`) around `:323` (the deck's own `.ch-cl-deck`) Wrong: the grid sits inside a grid, so the lane's `gap: 12px` applies twice and "Delete all classes" spaces differently from the binders. The deck-scoped selectors (`.ch-cl-deck .ch-cl-danger`) also match more widely than intended. Fix: render `{deck}` directly as desktop does (`:487`).
- **CRH-34 Join requests badge digit sits high in its pill** [P2] Roster phone and desktop, Join requests row. File: `roster.css:3084-3095` and `:3544-3555` (`height: 24px`, `text-align: center`, no `line-height` or flex centring; 13/18 type) Wrong: the numeral rides about 3px above centre. Fix: `display: inline-grid; place-items: center; line-height: 1`.
- **CRH-35 The term card's "now" knob is a hard-coded colour** [P2] Classes phone and desktop, term line. File: `classes.css:2868` (`background: #f4eedd`) Wrong: a raw hex where `--ch-on-green-ink` exists. The colour is right in both themes today because it sits on green, but it won't follow a token change. Fix: `background: var(--ch-on-green-ink)`.

### Ask (28)
- **ASK-1 Desktop artifact pane renders under the conversation as raw JSON** [P0] thread with an opened artifact (draft plan, lineup, message), desktop 1440, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Ask.tsx:400; src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:134 (`.ch-ak-pane`) Wrong: `<ArtifactView>` is rendered as a sibling after `.ch-ask-body` inside `main.ch-ask` (a flex column), not inside the body row. So the 452px pane drops below the thread, left-aligned, and pushes the dock off-screen. Its body is `<pre className="ch-ak-plain">{JSON.stringify(b, null, 2)}</pre>`, and `.ch-ak-plain` has no CSS anywhere. The coach sees monospace JSON in an unstyled pre. Fix: render `ArtifactView` inside the `.ch-ask-body` flex row, next to the thread column. Pass the real artifact renderer (the same TaskCard/visual body the thread uses) instead of a JSON `pre`, or remove the pane until there is one.
- **ASK-2 Composer hides under the iOS Safari keyboard** [P0] thread and home composer, phone (iOS Safari 26), light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:892, :895 (`.ch-ask` phone height uses `var(--keyboard-height, 0px)`); src/clubhouse/shell/PhoneScreen.tsx:186 (`useSafariKeyboardFit`) Wrong: Ask's phone height subtracts `--keyboard-height`. That value comes from CapacitorProvider's web fallback, `innerHeight - vv.height - vv.offsetTop`. The comment at PhoneScreen.tsx:186 says this reads 0 on Safari 26 because Safari shrinks innerHeight. Messages opts into `PhoneScreen keyboardAware`; Ask does not (no `keyboardAware` in chat/*.tsx). With the keyboard up, the column keeps its full height, Safari pans the page, and the topbar scrolls away or the dock sits under the keyboard. Fix: wrap the phone Ask in `PhoneScreen keyboardAware` (as Messages does), or drive the height from `useSafariKeyboardFit` / visualViewport `resize` + `scroll`, instead of `--keyboard-height`.
- **ASK-3 Phone artifact push screen sits under the top bar, with two PhoneTops portalled into one slot** [P1] artifact opened from the thread, phone 390, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:141 (`.ch-ak-push`); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Artifact.tsx (phone branch renders its own `PhoneTop`); src/clubhouse/shell/phone-chrome.tsx:359 Wrong: `.ch-ak-push` is `position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: var(--ch-z-sticky)` (40), the same z as the sticky phone topbar. It uses `padding-top: 48px` with no `env(safe-area-inset-top)`, so on a notched phone its content starts about 11px under the status-bar area. Ask's own PhoneTop (Chats / title / New chat) and the artifact's PhoneTop ("‹ Ask") both portal into the same bar slot. Which one wins depends on mount order, so the back button can be doubled or hidden. Fix: let the artifact view replace Ask's PhoneTop: render one PhoneTop and swap its props while an artifact is open. Use `padding-top: calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top))` and a z above the bar only if the push owns its own header.
- **ASK-4 Composition expand button sits on top of the card title** [P1] answer with a composition visual (`.ch-th-cv`), phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:1977–1995 (`.ch-th-cv__expand`), :1739 (`.ch-th-cv__head`) Wrong: the expand button is `position: absolute; top: 0; right: 0`, 32×32. `.ch-th-cv__head` has no right padding, so a long entity line or title ("Saturday Spring Preview Tournament") runs under the button. The button is also 32px, under the 44px target. Fix: put the expand button in the head flex row (`display:flex; gap:8px`, title `flex:1; min-width:0`). Or give the head `padding-right: 44px`, and give the button a 44px `::after` hit area.
- **ASK-5 Composition visuals are three cards deep inside the answer bubble** [P1] answer with a composition visual, phone 390 (most visible) and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:18 (`.msg-a`: padding 16, radius xl, bubble bg); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:1725 (`.ch-th-cv`: bg-subtle, padding 16, radius 20), :1777 (`.ch-th-cv__fig`: sheet bg, padding 12, radius 16) Wrong: bubble → composition card → figure card is three nested surfaces. At 390 the figure's content width is about 390 − 32 (page) − 32 (bubble) − 32 (cv) − 24 (fig) ≈ 270px. Radii run 20 → 20 → 16, where H17 wants outer minus padding (20 − 16 = 4). This is the "card nested in card" pattern the owner flagged on Coach Home. Fix: drop the `.ch-th-cv` background and padding inside `.msg-a` (keep it as a plain group with a hairline separator), and keep `.ch-th-cv__fig` as the only inset well, with radius `calc(var(--ch-radius-xl) - 16px)` or `--ch-radius-sm`.
- **ASK-6 Citation popover is trapped under the next turn and runs off the right edge** [P1] tapping a "read" chip in an answer, phone 390 and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:18 (`.msg-a { filter: var(--ch-bubble-theirs-shadow) }`), :44 (`.cpop`); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ReadChip.tsx Wrong: `filter` on `.msg-a` creates a stacking context, so `.cpop` (z `--ch-z-panel`) can't rise above the following `.ch-ak-turn`, and the next bubble paints over it. `.cpop` is `left: 0; width: 280px; max-width: 72vw` with no collision handling. A chip that sits past about 110px from the left at 390 pushes the popover past the right edge (no `overflow` clip on the thread column, so it causes horizontal scroll). Fix: render the popover through the shared `Menu`/popover primitive in `src/clubhouse/ui/` (portalled, flips and clamps to the viewport), or move the shadow to `box-shadow` so there is no stacking context, and clamp `left` against the column width.
- **ASK-7 Markdown in answers renders wrong: headings merge, numbered lists turn into bullets, code shows backticks** [P1] any answer the model formats, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/data/coachhelm-chat-thread.ts:579 (`parseProse`, heading handling near :617); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Prose.tsx; prompt rules lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/instructions.ts:56 Wrong: the prompt allows headings, lists and bold. `parseProse` pushes `#`–`######` lines into the current paragraph with no flush, so "## Driving\nFairways hit 62%" renders as one run-on line "Driving Fairways hit 62%". `1.` lists become `<ul>` bullets, so the order is lost. There is no handling for inline code, fenced code, `*italic*` or links, which show their raw `` ` ``, `*` and `[text](url)` characters. Fix: in `parseProse`, flush the paragraph on a heading and emit a heading block (render as `--ch-type-16-strong`). Emit `ol` for `^\d+\.`. Strip or render inline code and italics. Either render links as plain text with the URL dropped, or tell the model in instructions.ts not to emit them.
- **ASK-8 Confirm card source line wraps into the 96px label column** [P1] confirm card (evidence rows) before a write, phone 390 and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:132–133; src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:94 (`.evd .f` grid `96px minmax(0,1fr)`) Wrong: each row renders three children: `.k`, then `.v` or `.ns`, then `.inf`. The grid has two columns, so `.inf` auto-places onto a new row in the 96px label column and wraps every word or two. `.ns` and `.inf` have no CSS at all (no colour, no type), so "not set" and the source line look like the value. Fix: wrap `.v`/`.ns` and `.inf` in one `<span>` in the second column, or add `.evd .f .inf { grid-column: 2; font: var(--ch-type-14); color: var(--ch-text-tertiary) }` and `.evd .f .ns { color: var(--ch-text-tertiary) }`.
- **ASK-9 Capability icons on desktop Ask home fail contrast in dark mode (1.67:1)** [P1] Ask home "What I can do" capability list (v1 desktop), desktop, dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:1277, :1339 (`.ch-ask-cap__ic` background `--ch-green-700`, glyph `--ch-text-on-accent`) Wrong: in dark, `--ch-green-700` resolves to #84c9a0 and `--ch-text-on-accent` to #f4eedd, which is 1.67:1 for the icon glyph (needs 3:1). Fix: in dark use `--ch-accent` with `--ch-text-on-accent` (the pair the Button primary uses), or keep the light tint and use a dark glyph (`--ch-green-900`).
- **ASK-10 Home pulse rows are about 20px tap targets** [P1] Ask home v2 "pulse" list, phone 390, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css (`.ch-ak-home .pulse .rw`, near :300); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Home.tsx Wrong: each row is a button with `padding: 0` and no `min-height`, so its height is the line box, about 20px. Rows stack with small gaps, so taps hit the wrong row. Fix: `min-height: 44px; display: flex; align-items: center` on `.rw`, or render them with the shared list-row component from `src/clubhouse/ui/`.
- **ASK-11 Long URLs and tokens in answer prose overflow the bubble** [P1] answer with a long URL, file name or unbroken token, phone 390, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:224 (`.ch-th-prose`), :235 (`.ch-th-p`) Wrong: there is no `overflow-wrap`/`word-break` on prose or paragraphs, and `.msg-a` has `min-width: 0` but no clip. A 60-character token at 15px is about 420px wide, which pushes past a 326px bubble and gives the page a horizontal scroll. Fix: `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on `.ch-th-prose` (the composer already does this, quick-chat.css:213).
- **ASK-12 Phone dock has a 12px side edge and thread padding is fixed at 16** [P2] thread and home, phone 390 and 430, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:924–928 (`.ch-ask-dock` phone `padding: 8px 12px 8px`); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:17 (`.ch-th--phone` padding `16px 16px 12px`) Wrong: the composer sits 12px from the screen edge while the thread above it sits at 16, so the two edges don't line up. At ≥414 neither switches to 20 (ask/04 SHARED-7: 16, 20 at 430). Fix: use `padding-inline: var(--ch-page-x-phone)` on both `.ch-ask-dock` and `.ch-th--phone`.
- **ASK-13 Safe-area band below the composer and an extra 20px of page scroll on home** [P2] home and thread, phone (iOS with home indicator), light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:1217 (`.ch-canvas` phone `padding-bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 20px)`), :1414 (notabs); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:892, :895 Wrong: `.ch-ask` already subtracts the safe area and tab bar from 100dvh, but `.ch-canvas` adds `safe-area + 20px` padding below it. On home the page is 20px taller than the viewport and scrolls. In the thread the safe-area band below the dock shows the canvas colour, not `--ch-phone-composer-bg`, so the composer looks cut off. The dock itself has no `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`. Fix: give the Ask route a canvas variant with no bottom padding, and add `padding-bottom: calc(8px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))` to the phone `.ch-ask-dock` (notabs case).
- **ASK-14 Error notice uses danger red for a failed answer** [P2] thread when a turn fails, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:1021–1027 (`.ch-th-error` background `--ch-danger-100`) Wrong: a failed answer gets a red panel. Ask 04 says failures are amber and red is only for under par on hole strips. Fix: switch to `--ch-warning-100` / `--ch-warning-700` and the amber icon tone.
- **ASK-15 No jump-to-latest control, and auto-scroll scrolls the whole page** [P2] long thread while streaming, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Thread.tsx (`endRef.scrollIntoView({ block: 'end' })`, no jump button) Wrong: `scrollIntoView` scrolls every scrollable ancestor (canvas and window too), which jolts the phone topbar under iOS. When the coach scrolls up to read, there is no "jump to latest" pill when new content arrives. Fix: scroll the thread's own `ScrollRegion` (`el.scrollTo({ top: el.scrollHeight })`), only when already near the bottom, and show a jump pill otherwise.
- **ASK-16 Desktop history rows: text pinned to the top of a 36px row** [P2] conversation list panel, desktop 1440, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:304–314 (`.ch-ask-hist__row`) Wrong: `display: block; height: 36px` with no `line-height`, so the 14px title sits at the top of the row with about 16px of empty space below it. Phone fixes this with `line-height: 44px` (:1194); desktop does not. A 36px row is also under 44. Fix: `display: flex; align-items: center; min-height: 44px` (keep ellipsis on an inner span).
- **ASK-17 Hand-drawn buttons in the brief/quiz footer and the empty history state** [P2] brief/quiz footer, empty conversation list, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:193 (`.qft .p`, plus `.qft .sk`, `.qft .c`); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:369 (`.ch-ask-hist__emptynew`, 40px); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/History.tsx Wrong: the buttons are hand-styled, about 36–40px tall, with their own radius and padding, where `src/clubhouse/ui/Button` exists (ConfirmCard and AskError already use it). Fix: replace them with `<Button variant="primary|secondary|quiet">`.
- **ASK-18 "What it knows" panel misuses classes and has a 12px Remove link** [P2] Ask settings, "What it knows", phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/KnowsPanel.tsx:72 (reuses `.ch-ask-hist__new` for "Edit what it knows"), :77 (error shown with `.ch-ak-meter is-low`); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css (`.ch-ak-knows li button`, 12px, no hit area) Wrong: the edit control borrows the history "New chat" style, and errors borrow the usage-meter card. The input has no placeholder and no Save/Cancel. "Remove" is 12px text with no 44px area, next to the item text. Fix: use `Button` (quiet) for edit and remove with a 44px hit area, `States` for the error, and a `FormSheet` field with placeholder, Save and Cancel.
- **ASK-19 Follow-up pills are 36px, and their ring disappears in dark** [P2] suggested follow-ups under an answer, phone and desktop, dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:989–997 (`.ch-th-pill`, `min-height: 36px`), :1463 (dark ring `--ch-border-control`) Wrong: the pills are 36px tall (H10). The light ring is a raw `rgb(28 25 18 / .12)`; the dark override uses `--ch-border-control`, which is very low contrast on the dark bubble, so the pill reads as plain text. Fix: `min-height: 44px`, and use the shared `Badge`/chip or a `Button` size sm with a 44px `::after`.
- **ASK-20 Version arrows are 28px and unstyled in the phone dock** [P2] artifact with versions, desktop pane and phone push. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:143–145 (`.ch-ak-pane .vers button` 28×28); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Artifact.tsx (`Versions`) Wrong: the arrows are 28px (H10). The styles are scoped to `.ch-ak-pane`, so in the phone `.apdock` they render as default browser buttons. They show even with a single version, disabled. Fix: scope to `.vers` in both, 44px hit area, and render `Versions` only when `versions.length > 1`.
- **ASK-21 Hole strip marks under par by colour only, with cells about 33px wide** [P2] hole-strip visual in an answer, phone 390, light and dark, larger Text Size. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:169–173 (`.ch-ak-holes`, `.u b { color: var(--ch-flag) }`) Wrong: under-par holes differ only by red text (H13). Nine columns at about 33px with `--ch-type-14-strong` overflow once Text Size is raised (a 3-character "−2" plus padding fits; "10" with a mark does not). Fix: render each value with `ScoreMark` (circle for under par), and drop to two rows of 9 at XXL or allow horizontal scroll in a `ScrollRegion`.
- **ASK-22 Small type below the phone floor in the hole-strip header** [P2] hole-strip header, phone. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css (`.ch-ak-hs .hs-h small`, `--ch-type-11-strong`) Wrong: 11px text on phone. Fix: `--ch-type-12-strong`.
- **ASK-23 Quiz progress dots animate width** [P2] brief/quiz, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:179–181 (`.qdots i`, `.cur { width: 24px }`) Wrong: the current dot grows from 16 to 24 by changing `width`, which triggers layout on every step (H2). Fix: keep `width: 24px` and use `transform: scaleX(.667)` on the inactive dots, or overlay a scaled indicator.
- **ASK-24 Hard-coded colours in the user bubble with no dark override** [P2] coach's own message bubble, dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:71 (gradient end `#0f4a2e`); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:66 (`.ch-th-user` color `#f7fbf8`) Wrong: the gradient's bottom stop is fixed at `#0f4a2e`, where the spec's dark value is #173D2A, and the ink is a raw hex. In dark the bubble gets a darker band at the bottom that doesn't match the tokens. Fix: use `var(--ch-bubble-mine-fill-2)` (add the token with light and dark values) and `var(--ch-bubble-mine-ink)`.
- **ASK-25 Live "Working" row shows a chevron that does nothing** [P2] streaming answer, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Steps.tsx (live branch renders a ChevronDown with no button); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:21 (`.work .sum` margin `-12px 0`, also applied to `.is-live`) Wrong: while live, the row shows a chevron that suggests "expand", but it isn't a button (a dead tap). The −12px vertical margin, meant for the 44px collapsed button, also pulls the live row into the bubble padding above and below it. Fix: hide the chevron while live (or make it toggle the step list), and limit the negative margin to `.work .sum:not(.is-live)`.
- **ASK-26 Home greeting uses the device's clock, not the team's** [P2] Ask home greeting, any, first paint. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Home.tsx (`new Date(data.nowIso).getHours()`) Wrong: `getHours()` uses the server's zone during SSR and the browser's on the client. The greeting ("Good morning"/"Good evening") can differ between the two, which causes a hydration mismatch and a flash, and is wrong for a coach whose team plays in another zone. Fix: compute the hour with `Intl.DateTimeFormat(..., { hour: 'numeric', timeZone: team.tz })` on the server and pass the greeting down.
- **ASK-27 Confirm card primary capsule hit area is about 42px** [P2] confirm card "Add/Send" while busy, phone. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:101–103 (`.conf .btns .p`, `.p.cap`) Wrong: the primary is a hand-styled button, 8px padding plus a 15px line, with a `::after` inset of −3px 0, about 42px total. It also uses `overflow: hidden`, which clips the `::after` hit area. Fix: use `Button` size lg for the primary as well.
- **ASK-28 `.ch-ak-mark { all: unset }` removes the focus ring from tappable visuals** [P2] hole strip, putt profile, season compare and miss pattern blocks, keyboard and VoiceOver. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css (`.ch-ak-mark { all: unset }`, about :443); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/visual/blocks.tsx Wrong: these blocks are `<button>`s. `all: unset` strips `:focus-visible` and `cursor`, and no replacement focus style is declared, so keyboard users get no indication of focus.
