Fix: add `.ch-ak-mark:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--ch-focus-ring); outline-offset: 2px }`, as the thread pills already do (coachhelm-thread.css:1012).

### Quick chat (23)
- **QC-1 Answers show raw markdown, and charts and evidence are silently dropped** [P1] any assistant answer in the quick chat panel, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:410 (`if (b.kind === 'text') return <p className="say">{b.text}</p>`), :414 (`return null` for every other block) Wrong: text blocks go into a single `<p>`, so `**bold**`, `## headings`, `- lists` show their raw characters and newlines collapse into one paragraph. Evidence, visual, composition, standalone receipt and handoff blocks return `null`. An answer whose substance is a chart arrives as a one-line sentence or as nothing, with no "Open in Ask" fallback. Fix: render text through the shared `Prose` component (`screens/coachhelm/chat/Prose.tsx`). For blocks the panel can't draw, show a one-line "See the chart in Ask" row that links to the conversation (the `full()` handler already does this).
- **QC-2 Dark-mode overrides never match, so plan-bar text drops to 1.65–2.92:1** [P1] plan task bar, header buttons, tiles, composer, dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:172 (`.ch-qcs :root[data-theme="dark"] .pseg`), :287 (`.qbtn`), :305 (`.tile i`), :316 (`.cmp`) Wrong: `:root` can never be a descendant of `.ch-qcs`, and the app's attribute is `html[data-fw-theme='dark']`, so none of the four rules ever applies. `.pseg` stays `color: #fff` (:168) on the dark segment colours: white on #4fa877 = 2.92:1, on #7cc79b = 2.00:1, on #9dd6b4 = 1.65:1 (13px strong text needs 4.5). The dark `.qbtn` fill, the tile icon colour and the composer's dark shadow are lost too. Fix: rewrite them as `:where(html[data-fw-theme='dark']) .ch-qcs .pseg { color: #0e120f }` (and the same for the other three), or better, add a `--ch-qc-a-seg-ink` token with light and dark values.
- **QC-3 Failed, undone and cancelled receipts still show the green success disc** [P1] task card after a failed save, an Undo, or a cancel, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:253, :262 (`<div className="rcpt neutral">`), :271 (`<div className="rcpt fail">`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:193, :195–196 (`.rcpt__g.fail`, `.rcpt__g.neutral`) Wrong: the modifier class sits on the `.rcpt` container, but the CSS targets `.rcpt__g.fail` / `.rcpt__g.neutral` on the disc. Every receipt disc therefore keeps `background: var(--ch-qc-a-accent)`, so "Not saved", "Undone" and "Cancelled" all show the same accent-green disc as a success. The failed receipt also has no Try again or Cancel. Fix: put the modifier on the disc (`<span className="rcpt__g fail">`) or change the selectors to `.rcpt.fail .rcpt__g`. Add `Button` "Try again" (and quiet "Cancel") to the failed receipt.
- **QC-4 Task header chip is unstyled text** [P1] every task card header (Draft / Added / Not saved / Undone / Cancelled), phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:149–150, :159 (`<span className={chipClass}>`, `'chip' + ' ok' | ' fail'`) Wrong: there is no `.ch-qcs .chip` rule anywhere in quick-chat.css (or in the shared CSS under `.ch-qcs`). The state chip renders as bare inline text at inherited size, so "Not saved" reads like part of the title. PlanTask uses `Badge` for the same thing, so the two card types look different. Fix: use `<Badge tone={ok ? 'positive' : fail ? 'attention' : 'neutral'}>` from `src/clubhouse/ui/Badge`, as PlanTask does.
- **QC-5 Calendar rail clips long events and overlaps existing blocks** [P1] event task visual (add practice/meeting), phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/visuals/visuals.tsx:50 (`y = 12 + (h - first) * 32`), :75 (old blocks `height: 24, padding: '4px 10px'`), :84 (new block `height: Math.max(24, (end - start) * 32 - 2)`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:135 (`.rail` `height: 150px; overflow: hidden`), :138 (`.blk` left 62, right 10) Wrong: the rail is a fixed 150px, which is room for about 4.3 hours at 32px per hour. The new block's height isn't clamped, so a 4-hour practice (126px plus 1h of lead) is cut off at the bottom. Old blocks are 24px tall, but their content (4+4 padding plus 14px strong title plus 13px line) is about 40px, and it's clipped with no ellipsis. All blocks share left 62 / right 10 with no collision layout, so an existing event that overlaps the new one is drawn on top of it. Fix: size the rail from the hour span (`height = 24 + hours * 32`, capped at 220 with the window centred on the new block). Clamp block heights to the rail. Give old blocks `min-height: 32px`, show the title only with `text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap`, and split overlapping blocks into columns (left/right halves).
- **QC-6 Composer placeholder is 2.33:1 in Safari** [P1] composer empty, phone and desktop, light. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:213–214 (`.cmp__t`, `.cmp__t.ph` defined but unused), :320 Wrong: there is no `::placeholder` rule, so WebKit's default (#a9a9a9) applies: 2.33:1 on the #fffefb composer tile. `.cmp__t.ph` (tertiary ink) is never applied to the textarea. Fix: `.ch-qcs .cmp__t::placeholder { color: var(--ch-qc-a-ink-3); opacity: 1 }`, and delete the unused `.ph` rule.
- **QC-7 Stream errors have no Try again, and failed steps get a green check** [P1] answer that fails mid-run, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:296 (`{notice && <p className="say quiet">…}`), :349–401 (`Steps`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:88 (`.step.fail` defined, never used) Wrong: a dropped connection shows a single grey line with no action. `Steps` never emits `fail`, so the step that broke still shows the green done check, which reads as success. Fix: render the notice with the shared `States` error row (message plus `Button` "Try again" that resends the last message). Mark the last active step `fail` when `notice` is set.
- **QC-8 Composer doesn't grow past one line** [P1] typing a multi-line question, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:304–318 (`<textarea rows={1}>`, no auto-size) Wrong: the textarea is fixed at one row, so a second line scrolls inside a 34px box and the coach can't see what they wrote. Fix: auto-size on input (`el.style.height = 'auto'; el.style.height = Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 4 * lineHeight) + 'px'`), or reuse the Ask composer's autosize hook.
- **QC-9 iOS keyboard pan pushes the phone panel's header off-screen** [P1] panel open with the keyboard up, phone (iOS Safari). File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:72–73 (`kb = vv.height + vv.offsetTop`; height `min(700, kb - bar - 14)`), :95–97 (`vv.addEventListener('resize', place)` only) Wrong: the panel is `position: fixed` with `top: var(--ch-qc-qc-top, 104px)` (quick-chat.css:339). When iOS pans the visual viewport (`offsetTop > 0`, typical when focusing the textarea), the layout-viewport `top: 104px` moves up with the page, and only `resize` is listened to, not `scroll`. The header (close and expand) can end up above the visible area until the keyboard closes. Fix: also listen to `visualViewport` `scroll`, and set `top` to `vv.offsetTop + bar` and the height to `vv.height - bar - 14`.
- **QC-10 "Open full chat" button looks live but does nothing before the first send** [P2] panel header, new chat with no message yet, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:224 (`disabled={!chat.conversationId}`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css (`.qbtn` has no `:disabled` rule) Wrong: the button is disabled but styled the same as enabled, so a tap does nothing (dead tap). Fix: `.ch-qcs .qbtn:disabled { opacity: .4; cursor: default }`, or open an empty Ask chat instead of disabling.
- **QC-11 Live pill has no failed state and sits 2px off centre** [P2] Helm key live pill while a run is out, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatProvider.tsx:125–135 (icons for `work`, `wait`, `done` only; inline `maxWidth: phone ? 172 : 260`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:37, :374 (`.live` height 36, `margin-top: -16px`) Wrong: `run.state === 'failed'` renders an empty icon slot, so a failed run looks like idle text. `margin-top: -16px` on a 36px box centres it 2px low. The inline max-width bypasses the CSS and truncates without a token. Fix: add a `failed` branch with the amber dot (`--ch-warning-500`), use `margin-top: -18px` (or `translateY(-50%)`), and move max-width into CSS.
- **QC-12 "Earlier today" row pops in and pushes the thread down** [P2] reopening the panel after a task earlier today, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:252–266 (`{earlier && (<div className="earlier">…)}`) Wrong: the row mounts after its data arrives with no reserved space, so everything below it jumps by about 64px. Fix: reserve the row's height while loading (a skeleton from `src/clubhouse/ui/States`), or fade it in with transform only.
- **QC-13 Long words overflow message bubbles** [P2] user bubble, message-draft visual, answers, phone 390. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:73 (`.ub`), :74 (`.say`), :148 (`.mb`) Wrong: there is no `overflow-wrap` on any of them (the composer has `overflow-wrap: anywhere`, :213). A pasted URL or long token runs past the 84%/88% bubble and the panel edge. Fix: add `overflow-wrap: anywhere` to `.ub`, `.say` and `.mb`.
- **QC-14 Plan bar segment labels are cut mid-letter** [P2] plan task visual with short blocks, phone 390. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/visuals/PlanTask.tsx:115 (`style={{ flex: b.len }}`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:168 (`.pseg` `white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden`) Wrong: a segment's width is proportional to its length, and its label clips with no ellipsis, so a 10-minute "Putting" block at about 30px shows "Pu" cut through a letter. Fix: `text-overflow: ellipsis` on an inner span, and hide the label below about 40px (show it in the legend under the bar).
- **QC-15 Panel corner radius is 20 where the spec says 32 on phone and 26 on desktop** [P2] panel, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:327, :329, :339, :340 (`border-radius: var(--ch-radius-xl)`) Wrong: the panel uses 20px on both, so it reads like an ordinary card rather than the sheet in the design. The inner `.qc__body` (:332) also uses `--ch-radius-xl` with a 6px margin, so the inner and outer radii are equal (H17 wants outer minus inset). Fix: use the sheet radius token (or add `--ch-radius-sheet`), and set `.qc__body` to `calc(var(--ch-radius-sheet) - 6px)`.
- **QC-16 Desktop panel is a fixed 640px, taller than short windows** [P2] panel, desktop with a window under about 720px tall (laptop with dock). File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:340 (`top: 62px; height: 640px; max-height: none`) Wrong: at a 680px-tall window the panel's bottom (composer) is 22px below the viewport and can't be reached. Fix: `height: min(640px, calc(100dvh - 62px - 16px))`.
- **QC-17 Tone chips stay visible at 40% after a message is sent** [P2] message task after Send, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/task-motion.ts:82 (`tones.animate([{ opacity: 1 }, { opacity: 0.4 }], …)`) Wrong: the tone choices fade only to 0.4 and stay interactive, so they still look tappable after the message has gone. Fix: animate to opacity 0, then set `inert` (or `hidden`) on `.mtone`.
- **QC-18 Cancelled task card has no visual change** [P2] task card after Cancel, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:152 (adds `' cancelled'`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css (no `.task.cancelled` rule; `.task.dimmed` exists at :207–208) Wrong: the class is applied but unstyled, so the cancelled card's visual looks as live as a draft. Fix: apply the existing `.dimmed` treatment for `.cancelled` too.
- **QC-19 No scroll lock behind the phone scrim** [P2] panel open, phone. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:210 (`<div className="scrim" … onClick={onClose}>`); no body or canvas scroll lock in the file Wrong: dragging outside the panel, or overscrolling the panel's thread, scrolls the page underneath (iOS scroll chaining), so the header moves behind the scrim. Fix: reuse the Modal/FormSheet scroll lock, or `overscroll-behavior: contain` on `.qc__body` plus `touch-action: none` on `.scrim`.
- **QC-20 Escape is ignored while typing** [P2] panel, desktop keyboard. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:101–111 (Escape closes unless the target is an INPUT) Wrong: the composer is a TEXTAREA, so the check doesn't block it, but the spec wants a first Escape to clear or blur and a second to close. As written, one Escape while typing discards a half-written question. Fix: if the target is the textarea with text, blur it first; close on the next Escape.
- **QC-21 Reduce Transparency setting in the app is ignored** [P2] panel with the in-app Reduce Transparency setting on. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:345 (only `@media (prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce)`) Wrong: the app's own setting (`data-reduce-transparency` on root) doesn't turn off the panel's backdrop blur. Only the OS media query does, and Safari doesn't support that query yet, so iOS users never get the solid panel. Fix: add `:where(html[data-reduce-transparency]) .ch-qcs .qc { background: var(--ch-qc-a-card); backdrop-filter: none }`.
- **QC-22 Year coins use 11px type** [P2] roster visual coins (SR/JR/SO/FR, "+3"), phone. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:117, :123 (`.coin` `--ch-type-11-strong`, `.coin.more` `--ch-type-11`) Wrong: 11px text on phone. Fix: `--ch-type-12-strong`, or the shared Avatar size xs.
- **QC-23 Preview has no error or failed-task fixture** [P2] `/clubhouse-preview/*?qc=<state>`. File: src/clubhouse/preview/PreviewQuickChat.tsx:17 (`error: undefined`), :35 (`QC_FIXTURES`) Wrong: no fixture covers a stream error, a failed step or a failed receipt, so QC-3 and QC-7 never show up in captures. Fix: add `qc=error`, `qc=task-failed` and `qc=task-cancelled` fixtures.

### Practice plan creation, Practice tab, Documents (33)
- **PP-1 The full plan prints a blank team, UTC times starting "now" and every player as "Player" on live data** [P0] Ask → plan card → Open (full plan, the Sheet tab), phone and desktop, light and dark. Staging data, not previews.. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:682-700` (sheet fallbacks) - `src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/practice-draft.ts:72-94` (`planCardMeta`) Wrong:  - The full plan builds the sheet from `plan.sheet`, using fallbacks: - `team: plan.sheet?.team ?? ''` - `start: new Date(plan.sheet?.start ?? Date.now())` - `zone: plan.sheet?.zone ?? 'UTC'` - coaches `[]` - an empty name map - The live server never sends `sheet`. `planCardMeta` returns only `{when,start,drills,alternatives,sources,evidence}`. - The coach therefore sees: - no team name or mark - today's date - block times in UTC counting from the moment of opening - "Not set" coaches - every player in every group printed as "Player" - The previews look right only because their fixtures supply `sheet`. Fix:  - Return `sheet: { team, start, zone, coaches, names }` from `planCardMeta`. Read them from the team row, the event's start and timezone, and the roster that the draft already used to group players. - Until then, render the sheet's "—" (U+2014) no-data cells instead of fabricated values. Never fall back to `Date.now()` or 'UTC'.
- **PP-2 The plan card is a card nested inside the answer bubble** [P1] Ask thread, plan card (writing, ready and saved states) and the intake card, phone and desktop, light and dark. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/AnswerCard.tsx:90-91` - `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:18` (`.msg-a{padding:16px;border-radius:xl;background:var(--ch-bubble-theirs-bg)}`) Wrong:  - `PlanCard` and `PlanIntake` render inside `.msg-a`, so the bordered plan card sits inside a padded bubble. - Card against bubble is 1.00:1 in light (#fffefb on #fffefb) and 1.14:1 in dark (#191e1a on #232925). The card edge reads only as a shadow or hairline inside a second rounded box. - The card loses 32px of width (358 → 326 at 390). - The design (S-ASK.plan-card__ready) draws the card 358 wide directly on the thread, with no bubble. Fix: In AnswerCard, when the part is `plan` or `intake`, render it as a sibling of `.msg-a`, not as its child. Alternatively, add `.msg-a.is-bare{padding:0;background:none}` for those parts.
- **PP-3 The practice plan page has no styles: 0 edge on phone, full-bleed on desktop, no title or Back** [P1] `/golf/dashboard/practice/plan/[id]` (reached from "Edit in builder" and from saved plans), phone and desktop. File:  - `src/clubhouse/routes/practice.tsx` (`PracticePlanRoute`, `<main className="ch-page">`) - No `.ch-page` rule exists anywhere in `src/` (grep returns nothing) Wrong:  - The page is a bare `<main class="ch-page">` with a lone Print button (`PlanPageActions`) and the sheet. - `.ch-canvas` has no side padding, and `.ch-page` is unstyled. So on phone the sheet's text sits on the screen edge (0px, against 16/20), and at 1440 it stretches the full canvas width. - There is no page title, no PhoneTop and no Back, so on phone the only way out is the browser back. Fix:  - Wrap the route in the shared page shell used by other routes, with PhoneTop (title "Practice plan", back) on phone and the desktop page header. - Give the body `padding-inline: var(--ch-page-x-phone)` and a `max-width` of about 760 for a printable sheet. - Alternatively, define `.ch-page` once in the shell CSS and audit every user of it.
- **PP-4 "Edit in builder" goes to a read-only print page, not a builder** [P1] Full plan → overflow / dock "Edit in builder", phone and desktop. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx` (router push to `/golf/dashboard/practice/plan/${planId}`) - `src/clubhouse/routes/practice.tsx` Wrong: The action promises editing. The destination is the unstyled sheet in PP-3, which has only a Print button and no edit controls. Fix: Either route to the real practice builder, or rename the action "Open printable sheet" until a builder exists.
- **PP-5 Groups D–F draw an invisible disc: a white letter on no colour** [P1] Plan sheet → Groups and Rotation, any plan with 4 or more groups, phone and desktop, light and dark. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/plan-sheet-model.ts` (`LETTER = 'ABCDEF'`) - `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:3-5, 263-265` (only `.g-A/.g-B/.g-C` are defined) Wrong:  - The model allows six groups, but the CSS colours only three. - Groups D, E and F render the letter in text-on-accent (#f4eedd) on a transparent background. That is about 1.1:1 on the light card, so it is effectively invisible, and the rotation grid loses its key. Fix:  - Add `.g-D/.g-E/.g-F` from the existing categorical tokens, with dark overrides. - Alternatively, cap `LETTER` at the number of styled groups and fold extras into "+N".
- **PP-6 The full plan dock fires Publish / Save draft even when saving is off, and drops the "Comes with the Practice tab" copy** [P1] Full plan dock, teams without the Practice tab (`saveOn` false), phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:721-735` (dock), plus the `.foot` branch for `!saveOn` Wrong:  - "Publish…" always renders, and it calls `publish()` → `save()` → `savePracticePlanDraft` even when `saveOn` is false. That makes a network call that cannot succeed. - On desktop, "Save draft…" also shows regardless of `saveOn`. - With `!saveOn` the foot shows nothing, so the coach gets no explanation. - On phone in the draft state there is only Publish, though the spec draws Save draft and Publish. Fix:  - Gate both dock buttons on `saveOn`. - In the `!saveOn` branch, render the spec line with `InlineNotice`. - On phone, render both buttons (`Button` secondary "Save draft", primary "Publish") in the draft state.
- **PP-7 On desktop a failed save shows nothing, and the Saved receipt with Undo is hidden** [P1] Ask on desktop (≥821) with the plan pane open, save / publish / Undo. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx` (receipt and error render in `.foot`) - `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:1454` (`.ch-cpv.is-desk .art > :not(.art-h){display:none}`) Wrong:  - On desktop the card collapses to its header, which hides `.foot`. The "Saved to …" receipt, its Undo, and any save error therefore never appear. - No toast is raised either, so a failed save is silent. Fix: Render the receipt and errors in the desktop `.fdock`, or raise the shared `ch-toast` with an Undo action on desktop. Errors use `InlineNotice tone="error"`.
- **PP-8 The full plan title has no ellipsis and runs under the absolute "Ask" Back** [P1] Full plan bar, long plan titles, phone (390) and the desktop pane (452). File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:685-689` (`.fbar` `<b>{plan.title}</b>`) - `.ch-cpv-full .fbar` in `coachhelm-plan.css` Wrong:  - Back is absolutely positioned at the left, and the centred title has no `max-width`, `overflow` or `text-overflow`. - A live title such as "Short game and pressure putting before Hargrove" (48 characters at type-17) is about 380px. It wraps to two lines or overlaps the Back label. Fix: Use `.fbar b{max-width:calc(100% - 2*88px);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}`, or reuse PhoneTop's title slot, which already does this.
- **PP-9 The full plan overlay ignores safe areas: the bar sits under the notch and the dock under the home bar** [P1] Full plan, phone (notched devices, `viewportFit: cover` at `src/app/layout.tsx:105`), light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css`: - `.ch-cpv-full{position:fixed;inset:0}` - `.fbar{min-height:56px}` with no top inset - `.fdock{padding:12px 16px}` Wrong:  - The overlay covers the whole viewport, but neither the bar nor the dock adds `env(safe-area-inset-*)`. - On an iPhone, the Back and title sit under the status bar (about 47px), and the Publish button sits under the home indicator (34px). Fix:  - `.fbar{padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top)}` - `.fdock{padding:10px 16px max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))}`
- **PP-10 Drill fan cards overflow the card and collide with "+N more"** [P1] Plan card read picture `dr` (drills), phone 390, live drill titles. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/ReadPicture.tsx` (`dr`: cards `translateX((i-1)*84px) rotate((i-1)*5deg)`, 96 wide, in an 88-tall `.fan`; `.more` at `right:0;bottom:0`) - `coachhelm-plan.css` (`.ch-cpv-vz .fan`) Wrong:  - Three 96px cards spread ±84px. That is about 264px plus rotation overhang, inside about 268px of inner width (326 in a bubble, see PP-2). - The third card overlaps the absolutely placed "+N more". - Live `d.title` wraps to 3–4 lines, which makes cards taller than the fixed 88px fan, so they spill over the caption below. Fix:  - Derive the spread from the container width (`--fan-step: min(84px, (100cqi - 96px)/2)` with `container-type:inline-size`). - Clamp titles with `-webkit-line-clamp:2`. - Put "+N more" in normal flow below the fan.
- **PP-11 Evidence rows print raw numbers with a hyphen-minus and an unclamped bar width** [P1] Plan card → Evidence sheet, live data, phone and desktop. File:  - `src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/practice-draft.ts:90` (`String(m.value)`) - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx` evidence sheet (`.tr .m` `width: ${r.pct}%`) Wrong:  - Values arrive as `String(m.value)`, for example "-1.4321" or "0.38", with no formatting, precision or unit, and with a hyphen-minus. - The bar width is the raw `pct` with no clamp, so a value over 100 or under 0 overflows the track or goes negative. Fix:  - Format with the shared stats formatter (the one Stats uses for strokes-gained) on the server, or in the sheet before render. - Use `width: ${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, r.pct))}%`.
- **PP-12 Block sheet Cancel keeps the edits** [P1] Full plan → tap a block → FormSheet (minutes and reps steppers), phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx` (block FormSheet, the steppers call `setBlock` live) Wrong: The steppers write straight into plan state, so Cancel and Done both keep the change. A coach who cancels still sees the new times, and the total duration has shifted. Fix: Hold a local draft of the block in the sheet, and call `setBlock(draft)` only in `onDone`.
- **PP-13 The desktop pane opens only after writing finishes; meanwhile the card is a bare header** [P1] Ask on desktop, while a plan is being written. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:228-234` (pane opens on ready) - "Open" is hidden while writing - `coachhelm-plan.css:1454` Wrong:  - On desktop the in-card body is hidden (`.is-desk .art > :not(.art-h)`), and the pane waits for `ready`. - During writing (10–40s) the coach sees only a title row: no progress, no streamed blocks, no Open. Fix: Open the pane on the first `writing` state and render the streaming blocks there, or show the card body on desktop until the pane opens.
- **PP-14 At 821–1100 the open pane squeezes the thread to about 140–400px** [P1] Ask on desktop narrow (821–1100) with the plan pane open, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:1398-1408` (`.ch-cpv-full` 452 wide; `.ch-ask-main{margin-right:452px}`) Wrong:  - A fixed 452px pane plus the shell's left rail leaves the thread column at about 137px at 821 and about 400px at 1100. - The answers, composer and the plan card header wrap word by word. Fix: Add `@container`/`@media (max-width:1100px)` so `.ch-cpv-full` becomes an overlay with a scrim and no `margin-right`, or use `width: min(452px, 45vw)`.
- **PP-15 PlanFormat Undo ignores failure and leaves the undone format's name as "Current format"** [P1] Practice → Format (coachhelm-practice-format), after an upload → Undo. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/format/PlanFormat.tsx` (`undoPlanFormat(...).then(()=>setView({k:'empty'}))`) Wrong:  - There is no `catch`, so a failed undo silently shows the empty state while the server keeps the format. - `name` is not reset, so the next render shows the undone file's name as the current format. Fix: Use `.then(()=>{setName(prev); setView({k:'empty'})}).catch(()=>setView({k:'error'}))`, and show `InlineNotice` on error.
- **PP-16 PlanFormat draws its own green bar (a local copy of the shell bar)** [P1] Practice → Format, phone. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/format/PlanFormat.tsx` (`.fbar.is-page` with `margin:-16px -16px 0`, Back → `/golf/dashboard/calendar` labelled "Practice") - `src/clubhouse/shell/phone-chrome.tsx` (PhoneTop) Wrong:  - This is a hand-built copy of the green bar. It uses negative margins to bleed, has no safe-area top, and has a Back that goes to Calendar while it reads "Practice". - It also doesn't move to 20px at ≥414, because `.ch-cpv-fmt` is fixed at 16 (see PP-25). Fix: Replace it with `<PhoneTop title="Practice format" back={{href: practiceHref, label:'Practice'}}/>` and drop the negative margins.
- **PP-17 Dark-mode drill-kind dots fall below 3:1** [P2] Read pictures and the sheet, kind key (skill, pressure, random), dark. File: `coachhelm-plan.css` (`.ch-cpv-vz .k-skill/.k-random/.k-pressure`, hard-coded hex with no dark override) Wrong: On the dark card #191e1a the contrast is: - skill #3d6787: 2.81:1 - pressure #1f6b45: 2.61:1 - random: 3.37:1 The first two fail the 3:1 non-text minimum. Fix: Move the kinds to tokens with dark values, such as lighter tints (skill #7fa9c9, pressure #6bbb8c = `--ch-green-600` dark).
- **PP-18 Read-picture labels wrap instead of ellipsizing; cls axis labels collide and hang off the edge** [P2] Plan card read pictures `sg`, `pd` and `cls`, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/ReadPicture.tsx`: - `pd` labels are live `metric_label` in a fixed 64px column - `cls` boxes use `left`/`width` percentages with no clamp - axis labels are absolute at `a.at%` with `translateX(-50%)` and nowrap Wrong:  - `sg` and `pd` labels wrap to 2–3 lines in the 64px column and push rows apart. - In `cls`, the tag text gets clipped inside `.bx`, and the end axis labels overhang the card by half their width. Fix:  - Single-line ellipsis on the label columns. - Clamp the axis label position to `[labelW/2, 100% - labelW/2]`, or anchor the end labels left and right.
- **PP-19 The cal picture uses a fixed 5-column grid whatever the number of days** [P2] Read picture `cal` (previews only, not produced live). File: `ReadPicture.tsx` and `coachhelm-plan.css` (`.wk{grid-template-columns:1.6fr 1fr .8fr .8fr 2fr}`) Wrong: With 3 or 7 days the cells misalign or wrap into a second row. Fix: Use `grid-template-columns: repeat(var(--n), 1fr)`, with `--n` set from `days.length`.
- **PP-20 Rotation grid and meta row don't adapt on phone** [P2] Plan sheet, phone 390 and under 340. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/PracticePlanSheet.tsx` and `coachhelm-plan.css`: - `.pp-rot` uses `repeat(n,40px)` with no cap and no container query - `.pp-meta` is 3 columns - `.pp-groups` is 3 columns Wrong:  - 6 stations × 40 plus the label column overflows 358. - The 3-column meta (date, time, coaches) truncates coach names on phone. - A 4th group wraps onto its own row. Fix:  - Use `repeat(n, minmax(28px, 40px))` with `container-type:inline-size`. - `.pp-meta` becomes 1 column, and `.pp-groups` becomes 2 columns, on phone.
- **PP-21 11px type in the sheet and tags on phone** [P2] Plan sheet group detail and tag row, and the plan date, phone. File: `coachhelm-plan.css`: - `.ch-cp-sheet .gd` defined twice: line 6 is type-11-strong, line 29 is type-11 (w400) - `.tagr` is type-11 - `.pp-date` Wrong:  - The second rule silently overrides the first, and both are 11px on phone. - The `data-density` attribute the sheet sets has no CSS, so nothing scales up. Fix: Use one `.gd` rule at `--ch-type-12-strong`. Set `.tagr` and `.pp-date` to type-12. Remove the dead `data-density`, or style it.
- **PP-22 Tap targets under 44 in the plan card and sheets** [P2] Plan card refine chips, intake chips, the ask-in send button, source button, folder save row; phone. File: `coachhelm-plan.css`: - `.ch-cpv-sheet .ask-in button` 28×28 with no hit slop - `.refine button{min-height:36px}` - `.srcb::after` (about 39) - `.saverow` (about 37) - `PlanIntake.tsx` `.qo` (36) Wrong: Five interactive controls fall short of 44 on touch. Fix: Add the shared `::before{inset:-8px}` hit area (the pattern `.ch-msv-new` uses), or set `min-height:44px`. Keep the visual size.
- **PP-23 "Saving…" sweep and the writing caret are static; the scan line has no CSS** [P2] Plan card writing and saving states, and PlanFormat reading, light and dark. File: `coachhelm-plan.css`: - `.sw2` and `.caret` have no animation; the only keyframes in the file are the old `ch-cp-seg` - `.scanl` (used by PlanFormat) is not defined Wrong:  - The progress affordances in the spec are drawn but frozen, so the card looks stuck while writing or saving. - The format "reading" state shows no scan at all. Fix: Add `@keyframes` using `--ch-dur-*` / `--ch-ease-*` tokens under `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`, and define `.scanl`.
- **PP-24 Folder names lack ellipsis in the save rows and the folder strip** [P2] Save to folder sheet, saved receipt, and the documents strip, phone. File: `coachhelm-plan.css` (`.fr .n`, `.saverow b`, `.dstrip .fold b` in a 3-column strip) Wrong: Long folder names (up to 60 characters) wrap and push the row height, or overflow the 3-column strip. Fix: Use `min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap` on those spans.
- **PP-25 Format page side padding stays 16 at ≥414** [P2] Practice → Format, phone 414–430. File: `coachhelm-plan.css` (`.ch-cpv-fmt{padding:16px}`) Wrong: The page edge doesn't follow `--ch-page-x-phone` (20 at ≥414), so it sits 4px off every other page. Fix: `padding-inline: var(--ch-page-x-phone)`.
- **PP-26 The intake's folded state is never drawn** [P2] Ask thread after the intake questions are answered. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanIntake.tsx` (`if (done) return null`) Wrong: The intake disappears entirely, so the thread loses the record of what the coach chose (length, focus, groups). Fix: When `done`, render the folded summary row from the chosen answers.
- **PP-27 Refine chips are hard-coded and team-specific** [P2] Plan card refine row, every plan. File: `PlanCard.tsx` (`REFINE = ['Make it 2 hours','More pressure','Lighter before Hargrove']`) Wrong: "Lighter before Hargrove" shows for every team and every plan, whatever the schedule. Fix: Build the chips from `plan.when` and the next event, or drop the event-specific chip when there is no matching event.
- **PP-28 Publish coins cap at 9 with no "+N"** [P2] Publish confirm card (ConfirmCard), roster over 9. File:  - `src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/practice-draft.ts:162` - `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:113-170` Wrong: The coin row shows up to 9 avatars and silently drops the rest. Fix: Slice to 6 and render a `+{n-6}` coin with the shared Avatar overflow style.
- **PP-29 Publish sample preview shows an empty green square for the app icon** [P2] ConfirmCard → sample notification preview, light and dark. File: `ConfirmCard.tsx` (`.ch-cpv-sample .app`) Wrong: The icon box has a background and no glyph or mark, so it reads as a broken image. Fix: Render the shared app mark (or Avatar of the team) inside `.app`.
- **PP-30 The player practice page has no empty state and no desktop width** [P2] coachhelm-practice-player, a player with no stops; desktop 1440. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/player/PlayerPractice.tsx` Wrong:  - An empty `stops` array renders an empty card frame. - On desktop the content stretches the full canvas width. Fix: Use `EmptyState` ("No practice plan yet") when `stops.length===0`, and a page `max-width` like the other player pages.
- **PP-31 The three upload options do the same thing; "Take a photo" doesn't open the camera** [P2] Practice → Format → Upload choices, phone. File: `PlanFormat.tsx` (all three options call `input.click()`) Wrong: "Take a photo", "Choose a file" and the third option open the same file picker. "Take a photo" has no `capture`. Fix: Use separate inputs: `accept="image/*" capture="environment"` for the photo, and `accept=".pdf,image/*"` for files. Otherwise collapse to one option.
- **PP-32 The full plan overlay has no focus trap, no Esc, and no scroll containment** [P2] Full plan overlay, phone and desktop (keyboard). File: `coachhelm-plan.css` (`.ch-cpv-full`, `.fbody` with no `overscroll-behavior`) and `PlanCard.tsx` Wrong:  - Tab leaves the overlay into the hidden thread, and Esc does nothing. - On phone, scrolling to the end of `.fbody` scrolls the thread underneath. Fix: Add `overscroll-behavior:contain` on `.fbody`, plus the shared modal focus trap and Esc handler (as FormSheet uses).
- **PP-33 The folder-strip "flight" animation can't run on desktop** [P2] Save to folder, desktop. File: `PlanCard.tsx` (`stripRef`, inside the hidden `.foot`) Wrong: The strip the save flies to is inside the element that desktop hides (PP-7), so the animation has no target, and the receipt never shows. Fix: Covered by the PP-7 fix. Anchor the flight to the dock on desktop.

### Messages (19)
- **MSG-1 The phone inbox bar shows a compose key instead of the bell, plus a second New message key** [P1] Messages inbox (root), phone, light and dark, coach and player. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:~177` (`<PhoneTop title="Messages" start action={<PhoneIconAction icon={SquarePen} …/>}>`) - `src/clubhouse/shell/phone-chrome.tsx` (PhoneTop treats any `action` as non-root and drops the bell) Wrong:  - Because `action` is set, PhoneTop replaces the notifications bell with a SquarePen key. - The v2 inbox also draws its own `.ch-msv-new` key in the body. The screen ends up with two New message buttons and no way to reach notifications. Fix: Drop the `action` prop on the root PhoneTop so the bell comes back. Keep `.ch-msv-new` as the only compose entry.
- **MSG-2 Reaction badges overlap the bubble above and run off-screen with several reactions** [P1] Thread, any message with reactions inside a run, phone and desktop, light and dark. File:  - `src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:5075-5086` (`[data-ch-ms-v2] .ch-ms-reacts{position:absolute;top:-14px}`, `.on-theirs{right:-10px}`, `.on-mine{left:-10px}`) - `MessagesView.tsx:1290` (Bubble class assembly) and `:1482` Wrong:  - The chips hang 14px above the bubble. Nothing adds space above a reacted bubble: no class and no `:has(.ch-ms-reacts)`. In a run (2–4px gaps), the chips sit on top of the previous bubble's last line. - The row is a nowrap flex anchored 10px outside the bubble edge. Three or four distinct reactions on a short bubble ("ok") extend past the bubble, and for `on-theirs` past the left screen edge. Fix:  - Add `.ch-ms-msg:has(.ch-ms-reacts){margin-top:16px}`, or a class set in Bubble. - Cap the visible chips at 3 plus a "+N" chip. - Give the row `max-width: calc(100vw - 2*var(--ch-page-x-phone))`.
- **MSG-3 The phone composer is an opaque bar with a mismatched fade band, not the floating glass pill** [P1] Thread composer, phone, light and dark, at rest and with the keyboard open. File:  - `messages.css:2110-2121`: the old phone rule, `padding:8px 8px calc(8px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`, opaque `--ch-phone-composer-bg` (rgb(253 252 248/.94) light, rgb(27 32 28/.94) dark), and a top hairline - `messages.css:4679`: the v2 `::before`, a 24px fade to `--ch-grouped-bg` Wrong:  - The v2 fade blends to grouped-bg (#f2f0e9 light, #0e120f dark) but sits on a bar of a different colour (#fdfcf8 / #1b201c). That leaves a visible band and a hard edge above the composer in both themes. - The bar is full-bleed with 8px sides. - Bottom is 8 plus the full safe-area inset, about 42 on iPhone, so it sits too high. Fix: Under `[data-ch-ms-v2]`: - Set the phone composer wrapper `background:none`, with no hairline. - Give the pill `background:var(--ch-glass-strong)` and `margin:0 10px`. - Use `padding-bottom:max(8px, calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) - 4px))`. - Keep the `::before` fade, which now matches the page.
- **MSG-4 Group typing on phone names an arbitrary member when the typer is unknown** [P1] Group thread, typing indicator, phone. File: `MessagesPhone.tsx:683` (`api.typingUserIds?.length ? api.typingUserIds : [conv.memberIds[0] ?? ""]`) Wrong:  - When the typer list is empty, the indicator falls back to the first member's name. It says "Jordan is typing" when Jordan is not typing, and that member can even be the viewer. - Desktop says "Someone" in the same case (`MessagesView.tsx` ~2515). Fix: Use the desktop fallback: no name ("Someone is typing"), or hide the indicator when there is no typer id.
- **MSG-5 Details shows "Leave" on the team channel for anyone who didn't create it** [P1] Team channel → Details quick actions, phone (redesign), player and assistant coaches. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/messages/details-v2.tsx:151-156` (`{conv.group && !mine && (… Leave …)}`), wired at `MessagesPhone.tsx:969` Wrong:  - The tile's only condition is `conv.group && !mine`, so the team channel offers Leave to every non-creator. The team channel is a group whose membership the roster owns. - The pin and row code treat the team channel as its own kind (see MSG-9), but Details doesn't. Fix: Use `conv.group && !mine && conv.channel !== 'team'`. Use the same kind test as the rows (`c.channel`).
- **MSG-6 Hold menu uses its own 768 phone breakpoint and fixed insets** [P2] Long-press on a bubble (lift, reaction bar and menu), widths 768–820 and very tall bubbles, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/messages/hold-lift.tsx:50-55` (`const phone = window.innerWidth < 768`, insets 60/36 or 12/12) and `:143-145` Wrong:  - The shell is phone up to 820, but the lift switches to desktop insets at 768. On an iPad mini portrait (768) the reaction bar can go to 12px from the top, under the status bar. - Insets are fixed numbers, not `env(safe-area-inset-*)`. - For a bubble taller than the viewport, `Math.max(limit-bottom, insetTop-top)` keeps the top and pushes the menu off the bottom. Fix:  - Use `useChPhone()`. - Add `env(safe-area-inset-top/bottom)` to the 60/36. - When `bottom - top > innerHeight - insets`, pin the menu to the bottom inset and let the copy clip.
- **MSG-7 Thread bar sizes are off spec on phone, and the team channel bar has no flag** [P2] Thread bar (`.ch-msp-who`), phone, light and dark. File: `MessagesPhone.tsx` (`.ch-msp-who`: Avatar 30, name type-15, role 12) and messages.css `.ch-msp-who` Wrong:  - The bar uses a 30 avatar, a 15 name and a 12 role. The spec is 36, 17/600 and 13. - The team channel shows a generic group mark, while its pin shows a Flag. Fix:  - Use `<Avatar size="md">` (36), `--ch-type-17-strong` and `--ch-type-13`. - Render the Flag group mark when `conv.channel === 'team'`.
- **MSG-8 The Unread filter still shows the Pinned card, and pinned unread conversations drop out of "Unread"** [P2] Phone inbox → Segmented "Unread", light and dark. File: `MessagesPhone.tsx:259-345` (pinned card; `recentTitle` at 275; segmented at 295) Wrong:  - With the Unread filter on, the Pinned card still renders all pins, including ones with nothing unread. - The list below excludes pinned conversations, so a pinned unread thread appears only as a pin, not in the "Unread" list the filter promises. Fix: When `filter !== 'all'`, hide the Pinned card and include pinned matches in the filtered list. Alternatively, filter `pinned` by the same predicate.
- **MSG-9 Pins decide "team" by title; rows decide by channel** [P2] Phone inbox pins, any group whose title matches the team name. File: `MessagesPhone.tsx:337, 341` (`c.title === api.teamName ? Flag : Users`, label "Team") Wrong:  - A user-made group titled like the team gets the team Flag and the "Team" label. - A renamed team channel loses both. - Rows use `c.channel`, so the pin and the row disagree. Fix: Use `c.channel === 'team'` in both places.
- **MSG-10 Desktop redesign still uses the old ThreadBelow, not the jump key** [P2] Desktop thread, scrolled up with new messages, light and dark. File: `MessagesView.tsx:2239` (`ThreadBelow`), used at `:2538` Wrong: The redesign phone thread uses the v2 jump key. Desktop still renders the legacy "N new below" bar, so the two surfaces differ. Fix: Render ThreadJumpKey on desktop when `api.redesign`, and keep ThreadBelow only for the legacy path.
- **MSG-11 The details column opens with a raw 462ms grid-template-columns transition** [P2] Desktop thread → Details open and close. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/messages/details-v2.tsx:337` (`grid.style.transition = 'grid-template-columns 462ms var(--ch-ease-spring-smooth)'`) Wrong:  - This is a layout-property animation with a hard-coded duration set inline. - It isn't gated on reduced motion, and it reflows the thread column every frame. - The spec sanctions the motion, not the raw number. Fix: Use `var(--ch-dur-spring-smooth)` (or the matching token) and skip the transition when `useChReducedMotion()` is set.
- **MSG-12 Photo tiles in Details have no loading or failure state** [P2] Details → Photos grid, phone and desktop, slow or failed fetch. File: `details-v2.tsx:249` (`PhotoTile`) and `:299` Wrong:  - Each tile fetches its own signed URL. - While the fetch runs, and if it fails, the tile is a flat grey square with no shimmer, icon or retry. A grid of failures looks like an empty box. Fix: Use the shared skeleton shimmer while loading. On error, show an image-off icon with the file name.
- **MSG-13 Tap targets under 44: hold menu rows and wrapped poll foot links** [P2] Long-press menu, and poll card foot when the links wrap, phone. File:  - `messages.css:4927-4935` (`.ch-msv-holdmenu > button{min-height:42px}`) - `messages.css:3757-3782` (`.ch-ms-poll__foot{flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px 16px}`, `.ch-ms-poll__link{min-height:44px;margin:-8px}`) Wrong:  - The menu rows are 42. - The poll links' 44px hit boxes use −8px margins and lay out at 28px. When the four foot items (count, See votes, Remind N, Close poll) wrap onto two lines in the 272 card, the row gap of 4 makes the two rows' hit areas overlap by about 12px. Fix:  - Set the menu rows to `min-height:44px`. - Set the poll foot to `row-gap:16px`, or move the count onto its own line so the three links fit on one row.
- **MSG-14 Four `.ch-msv-seeall` rules and two toast styles in one file** [P2] Inbox "See all", toasts, phone and desktop. File:  - `messages.css:5300, 5333, 5431, 5438` (`.ch-msv-seeall`) - `messages.css:5088-5094` (`body:has([data-ch-ms-v2]) .ch-toast`, glass-strong) - `messages.css:5825` (`.ch-toasts.is-pill .ch-toast`, glass-bg) Wrong:  - Later rules partially override earlier ones, so the result depends on source order. - Two toast surfaces exist, despite the comment "One toast style in Messages", so the toast looks different depending on which container raised it. Fix: Collapse `.ch-msv-seeall` to one rule. Delete the `.is-pill` variant, or make it use `--ch-glass-strong`.
- **MSG-15 The reply quote is still the old elevated card** [P2] Thread bubble with a reply quote, and the composer reply strip, light and dark. File: `messages.css:3049` (`.ch-ms-quote`) and `:3085`. There is no `[data-ch-ms-v2]` restyle. Wrong: The quote keeps the pre-redesign elevation card inside the bubble, so it is a card nested in a bubble, with a shadow that doesn't match the v2 surfaces. Fix: Under `[data-ch-ms-v2]`, set `.ch-ms-quote{box-shadow:none;background:var(--ch-well-flat)}` (on mine, use `--ch-accent-tint`).
- **MSG-16 Own-reaction halo uses the page colour token from the old layout** [P2] Thread, your own reaction chip, light and dark. File: `messages.css:5848` (`--ch-messages-x7: 0 0 0 4px var(--ch-bg-page), …`) and `:5717-5720` Wrong: The v2 thread sits on `--ch-grouped-bg`, but the halo is `--ch-bg-page`, so a 4px ring of a slightly different colour shows around your own chip in both themes. Fix: Use `0 0 0 4px var(--ch-grouped-bg)` under `[data-ch-ms-v2]`.
- **MSG-17 Hard-coded ivory on the reaction chips of others' bubbles** [P2] Reaction chips on others' bubbles, and the reaction sheet head, dark. File: `messages.css:805-807` (`.ch-ms-reacts.on-theirs .ch-ms-react, .ch-rx__head{color:#fffdf6}`) Wrong: A raw hex overrides the token pair set at 773-776 (`--ch-on-fill-50`), so it never follows a theme change. Fix: Replace it with `var(--ch-on-fill-50)`.
- **MSG-18 The mute menu on phone floats at a fixed 40% top, detached from its row** [P2] Inbox row hold → Mute, phone; Details → Mute, desktop near the right edge. File:  - `messages.css:5288` (`.ch-msv-holdmenu.is-mute{left:var(--ch-page-x-phone);top:40%}`) - `details-v2.tsx:45` (`Math.min(r.left, window.innerWidth - 236 - 16)`) Wrong:  - On phone the mute options appear at 40% of the screen whatever row was pressed, so for a row near the bottom they cover other rows and are far from the finger. - On desktop the clamp uses a hard-coded 236 width and doesn't flip upward near the bottom edge. Fix: Anchor the menu to the row rect, flipping when it would leave the safe area. On desktop use the menu's measured `offsetWidth/offsetHeight`.
- **MSG-19 Inbox skeleton uses inline raw sizes, including 9 and 11px bars** [P2] Phone inbox loading. File: `MessagesPhone.tsx:306-313` (`style={{ width: 124.2, height: 11 }}`, `{ width: 210.6, height: 9 }`) Wrong:  - The skeleton rows are inline magic numbers that don't match the loaded row's type metrics (15/13), so the layout jumps when the content lands. - The 44 avatar doesn't match the loaded row avatar. Fix: Build the skeleton from the row's own classes, with `.ch-msv-sk` line heights set from the type tokens.

### Root cause (Read) (18)
- **RC-1 The ⋯ "More" button opens an empty sheet for players** [P1] Read page, player view, phone (bar ⋯) and desktop (deskbar ⋯), both themes. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/read/ReadPage.tsx:82` (action shown whenever `read` exists), `:153-155` (desktop ⋯ always shown), `:184-203` (sheet content: Dismiss only when `target`, which is coach-only at `:172`; Message only when `coach`) Wrong: For a player both children are false, so the ⋯ button opens a Modal titled "More for this read" with nothing in it. That is a dead tap. Fix: Build the action list per role: coach gets Dismiss, Message, Ask, Copy link; player gets Ask, Copy link. Hide ⋯ when the list is empty.
- **RC-2 The More sheet is the wrong component and is missing its actions** [P1] Read page ⋯, phone and desktop. File: `ReadPage.tsx:184-203`; Message link falls back to `href ?? '#'` at `:198` Wrong: The spec's iOS action sheet (rows 57 tall, Dismiss in `--ch-flag`, Message, Ask, Copy a link, then Cancel in its own group) is built as a generic `ui/Modal` with a ghost "Dismiss this read" button and one `.lk` chip. There is no Ask, no Copy link and no Cancel. When `rebuiltHref` returns null the Message chip links to `#`, another dead tap. On desktop it opens as a centred dialog instead of the 250-wide popover under the bar. Fix: Use `ui/Menu` on desktop and a Menu or action-sheet variant on phone (the CSS for `.ash` is already in `coachhelm-read.css:1008-1059`, unused). Rows: Dismiss (destructive), Message {first}, Ask CoachHelm about this, Copy a link. Drop the `'#'` fallback and hide the row instead.
- **RC-3 Round rows show a chevron but can't be tapped** [P1] "The rounds behind it" card, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/read/cards.tsx:370-385` (`<div className="row">` with `.chev`) Wrong: Each round row draws a chevron, the "opens something" cue, but it is a plain `div` with no link or handler. Tapping does nothing. Fix: Make the row a `Link` to the round (the same href the hole chips use, `h.href` in `cause.holes`; add `href` to `rounds.list`) with `.tap`. If there is no round page, remove the chevron.
- **RC-4 Rail player switcher popover is clipped by its own card** [P1] Read page, desktop 1440 and 1180 to 1279, coach view. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/read/ReadsRail.tsx:328-340` (`details.ch-hr-swr` inside `nav.card`); `coachhelm-read.css:191-196` (`.card { overflow:hidden }`), `:1294-1305` (`.pop { position:absolute; left:0; width:250px }`), `:1264-1270` (summary in column 2 after a 36px avatar) Wrong: The summary starts about 60px into a 250-wide rail card (16 padding + 36 avatar + 8 gap). The 250-wide popover therefore ends at about 310, and the card's `overflow:hidden` cuts the last ≈60px. With a full roster (12 names × 36px = 432) the popover is also cut at the bottom when the rail card is shorter. The control is a hand-built `<details>` with a 34px summary and 36px rows. Fix: Use `ui/Menu` (it portals and positions) anchored to the name, or `ui/Segmented` as the spec's switcher. If you keep it, set `.ch-hr-rail { overflow:visible }` and `.pop { left:auto; right:0 }`.
- **RC-5 Cause "arms" draw one 14px dot per hole, with no cap** [P1] "Why CoachHelm thinks so", likely cause, coach, phone and desktop. File: `cards.tsx:151-155` (`Array.from({ length: a.n })`); arm B is "All other holes" (`data/coachhelm-read-view-shape.ts:147`); `coachhelm-read.css:542-556` (14px dots, gap 4, wrap) Wrong: `n` is a raw hole count. "All other holes" over a season is easily 200 to 300. At 326 wide that is 18 dots a row, so n = 300 gives 17 rows (≈306px) of dots, and the card becomes a wall of circles. The empty dots (`--ch-hr-track`) are 1.24:1 light and 1.29:1 dark against the card, so the denominator, which carries the information, can barely be seen. Fix: Above about 30, draw a proportion bar (filled share of a 12px track) or bucket dots (1 dot = n/30 holes, shown in the count line). Give empty dots a 1px `--ch-hr-neutral-fill` ring.
- **RC-6 "How it has moved" prints hyphen-minus and drops the decimal** [P1] Moved card slope labels, phone and desktop. File: `cards.tsx:296` (``const f = (v) => String(Math.round(v * 10) / 10)``), used at `:312`, `:315` Wrong: −1.2 renders "-1.2" (U+002D) and 3.0 renders "3". Elsewhere on the page the same values read "3.0" and "−1.2". Fix: `formatSigned(v)` or `formatFixed` with `MINUS` from `lib/format.ts`, with the read's unit.
- **RC-7 Desktop action bar overflows the middle column between 1180 and about 1280** [P1] Read page desktop, window 1180 to 1279, with the 232 sidebar. File: `ReadPage.tsx:142-156` (two `size="lg"` Buttons + ⋯, no wrap); `coachhelm-read.css:1080-1100` (3 columns from a `max-width:1179px` window query), `:1218-1223` Wrong: The breakpoint uses the window, not the main area. At 1180 the main area is 1180 − 232 − 48 = 900, so the middle column is 900 − 250 − 320 − 40 = 290px. "Ask about this read" + "Message {first}" + ⋯ is ≈380px in a non-wrapping flex row, so it spills about 90px into the evidence column. The headline card and the 44px hero are cramped at 290. Fix: Switch to 3 columns with a container query on `.ch-hr-desk` (min-inline-size about 1000px), not the window. Let `.ch-hr-deskbar` wrap, or put these actions in the glass bar as 06 draws them.
- **RC-8 Benchmark ladder labels still collide when three marks are close** [P1] Read headline and board summary ladder, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/read/ReadLadder.tsx:276-286` Wrong: The split runs pairwise and later pairs overwrite earlier ones. Take marks at 120, 170 and 220: pair 1 sets a to end and b to start, then pair 2 sets b to end and c to start. a and b are both end-anchored 50 units apart, and a label like "Team average" (≈75 units at 12.5) overlaps. A mark near an edge that the split forces to `end` (e.g. cx 20, then tx 16) runs past x 0, and only `overflow:visible` (`coachhelm-read.css:298`) lets it show, into the card padding. Fix: Alternate rows: when two marks are closer than 76, put the second label below the track (y 58 for one, y 8 or a third row for the other) instead of flipping anchors. Keep edge anchoring last, so it wins.
- **RC-9 Ladder text size is set by CSS that scales and overrides the SVG's own size** [P2] Ladder, phone at Text Size above default. File: `coachhelm-read.css:300-304` (`.ladder text { font-size: var(--ch-hr-cap-size) }`), `:1214-1216` (`12px × min(k, 1.2)`), `:292-299` (margin hacks for the overflow); `ReadLadder.tsx:295-305` (`fontSize="12.5"` attributes, which the CSS overrides) Wrong: The second label line's baseline is 58 + 1.15em, which is 71.8 at 12px and 74.6 at ×1.2. With descenders it sits past the 78-unit viewBox, and the layout hides that with a computed `margin-bottom` and `overflow:visible`. The spec's 12.5 becomes 12. Fix: Grow the viewBox height with k (78 + 16 × (min(k,1.2) − 1)) and drop the margin hack and `overflow:visible`. Use 12.5.
- **RC-10 Moved card: the "Now" label is a date and runs off the right edge; the line is always amber** [P2] Moved card, phone and desktop. File: `cards.tsx:320-324` (`{m.through}` centred at x 260), `:308` (line always `--ch-hr-loss-fill`), `:311-316` (labels always 12 above) Wrong: The right label is the read's as-of date ("Through Oct 26", ≈85 units) centred at 260, so it spans ≈217 to 302 and is cut at the 300 edge. The slope is amber even when the read improved. Both value labels sit 12 above their points, so when the lower point's label lands on the line it collides. Fix: Label the right point "Now". Choose the stroke from `m.tone`. Place the lower value 20 below its point.
- **RC-11 Hole chips: no cap and about 30px tall** [P2] Why card, counted cause. File: `cards.tsx:186-199` (all `cause.holes`); `coachhelm-read.css:633-645` (`.hole { padding:6px 8px }`, 13px) Wrong: Every hole is listed, where the spec caps at 5, and each chip is ≈30px tall with no extended hit area. Fix: `holes.slice(0, 5)` plus a "+N more" line; `.hole { min-height:36px; display:inline-flex; align-items:center; position:relative } .hole::after { content:''; position:absolute; inset:-4px 0 }`.
- **RC-12 Link chips are 36px with no hit area** [P2] What to do card link row (Message, Ask), More sheet. File: `coachhelm-read.css:814-825` (`.lk { padding:8px 12px }`, ≈36px) Wrong: The tap height is ≈36. Fix: `min-height:36px; position:relative` plus `::after { inset:-4px 0 }`.
- **RC-13 "Show newest 8 of N" shows all N** [P2] Rounds card with more than 8 rounds. File: `cards.tsx:367` (`all ? list : list.slice(0, 4)`), `:390` Wrong: The label promises 8 but the open state shows the whole list. Fix: `all ? list.slice(0, 8) : list.slice(0, 4)` and link the rest out, or label it "Show all N".
- **RC-14 Rounds subline has no hole dots** [P2] Rounds card rows. File: `cards.tsx:377` Wrong: Only "N counted holes" is shown. The spec's 9×9 dots, one per counted hole, are missing. Fix: Render `r.counted` dots (cap about 9) before the text, in `--ch-hr-loss-fill`.
- **RC-15 Not-traced meter: the spec's ring and ten segments are drawn as N segments** [P2] Why card, not traced. File: `cards.tsx:205-211` (`cause.gap.need` segments; `need = have + target` at `coachhelm-read-view-shape.ts:158`); count-down draws no meter Wrong: A data gap draws `need` flex segments (for example 40 segments of ≈4px), not a 54px ring. The count-down state, which should have the ten-segment meter, draws none. Fix: Draw the ring for `gap`, and ten segments scaled to `countDown.haveEvents/needEvents`.
- **RC-16 Phone loading skeleton has no bar, so it jumps on load** [P2] Read page loading (Suspense fallback), phone. File: `ReadPage.tsx:26-45` (`ReadSkeleton` renders `.sheet` only; the loaded page adds `PhoneTop` at `:78-84`); route `routes/coachhelm.tsx:260` Wrong: The green bar with back and title appears only once data lands, and the cards drop by the bar's height. Fix: Render `<PhoneTop back title="CoachHelm" />` in the skeleton.
- **RC-17 Under Reduce Motion the Assign spinner runs at 1ms per turn** [P2] What to do, "Assigning…". File: `coachhelm-read.css:1101-1106` (`.ch-hr * { animation-duration:1ms!important }`) Wrong: The shared Button spinner keeps `infinite` but turns once per millisecond, so it reads as a static or flickering ring. Fix: Exclude the spinner from the blanket rule, or scope the rule to the named motion classes (`.fbar`, `.dots i`, `.pn`, `.flash`).
- **RC-18 Hero figure tracking reset to 0** [P2] Read headline and board summary. File: `coachhelm-read.css:1271-1273` (`.hero b { letter-spacing:0 }`) overriding `:277-284` Wrong: The 44px figure loses its −0.04em tracking and the 'wdth' 92 setting is 96. It looks loose next to the Team card's figure. Fix: Delete the override and set `font-variation-settings:'wdth' 92; letter-spacing:-0.04em`.

### Coach vs player (20)
- **CVP-1 The file's loading skeleton has no page edge, no top bar and no lens control, so the layout jumps on load** [P0] Player file (coach and player lens), loading state, on phone and desktop, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/FileSkeleton.tsx` (renders `<main className="ch-hf is-loading">`); it is used as the Suspense fallback at `src/clubhouse/routes/coachhelm.tsx:120` Wrong:  - The skeleton has no `is-phone`/`is-desk` class and no `PhoneTop`. - On phone, `.ch-hf.is-phone{padding:0 16px 48px}` (coachhelm-file.css:59) never applies, so the shimmer cards run to the screen edges with 0px inset. - On desktop, there is no `.dfile` padding and no rail. - When the file loads, the bar, the lens Segmented and the 16/20px insets all appear at once. - The comment in the file says the lens control is drawn, but it is not. - It also shimmers twice: `.ch-skel` and `.ch-hf .sk i::before` are both applied. Fix:  - Render the skeleton inside the same shell as `PlayerFile`: the same `is-phone`/`is-desk` class from `useIsPhone()`, `PhoneTop` with the title placeholder, and a disabled `Segmented` for the lens. - Keep one shimmer only: the ui `Skeleton`/`.ch-skel`.
- **CVP-2 The phone page edge is 16px on 414–429px phones, where the token says 20px** [P1] Player file, Your week and lenses, on phone at 414/428 wide (Plus/Max sizes), in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:59-60` (`.ch-hf.is-phone{padding:0 16px 48px}` with `@media (min-width:430px){padding-inline:20px}`); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-views.css:14-19` (`.ch-hv-chips{margin:0 -16px;padding:4px 16px}`) Wrong:  - Both edges are hard-coded at 16px, with a 430px breakpoint. - `--ch-page-x-phone` switches to 20px at ≥414 (tokens.css:1446). - So on 414–429 phones this screen's content is 4px narrower in from the edge than every other tab, and the chip strip bleeds 16px into a 20px gutter. Fix: Use `padding:0 var(--ch-page-x-phone) calc(48px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`, and `.ch-hv-chips{margin-inline:calc(-1*var(--ch-page-x-phone));padding-inline:var(--ch-page-x-phone)}`.
- **CVP-3 The "Goal" label on the goal sparkline collides with the latest point and can clip above the chart** [P1] Player file, Goal block (coach and player lens), on phone and desktop, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/cards.tsx:273-314` (`GoalBlock`, `<text x={W} y={y(goal.target)-4} textAnchor="end" className="gt">` at :295-296); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:83` (`.gt` uses `font:var(--ch-type-12)`) Wrong:  - The label is end-anchored at x=W. The latest data point is drawn at x=W-6, so whenever the player is near the target, the label sits on top of the newest dot. - When the target is the top of the range, `y(target)` is about 11.8, so the baseline is about 7.8. At 12px or larger, the cap height pushes the text above y=0 and it is clipped. - The text uses a type token, so at XXL it grows into the line. - The viewBox is 320×72 with the default `meet`, so on the desktop rail there are empty bars on each side. Fix:  - Put the goal label on the left (`x=0`, `textAnchor="start"`), or outside the SVG as HTML, and clamp its y to ≥ 12. - Pin the font at 11px in the SVG. - Use `preserveAspectRatio="none"` on the line layer with a non-scaling stroke.
- **CVP-4 The correction and "why" buttons use undefined tokens, so their background and transition are dropped** [P1] Player file, coach corrections (`.corr`) and "Why this" (`.cx__why`), on phone and desktop, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:245` (`.corr button, .cx__why button{background:var(--ch-hf-card); transition: … var(--ch-hf-ease)}`) Wrong:  - `--ch-hf-card` and `--ch-hf-ease` are not defined anywhere. The file defines `--ch-hf-a-card`, and the app uses `--ch-ease-*`. - Because of that, the background resolves to transparent, so the buttons show the tint behind them instead of a card chip. - The whole `transition` declaration is also invalid, so pressing them has no feedback. Fix: Use `background:var(--ch-card-bg); transition:background var(--ch-dur-1) var(--ch-ease-standard)`. Better still, render ui `Button variant="tertiary" size="sm"`.
- **CVP-5 The lens Segmented and the Your week tabs are fixed at 300px with nowrap labels, so long first names overflow** [P1] Player file lens control ("As {first} sees it") and Your week header tabs, on phone and desktop, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:304` (`.dhead .aseg{width:300px}`); `src/clubhouse/styles/controls.css:26` (`.ch-seg__b{white-space:nowrap}`, no ellipsis); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-views.css:4-10` (header `flex-wrap`) Wrong:  - Each segment gets about 143px of text room at 13px. - "As Christopher sees it" is about 157px, so it runs past its segment and under the next one. - On desktop Your week, the 300px Segmented and the "Development" button don't fit together on one line, so the header wraps onto two misaligned rows. - This is the owner's "Short game" tab-wrap pattern. Fix:  - Drop the fixed width and use `width:auto; max-width:100%`. - Add `overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;min-width:0` to `.ch-seg__b`, in the shared control, which fixes every caller. - Shorten the label to "{first}'s view" when it is longer than about 12 characters.
- **CVP-6 The sticky "Previewing X's screen" pill uses a magic 158px offset** [P1] Player file, coach previewing the player lens, on phone, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:224` (`.pv{position:sticky;top:158px;margin:-12px 0 -8px}`) Wrong:  - 158px is a hard-coded guess at the bar height plus the sticky lens control. - It ignores `env(safe-area-inset-top)`, which is 47–59px on notch phones, and it ignores Text Size, which grows the Segmented. - On real devices the pill either sits over the lens control or floats with a gap, and the negative margins pull it into the cards next to it. Fix:  - Make it part of the sticky lens container, as a second row in the same sticky element. - Or use `top:calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top) + var(--ch-hf-seg-h))`, with `--ch-hf-seg-h` measured. - Remove the negative margins.
- **CVP-7 The plan stepper's undone ring is 1.24:1, and the dark check is 2.92:1** [P1] Player file, focus or plan stepper (`.hs__s`), on phone and desktop, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:172-176` (`.hs__s i` with an inset 2px `--ch-hf-c-track` ring; `.hs__s i svg{stroke:#fff}` on `--ch-hf-c-gain`) Wrong:  - The ring of an unfinished step against the card is 1.24:1 in light and 1.29:1 in dark, so you can't see the steps that are left. - In dark, the white tick on #4fa877 is 2.92:1. Fix:  - Draw the ring with `--ch-ink-4`/`--ch-hairline-strong`, which is ≥3:1 on the card. - In dark, use `--ch-forest-900`/`--ch-ink-on-gain` for the tick, or darken the fill to `--ch-chart-gain`.
- **CVP-8 The file runs on a private hex palette that duplicates the app tokens** [P1] All of the player file, in light and dark, including Increase Contrast. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:3-30` (`--ch-hf-a-card:#fffefb`, inks, gain/loss, track and so on, with their own dark block) Wrong:  - The design-page palette was copied over as is. - The values happen to match today (`--ch-card-bg` #fffefb / #191e1a), but they skip the Increase Contrast overrides and the theme system. - That is why the typos in CVP-4 go unnoticed: nothing checks these names. Fix:  - Map each `--ch-hf-*` to the app token: `--ch-hf-a-card: var(--ch-card-bg)`, ink to `--ch-ink-1..4`, gain/loss to `--ch-chart-gain/-loss`, track to `--ch-fill-2`. - Then delete the private dark block.
- **CVP-9 The "you" dot in the field strip is drawn 2px off its value, and dots are unclamped** [P1] Player file, "Where you sit" field card (`.dots`), on phone and desktop, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:210-211` (`.dots i{margin-left:-6px}` for a 14px dot; `.dots i.me{margin-left:-8px}` for a 20px dot); `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/cards.tsx:316-348` (`FieldCard`, `at(v)` unclamped) Wrong:  - Each dot should be centred with a margin of minus half its width: −7 for the 14px dot and −10 for the 20px dot, as in the design. - The current values put the player's dot 2px right of its true position, and team dots 1px right. - `at(v)` has no clamp, so a value outside the scale draws the dot past the track and over the card padding. Fix: Use `transform:translateX(-50%)` instead of margins, and set `left: clamp(0%, at(v), 100%)`. Add an edge caret when the value was clamped.
- **CVP-10 Strokes-gained bars are capped at ±1.0 with no overflow mark** [P2] Player file, SG card (`.sg__row`), on phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/cards.tsx:350-400` (`SgCard`, `Math.min(1,|strokes|)*50`) Wrong:  - −3.9 putting draws the same bar as −1.0, so the worst area looks no worse than a mild one. - The cap is the right idea, the opposite of TeamHole's uncapped size, but nothing shows that it was hit. Fix: Keep the clamp, add a ▸ cap mark or a 2px notch at the end when `|v|>1`, and always print the value. It is already printed, so the mark is the only missing piece.
- **CVP-11 The SG row's fixed 108px label column wraps "Around the green"** [P2] Player file, SG card, on phone, and on all sizes at large Text Size. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:189, 198` (`.sg__row`, `.sg__axis{grid-template-columns:108px minmax(0,1fr) 46px}`) Wrong:  - "Around the green" at 15px medium is about 120px wide, so in a 108px column it wraps to two lines and that row is taller than the others. - At XXL every label wraps. - This is the same label the owner saw run off the TeamHole card. Fix: Use `grid-template-columns:minmax(max-content, 40%) minmax(0,1fr) auto`, or say "Short game" / "Around green" on phone.
- **CVP-12 The card body is inset 20px while the card title is inset 16px, and the coach line and track rows bleed back to 16px** [P2] All player file cards, on phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:115` (`.card__h` padding 16), `:119` (`.card__b{padding:4px 20px 16px}`), `:78` (`.ch-hf-coachline{margin:0 -20px}`), `:91` (`.ch-hf-trackrows{margin:0 -20px -16px}`) Wrong:  - Inside one card the title starts at 16px, the body text at 20px, and the full-bleed rows' content at 16px again. - So three left edges are 4px apart. The 20px comes from the design page (redesign.css:224), but the live card header is 16px. Fix: Use one inset, `--ch-card-pad-x`, for header, body and rows. Use `Surface`/`Section` padding instead of local negative margins.
- **CVP-13 The IntentSheet "picks" are hand-drawn chip buttons for single-choice groups** [P2] Player file, set-intent sheet (Aim, Alerts, note), on phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/IntentSheet.tsx` (`.picks` with `aria-pressed` buttons); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:283-284` (`.picks button`), `:285` (`.note` textarea) Wrong:  - Aim and Alerts are one-of-n choices built from toggle buttons. Screen readers announce them as independent toggles, and keyboard users get no arrow-key movement. - The textarea is styled by hand rather than with the FormSheet field. - The chips set their own height (~34px), which is under 44px. Fix:  - Use `<Segmented>` for Aim and Alerts, or a `role="radiogroup"` from the ui form kit. - Use the FormSheet `Field`/`TextArea`, and set the tap target to at least 44px.
- **CVP-14 The retry and more buttons in the player file are hand-drawn** [P2] Player file, failed state, partial failure, and the desktop header "more" button. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/PlayerFile.tsx:106-118` (`FileFailed`), `:395-410` (`PartFailed`), `:365-369` (`button.ib` 36×36); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:121` (`.ch-hf-partfail button`), `:295` (`.dhead .ib`) Wrong:  - These are local copies of controls. - The 36×36 icon button is under 44px. - The partial-failure "Try again" is a bare text link with no focus ring. Fix: Use `<Button variant="secondary">Try again</Button>` inside ui `States`, and ui `IconButton` with `Menu` for "more". The hit area should be at least 44px even where the icon box is 36px.
- **CVP-15 Three-across stat wells are nested inside cards on phone, and "Focuses assigned" wraps** [P2] Player file, feature card comparison (`.feat__cmp`) and the three-tile row (`.tr3`), on phone, in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:133-137` (`.feat__cmp`), `:219-223` (`.tr3{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)}`) Wrong:  - At 390 wide each tile has about 76px of content inside the card's padding, so "Focuses assigned" and figures with units wrap to two or three lines and the tiles end up different heights. - The tinted wells inside a card are the card-in-card pattern the owner flagged on Coach Home. Fix:  - On phone, show them as one `Section` list of label/value rows, or as a two-column grid with the third tile spanning. - Drop the well background and separate the items with hairlines.
- **CVP-16 Off segments in the progress meter are 1.67:1 on the feature card** [P2] Player file, feature card week progress (`.prog`), in light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:267` (`.prog i{background:rgb(244 241 232/.18)}`) Wrong: The empty segments against the green feature surface are 1.67:1 in light and 1.65:1 in dark, so the number left to do can't be read. Fix: Use `rgb(244 241 232/.38)` (about 3.1:1), or the feature-surface track token, and show the "2 of 5" figure as text beside it.
- **CVP-17 The fold animates height and margin-top** [P2] Player file, expand/collapse sections, on phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/PlayerFile.tsx:47-88` (`Fold`, WAAPI keyframes on `height` and `marginTop`) Wrong:  - It animates layout properties, which triggers a reflow on every frame and stutters on long files. - Spec M-LNS-04 asks for a height fold, so this follows the spec, but it breaks H2. - The reduced-motion branch should be confirmed to skip the animation, not just shorten it. Fix: Use `grid-template-rows:0fr→1fr` with the duration tokens, or clip-path/transform. Skip the animation entirely when `prefers-reduced-motion` is set.
- **CVP-18 Feature cards and read rows put buttons inside role="button"; badge widths throw the titles out of line** [P2] Player file, feature card and "read" rows, on phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/cards.tsx:47-133` (`FeatureCard`, section `role="button"` contains buttons and links), `:402-421` (`readRow`, hand-drawn chevron SVG, variable-width badge before the title) Wrong:  - Nested interactive elements: one tap target swallows the inner actions for VoiceOver, and a tap on an inner link also fires the card. - The badge goes before the title and is a different width on each row, so the titles start at different x positions. Fix: Make the card a `Surface` with the primary action on its header only. Use `ListRow` with a trailing `Badge`, and the ui chevron.
- **CVP-19 The Your week large title is hand-drawn instead of ui LargeTitle** [P2] Your week, on phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/YourWeek.tsx` (`.dhead.is-week h1.lt`) Wrong: It uses its own font and margins, so the title sits at a different size and baseline from every other tab's LargeTitle and doesn't take part in the collapse into the phone bar. Fix: Use `<LargeTitle>` from `src/clubhouse/ui/`.
- **CVP-20 The Standing "behind" fill is a hard-coded amber gradient with no dark variant** [P2] Standing view, the behind-the-gap bar, in dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-standing.css:829` (`linear-gradient(90deg,#d9a441,#b8862b)`) Wrong: The fixed light-theme amber glares on the dark card, and it is not the warning token the rest of the app uses for "behind". Fix: Use `background:var(--ch-warn)` or `var(--ch-chart-warn)`, which have dark values. Drop the gradient.

### Stats (19)
- **STATS-1 Coach header on a player's Stats page sits 2px from the screen edge** [P0] Stats, player opened by a coach (`?player=`), phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:209-220` (`header.ch-spm-head`); `src/clubhouse/styles/stats.css:2485-2490` (`.ch-spm-head{padding:4px 2px 0}`); `src/clubhouse/styles/stats.css:2091` (`.ch-st.is-phone{padding:0}`) Wrong: The header is rendered outside `.ch-shs-sheet`, so it gets none of the sheet's page padding. The phone wrapper has zero padding, so the avatar, large title and message button sit 2px from the left and right screen edges. The cards below are inset 16 or 20px, so the header and the cards don't line up. Fix: Use `.ch-spm-head{padding:4px var(--ch-page-x-phone) 0}`, or move the header inside the `.ch-shs-sheet` padding box.
- **STATS-2 Green-reading diagram labels collide with arcs whose thickness comes from the data** [P1] Stats, player Overview, short-game zone, Green diagram, desktop, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/OverviewZones.tsx:584-614` (`GreenDiagram`) Wrong: Arc stroke width is `5 + (v/100)*28`, which runs from 5 to 33px on r=84 arcs. The direction labels sit at fixed points: - Left: x=62, end-anchored, y=119 - Right: x=238 - Long: y=24 The left arc spans x≈66±stroke/2, so once the left share is above about 21% (stroke ≥ 11) the stroke runs over the "Left" label. The fixture's left=45 gives a 17.6px stroke, so it already overlaps. The right label (238) and the long label (24) do the same against arcs at 234±16.5 and 31±16.5. This is the owner's TeamHole bug: a size derived from data, with labels at fixed coordinates. Fix: Cap the stroke, e.g. `Math.min(18, 5 + v/100*28)`, and place each label from the arc radius plus half its stroke plus 6. Or put the share in a legend row under the SVG.
- **STATS-3 GIR% figure in the Green diagram is 1.10:1 in dark mode** [P1] Stats, player Overview, Green diagram centre figure, desktop, dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/OverviewZones.tsx:600` (`fill="var(--ch-green-900)"` on a `--ch-green-100` disc) Wrong: In dark mode, `--ch-green-900` is #0a2a1b and `--ch-green-100` is #1a3024, a ratio of 1.10:1. The figure can't be seen. Light mode passes. Fix: Use `--ch-forest-900`, which tokens.css:26-29 documents as flipping to pale mint in dark mode, or `--ch-ink-1` on `--ch-green-100`.
- **STATS-4 Team chart's "Mean" label sits at a fixed left position and collides with the first point and the line** [P1] Stats, Team, Scoring feature card, phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/ScoringFeatureChart.tsx:285-286` (`<text x="4" y={y(mean)-6}>Mean …</text>` in `TeamFeatureChart`, :225-292) Wrong: The label is pinned at x=4, which is where the first data dot is drawn (x=0, r 3.4, half outside the SVG). The area and line pass through the label's box whenever early rounds are near the mean. When the mean is near the top of the range, `y(mean)-6` falls above y=0 and the label is clipped. Fix: Anchor the label at the right end of the mean line (`x={width-PAD.r}`, `textAnchor="end"`), clamp y to `[12, H-PAD.b]`, and inset the first point by r so it isn't cut in half.
- **STATS-5 Sticky Player/Team switch ignores the top safe area** [P1] Stats, phone (Home-screen web app and iOS standalone), light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:41-47` (`.ch-shs .ch-shs-ctl{position:sticky;top:var(--ch-phone-topbar-h,0px)}`) Wrong: Every other sticky element under the phone top bar adds `env(safe-area-inset-top)`: calendar.css:3933, classes.css:2153, roster.css:3034, shell.css:2534, coachhelm-file.css:62 and stats.css:66. This one doesn't, so with a notch or Dynamic Island the switch sticks 47–59px too high, under the bar. Fix: `top: calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top))`.
- **STATS-6 Gain and loss pills on the green Scoring card use the paper tint, not the feature tint** [P1] Stats, player and Team, Scoring feature card, phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:372-394` (`.ch-shs-pill.is-gain/.is-loss` read `--ch-chart-gain-tint` and `--ch-chart-gain` directly); the re-pointed tokens sit unused at `:254` (`--ch-shs-s-gain-tint`) Wrong: On the dark-green feature surface, the delta pill renders as a pale #e6f2ea chip with #155a39 text. The ratio is 7.15:1, so it's readable, but it's a light sticker on the green card. The design (redesign/stats-phone.css:124) uses `var(--gain-tint)`, which `.feature` re-points to a translucent tint. That's the "design CSS not matched to live tokens" pattern. Fix: Inside `.ch-shs-feat`, set `.ch-shs-pill.is-gain{background:var(--ch-shs-s-gain-tint);color:var(--ch-shs-s-gain)}`, and do the same for loss. Better, render `<DeltaChip tone="onFeature">` from `src/clubhouse/ui/`.
