- **STATS-7 Retry buttons and notices are hand-drawn instead of ui Button and InlineNotice** [P1] Stats, player and Team, error and partial-failure states, phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:324-354` (`FailedCard`); `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/StatsTeamPhoneShs.tsx:239-266` (`TeamFailedCard`); `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:889-918` (`.ch-shs-btn`) and `:919-940` (`.ch-shs-notice`) Wrong: These are local copies of the button and the notice, with their own height, radius, focus ring and pressed state. They don't follow Button's 44px minimum, the token focus ring or the Increase Contrast overrides. This is the "local copies of controls" pattern. Fix: Replace both with `<Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={retry}>Try again</Button>` inside the ui error state (`States` / `InlineNotice`), then delete `.ch-shs-btn` and `.ch-shs-notice`.
- **STATS-8 Summary cards nest buttons and a slider inside a role="link" section** [P1] Stats, player phone summary (Scoring, Rounds, Glance cards), phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/SummaryCard.tsx` (section `role="link"` wraps `button.ch-shs-dlink` and the chart's `role="slider"`) Wrong: Interactive elements inside an interactive element: VoiceOver reads it as one link. A tap on the chart scrubber also bubbles up and opens the detail, so you can't scrub without navigating away. The file also has local `ShsPill`, `ShsChevron` and `ShsCard` copies. Fix: Make the card a plain `Surface`. Put the link on the header row only (title plus chevron), and stop propagation from the chart. Swap `ShsPill` for `DeltaChip` or `Badge`, and `ShsCard` for `Surface`.
- **STATS-9 SVG axis text uses a Text Size type token, so labels get bigger than the chart's padding** [P1] Stats, Scoring feature chart (player and Team), phone, at Text Size XL and XXL. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:336-340` (`.ch-shs-ax{font:var(--ch-type-13)}`), used at `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/ScoringFeatureChart.tsx:193,212,216,265,276,280,285` Wrong: `--ch-type-13` scales with Text Size, reaching 17.7px at XXL. The chart's padding is fixed: `PAD.r` is 30 and `PAD.b` is 26. Y-axis figures such as "74.2" (~36px at 17.7px) overflow the right gutter, and the x labels run into the plot. Fix: Pin SVG text, e.g. `.ch-shs-ax{font:500 11px/1 var(--ch-font-num)}`, as other charts do. Or derive `PAD` from the measured label width.
- **STATS-10 Glance tiles stay three across on phone, and "Scrambling" overflows at 340px** [P2] Stats, player phone summary, "At a glance" card, phone 340–390, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:459-530` (`.ch-shs-glance`, `grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)`; drops to two columns only under `.is-big`) Wrong: Each column has ~91px of content at 390 wide and ~74px at 340. The 15px label "Scrambling" is ~75px wide, and figures like "62.5%" plus a delta chip don't fit at 340. This is the "multi-column kept on phone" pattern. Fix: Add `@container (max-width:360px){.ch-shs-glance{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}}`, keyed on the card's container rather than `.is-big`, plus `min-width:0` and an ellipsis on labels.
- **STATS-11 "Needs 5 rounds" rail text is nowrap and runs into the next columns** [P2] Stats, detail tables (thin-sample rows), phone, at large Text Size. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:1657-1666` (`.ch-shs-needs{white-space:nowrap}`) Wrong: At Text Size 1.36, the rail column is ~96px and the string is ~118px. With no overflow or ellipsis, it draws over the label and value columns. Fix: Add `overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;min-width:0`, or let it wrap to two lines (`white-space:normal`).
- **STATS-12 Ribbon chart's reference label and figures share a fixed 40px gutter** [P2] Stats, detail ribbon (SG over rounds, vs Tour or Mean), phone, at large Text Size. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:1176-1184` (`.ch-shs-rib{padding-right:40px}`, `.ch-shs-rib .rref{right:-42px}`); overlap guard in `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/ShsBlocks.tsx` (`Ribbon`, `Math.abs(y(v)-y(ref)) < 14`) Wrong: The "Tour" / "Mean" label and the `.ry` figures are placed with absolute positioning in a 40px gutter. The 14-unit collision guard is in viewBox units and doesn't scale with font size. At 17.7px, "Tour" (~38px) plus a figure collide and spill past the card's right padding. Fix: Measure the gutter from the label (`ch` units or `max-content`), and run the collision guard in CSS px using the actual line height.
- **STATS-13 Team "Who gains and loses" truncates names in a fixed 104px column** [P2] Stats, Team, gains-and-losses rows, phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:1847-1855` (`.ch-shs-prow{grid-template-columns:20px 104px 1fr 44px}`) Wrong: Long names like "Christopher Bainbridge" (~150px at 15px) are cut at 104px, and the bar column (`1fr`) keeps the extra room. At 390 wide the bar gets ~170px, which is more than it needs. Fix: Use `grid-template-columns:20px minmax(0,1.2fr) minmax(64px,1fr) 44px` and show initial plus last name with ellipsis.
- **STATS-14 Tee-direction stack: Left and Right fills are 1.18:1 apart** [P2] Stats, player, Off the tee detail, direction stack, phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:613-617, 633-646` (`ch-shs-fill-flat` on Left, `ch-shs-fill-miss` on Right) Wrong: The two miss segments contrast with each other at 1.18:1 in light and 1.29:1 in dark. Only the order and a legend tell them apart. Fix: Label the segments inline with "L 18%" and "R 22%", or add a 2px `--ch-card-bg` gap between them and hatch one side.
- **STATS-15 Score-mix bar uses raw px and weight 700, off the type scale** [P2] Stats, Scoring detail (`GameDetail asDetail`), phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats.css:7137-7139` (`.ch-sv-mix-seg b{font:700 13px/1}`), `:7201` (`.ch-sv-mix-key b{font:700 17px/1}`), `:7224` (`font-size:12px`), `:7259-7283` (phone block, raw 15px and 14px) Wrong: The figures ignore Text Size, use a weight that isn't on the scale, and sit next to `--ch-type-*` text, so they look off. Fix: Use `font:var(--ch-type-13-num)` / `var(--ch-type-17-num)` with the scale's weight. Keep raw px only inside the SVG segment labels.
- **STATS-16 Skeleton doesn't match the loaded layout (fixed heights; coach header missing)** [P2] Stats, loading, phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/ShsSkeleton.tsx` (fixed card heights 342, 193, …; no header for `?player=`) Wrong: Card heights are px constants, so they're too short at large Text Size and the page jumps when it loads. When a coach opens a player, the 64px header pops in above everything. Fix: Render the `ch-spm-head` placeholder when `coach` is set. Size the placeholders in `em` or with `--ch-type-*` line heights.
- **STATS-17 Rounds ticker with a single round draws one full-width grey block** [P2] Stats, player, Rounds card, partial state (1 round), phone. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/MiniCharts.tsx` (`tickerBars`, bar width = 300/n) Wrong: With n=1 the one bar fills the 300-wide viewBox, so it reads as a broken slab rather than one round. Fix: Cap the bar width, e.g. `Math.min(14, 300/n)`, and right-align. Or show the partial-state copy ("1 round — more after your next") instead of the chart.
- **STATS-18 Sticky switch's scroll shadow is raw rgb with no dark value** [P2] Stats, phone, dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:1937-1948` (`.ch-shs-ctl::before`, gradient `rgb(0 0 0/.1)`), `:2236` (raw-rgb shadow) Wrong: The fixed black shadow is invisible on the dark grouped background (#0e120f), so there's no edge between the switch and the scrolling content in dark mode. Fix: Use the shell's scroll-edge token (`--ch-shadow-1` / `--ch-hairline`), as `shell.css` does.
- **STATS-19 Desktop Stats still has around 50 raw px font declarations** [P2] Stats desktop (Overview, Game detail, strokes-gained table), light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats.css` (e.g. `:5401 .ch-sgt__callout{font:700 15px}`, `:7031`; RUBRIC already lists `:5365` and `:6556`) Wrong: Text set in raw px ignores Text Size and drifts from the 11-step scale next to tokenised neighbours. Fix: Replace them with `--ch-type-*` tokens. `grep -nE "font(-size)?:\s*[0-9]{3}? ?[0-9]+px" src/clubhouse/styles/stats.css` lists them.

### Motion across all lanes (45)
- **MOT-01 The Reduce Motion floor turns every lane's CSS 180ms fade into a hard cut** [P1] every screen under iOS Reduce Motion, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/base.css:303-311` (`.ch-root * { transition-duration: 1ms !important; animation-duration: 1ms !important; animation-iteration-count: 1 !important }`). Lane fades it overrides: - `messages.css:5402`, `:5704`, `:5811` (`var(--ch-fade-ms, var(--ch-dur-quick))` without `!important`) - `coachhelm-ask-v2.css:476-477`, `:498` - `quick-chat.css:27,128,352` (`0.18s linear`) - `screens/stats/shs-motion.tsx:86` (inline `animationDuration = 180ms`) Wrong: An `!important` stylesheet duration beats any normal or inline declaration, so each lane's own Reduce-Motion crossfade plays in 1ms. Only WAAPI (dialog-lifetime, team-motion, useQcMotion) and the two `!important` Team rules (`coachhelm-team.css:1909`, `:1916`) actually fade. Separately: - `--ch-fade-ms` is defined nowhere. - `--ch-dur-crossfade-rm` (180) is defined only inside the Animations-off block (`tokens.css:1306`) and never used. - Ask uses `--ch-dur-rm-fade` (120) instead of 180. Fix:  1. Define `--ch-dur-crossfade-rm: 180ms` on `[data-ui='clubhouse']` root (not only under `data-motion='off'`). 2. Narrow the floor so it exempts opacity-only fades: `transition-property: opacity` or a `[data-ch-rm-fade]` opt-out. 3. Replace `--ch-fade-ms` and `--ch-dur-rm-fade` in lanes with `--ch-dur-crossfade-rm`. 4. Move Stats' ghost fade to WAAPI like `dialog-lifetime.ts:139`.
- **MOT-02 Stats cards grow their bars, rings and marks the first time they scroll into view** [P1] S-STATS / S-TEAMSTATS summary and detail cards, phone (SHS), light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/shs-motion.tsx:15-38` (`useGrowOnce`: `.is-waiting` until 25% in view); `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:1887-1911` (`[data-ch-grow]` `scaleX(0)`, `[data-ch-grow-y]` `scaleY(0)`, ring `stroke-dashoffset: var(--ch-ring-c) !important`, opacity 0 → 280ms `--ch-ease`) Wrong: Every card above the fold draws in on first paint, and every card below draws in as it scrolls up, over 280ms. That is the draw-in the owner banned. It also prints zero-length bars in the first frame of each card. Fix: Delete `useGrowOnce` and the `.is-waiting` rules; marks render at rest. Keep the window-change glide (`useGlide`) as the only chart motion.
- **MOT-03 Team hole fly-in runs on first paint and hides the stations for up to about 2.2s** [P1] S-TEAM.hole, first visit in a session, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/team-motion.ts:13-90` (`useFlyIn`); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:267-275` (`.stn.pre { transform: scale(.3); opacity: 0 }`) Wrong:  1. The ball flies 1900ms after `fonts.ready` + 250ms, while each station stays invisible until the ball passes it. Putting is hidden about 1710ms + 250ms + font wait. 2. It is driven by rAF, writing SVG `cx`/`cy` attributes and `strokeDashoffset` every frame on the main thread. That is not compositor motion: it competes with hydration and Lenis. 3. The Animations-off guard reads `useChReducedMotion()` inside the first layout effect, which still holds the server snapshot (`showAnimations` default on). The code covers that gap for the OS query (`window.matchMedia`) but not for `data-motion='off'`, so a hard load with Animations off can still fly. 4. Stations pop on `--ch-ease-spring` (`cubic-bezier(.34,1.3,.64,1)`, about 10% overshoot) on data-sized circles. Fix: Remove `useFlyIn`, the `.pre` state and the `.ball`. Draw the flight path and stations at rest. If the owner wants the flight kept, run it only on an explicit "Play" tap, as the design page has, through WAAPI `offset-path`.
- **MOT-04 Both Homes grow the team-legs bars on every mount** [P1] Coach Home and Player Home (CRH on), phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/home.css:4806` and `:5341` (`animation: ch-hm-leg-grow var(--ch-dur-release) var(--ch-ease) both`, keyframe `:4841-4845` `scaleX(0)`) Wrong: A 280ms grow from zero plays on first paint and on every return to Home (tab switch, Back), because a CSS animation restarts whenever the element mounts. It also plays on a quiet refresh that remounts the block. Fix: Delete the `animation` declarations and `@keyframes ch-hm-leg-grow`; the bars render at rest. The reduce-motion blocks at `:4846-4858` then go too.
- **MOT-05 Team "Working on" proposed rows pop in with an overshoot on every mount** [P1] S-TEAM.working, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Cards.tsx:143` (`className="wk is-new"` for every `proposed` row, including server rows with `read.assigned === 'proposed'`); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:1896-1903` (`ch-htb-wk` scale .94 → 1 over 520ms on `--ch-ease-spring`, overshoot to about 1.03) Wrong: The pop is meant for the moment a write answers (M-TEAM-28). Instead it plays on first paint and on every remount for any row already proposed in the data, and it springs past its mark on a data row. Fix: Add `is-new` only for ids in a "just proposed this session" set (`fa.assigned[read.id]` set by the tap), and drop it after the animation ends. Use `--ch-ease-spring-smooth` (no overshoot) or a fade.
- **MOT-06 Read "Where it shows up" bars, missed dots and path nodes draw in when scrolled into view** [P1] S-READ.where, S-READ.why, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/read/cards.tsx:75-81` (`useInViewOnce` → `'fun' + (seen ? '' : ' pre')`); `src/clubhouse/lib/use-in-view.ts:9-32`; `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-read.css:471-489` (`.fun.pre .fbar { scaleX(0) }` → 360ms `--ch-dur-marks`), `:547-561` (`.dots.pre i.f` scale .4, opacity 0) Wrong: The server render ships every bar at `scaleX(0)` and the dots at opacity 0. They draw when 35% of the card enters the viewport, which is first paint for an above-the-fold card. With JS slow or failing, the funnel stays empty. Fix: Drop `useInViewOnce` and the `.pre` classes; render at rest. Remove the `useInViewOnce` hook if this is its last user (it is: see the grep in this audit).
- **MOT-07 Stats glides SG bars, rank dots and team legs by `left`/`width`, re-rendering React every frame** [P1] S-STATS SG legs, S-TEAMSTATS rank and legs, on a window change, phone and desktop. File:  - `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/SgBars.tsx:60-66` (`[left|right]: '50%', width: …%` from `drawn`) - `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/TeamRank.tsx:85-86` (`left`, `width`) - `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/StatsTeamPhone.tsx:405` (`left`/`right`, `width`) - driver `src/clubhouse/lib/motion-glide.ts:47-58` (rAF → `setShown` each frame for 260ms) - `src/clubhouse/ui/charts/Line.tsx:276` Wrong: Each window tap re-renders every chart component about 16 times and lays out each bar per frame (H2: animating `width`/`left`). Several charts glide at once on Stats, on the main thread, during the same frames as the WindowCrossfade clone (MOT-23). The line morph runs 260ms `CH_EASE`, where M-STATS-10 asks for 360ms ease-out cubic. Fix: Do what `LegBars.tsx:79-93` already does: keep `left`/`width` at the target and glide with `spanTransform`/`shiftTransform`. Better, compute the transform once and run one WAAPI animation per mark instead of `useGlide` state. Set the line morph to 360ms.
- **MOT-08 Player file lens fold animates `height` and `margin-top` for 462ms** [P1] S-FILE.lens flip, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/PlayerFile.tsx:46-90` (`Fold`: `el.animate([{height:'0px', marginTop:'-24px'}, {height:`${h}px`, marginTop:'0px'}], spring 462)`) Wrong:  - It relayouts the whole file below the fold every frame for 462ms. It runs at the same moment as four or five `Swap` height transitions (MOT-09) on the shared cards. - `-24px` is a literal that assumes the card gap and goes wrong when the gap token changes. - The spec row M-LNS-04 itself asks for height, which conflicts with H2. Fix:  1. Fold with a FLIP: fade the leaving card (180) and translate the cards below by `-h` → 0 on the smooth spring, using `useRowFlip`'s pattern from `team-motion.ts:93-111`. 2. Or take the lens-only cards out of flow (`position:absolute`) while fading. 3. Raise the spec conflict to the owner.
- **MOT-09 `ui/Swap` eases the frame's `height` on every swap; the lenses fire five at once** [P1] S-FILE.lens and any `Swap` with the default `resize="ease"`, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/ui/Swap.tsx:76-116` (`el.style.transition = 'height var(--ch-dur-quick) var(--ch-ease)'`); users `screens/coachhelm/file/PlayerFile.tsx:250-261` Wrong: A height transition on a block frame reflows everything after it for 180ms (H2). The file explains `resize="instant"` exists because of exactly this (F-0007) but keeps `ease` as the default. The lens flip stacks five such frames plus MOT-08. Fix: Make `resize="instant"` the default. Where a height change must read, translate the following siblings by the delta (FLIP) instead of animating `height`.
- **MOT-10 Desktop sidebar fold animates `width`, `margin`, `padding` and `grid-template-columns`** [P1] rail fold / unfold, desktop 1440, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:4047` (`.ch-sidebar { transition: width 462 spring-smooth }`), `:4111-4113` (`.ch-brand` margin + padding), `:4115` (`gap`), `:4121-4140` (`.ch-brand__fold` `grid-template-columns` 1fr → 0fr) Wrong: Every frame of the 462ms spring relayouts the sidebar and the page column beside it. `lib/rail-fold.ts` already has to pause the page's size readers (`data-ch-rail-moving`) and drop backdrop blur because of it. This is the exact H2 grep hit (`transition:.*width`). Fix: Keep the rail at its full width and clip it with `clip-path: inset(0 calc(100% - var(--ch-rail-w)) 0 0)`, or `translateX` the label column. Slide the page column with `translate` and settle the real `--ch-sidebar-w` once at the end (one layout). Fade the brand word instead of a 0fr track.
- **MOT-11 Team story timer restarts a full 5200ms after a press-and-hold while the bar resumes mid-way** [P1] S-TEAM.story, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Stories.tsx:124-129` (`setTimeout(next, STORY_MS)` re-armed on every `held` change); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:1844-1849` (`animation-play-state: paused`) Wrong: Hold for 2s at 3s into a story, then release. The CSS bar resumes from about 58% and fills at about 5.2s of elapsed bar time. The JS timer starts again from 0 and advances only 5.2s after release, so the bar sits full for about 3s before the story moves on. The longer the hold, the larger the drift. This is not "accurate". Fix: Drive the advance from the bar's own `animationend` (or WAAPI `finished`) instead of a separate timer; pause by `animation.pause()`. Under Reduce Motion keep the no-auto-advance path.
- **MOT-12 Desktop Team inspector slides out empty** [P1] S-TEAM.inspector close (× / Esc), desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamBoard.tsx:384-386` (`<Inspector open={!!openPanel}>{openPanel}</Inspector>`); `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Inspector.tsx:28`; `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:1893-1896` (out 280ms) Wrong: `openPanel` becomes `null` on close, so the content is gone in the first frame and a blank 420px panel slides away for 280ms. The phone sheet doesn't do this because `ui/Modal` retains content through `dialog-lifetime.ts` `retainContent`. Fix: Keep the last non-null panel in a ref (as `useDialogLifetime(...).retainContent` does) and render it until `transitionend`.
- **MOT-13 Team sub-tab switches hard-cut on desktop, while Board, Ask and Standing crossfade** [P1] CoachHelm sub-tabs Board ↔ Team ↔ Ask, desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamBoard.tsx:360` (`<main className="ch-htb …" aria-busy={sw.pending}>`, no `{...sw.enter}`); enter and dim rules exist only for `.ch-hl` (`styles/coachhelm.css:1062`, `:1078-1080`) and `.ch-ask` (`styles/coachhelm-ask.css:1100-1102`, `:1393`) Wrong: Leaving Team, the board isn't dimmed during the wait. The next view then starts its rise at `--ch-enter-from` (about .55), which shows a dip. Arriving at Team, the new board appears in one frame. The other sub-views get the dim plus 180ms rise. Two transition styles on one tab bar. Fix: Spread `{...sw.enter}` on the Team `<main>` and add `.ch-htb[aria-busy='true']:not([aria-label^='Loading'])` dim and `.ch-htb[data-ch-enter]:not(.is-phone) > *` enter rules, matching `coachhelm.css:1062-1080`.
- **MOT-14 Ask's phone artifact / full-plan push is a hand-rolled CSS slide, not the shell push** [P1] S-ASK.full-plan, S-FMT.see, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Artifact.tsx:77` (`.ch-ak-push`); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:470-474` (`ch-ak-slide-in` / `-out`, smooth spring both ways) Wrong:  - The page beneath doesn't draw back by 24% or dim. - There is no edge-swipe Back. - The exit uses the in-spring instead of a 280 ease. - A Back mid-entry jumps: a keyframe with no `from` restarts from the underlying value, not where the slide had got to. - Under Reduce Motion it fades 120ms, not 180. - Every other lane's push (Stats, Messages, Roster, Read, File) uses PhoneScreen or the route push. Fix: Render the phone artifact in `shell/PhoneScreen.tsx` (underlay, dim, edge swipe and interruptible spring come with it). Delete `ch-ak-slide-*`.
- **MOT-15 Stats sticky window switch ignores the safe area, and its stuck shade is per-scroll React state** [P1] S-STATS.stuck, phone (standalone or native shell with a notch). File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:41-47` (`position: sticky; top: var(--ch-phone-topbar-h, 0px)` = 50px); `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/shs-motion.tsx:44-62` (`useStuck`: window scroll → rAF → `setStuck(scrollY > 1)`) Wrong:  - Every other phone sticky sits at `calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top))`: `classes.css:2153`, `roster.css:3034`, `shell.css:2534`, `calendar.css:3933`. With a 47-59px inset, the stuck switch slides 47-59px under the green bar. - "Stuck" means `scrollY > 1`, not that the switch has reached the bar, so the shade appears the moment the page moves. Fix: `top: calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top))`. Drive `.is-stuck` from an IntersectionObserver on a 1px sentinel above the switch, with `rootMargin` equal to the bar height.
- **MOT-16 Animations off doesn't stop the hard-coded loops in Quick chat, Ask, Read and the file** [P1] Settings > Animations off, phone and desktop. File:  - Quick chat (no `[data-motion='off']` rule anywhere in the file): `quick-chat.css:31,356` (`2.4s infinite`), `:40,377` (`0.9s infinite`), `:79` (`1.2s infinite`), `:90` (`1.9s infinite`), `:111` (`1.6s infinite`), `:113` (`1s steps(1) infinite`) - Ask: `coachhelm-ask-v2.css:32` (`0.6s infinite alternate`), `:70` (`1.2s infinite`), `:103` (`0.9s infinite`), `:130` (`1.1s infinite`) - Read: `coachhelm-read.css:873` (`1.6s infinite`) is covered by `:1107` - File: `coachhelm-file.css:253` (`1.9s infinite`) Wrong:  - Animations off works by zeroing the `--ch-dur-*` tokens (`tokens.css:1292-1324`). Literal durations ignore that, so these loops keep moving with Animations off. That is a rubric H19 fail ("anything still moves"). - Quick chat's `.is-reduce` comes from `useChReducedMotion()`, which also covers Animations off, so Animations off still fades 180ms (`quick-chat.css:27,128,352`) instead of swapping at once. Fix:  1. Replace the literals with tokens: `--ch-dur-shimmer`, `--ch-dur-typing`, `--ch-dur-sweep`, or new `--ch-dur-spin`. 2. Have Quick chat read `useChMotionMode()` and set `is-reduce` only for `'fade'` and `is-off` for `'off'`. 3. Add `[data-motion='off'] .ch-qcs *, .ch-qck * { animation: none; transition: none }`.
- **MOT-17 `width` and `grid-template-rows` transitions in Ask's brief dots and Messages' poll composer** [P1] S-ASK brief (dots), S-MSG.poll-composer (custom rows), phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:489` (`.qdots i { transition: width 417 settle }`); `src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:4016-4021` (`.ch-ms-pollf__custom { transition: grid-template-rows 260 }`) Wrong: Both are exact H2 grep hits. The poll one opens 0fr → 1fr and reflows the composer sheet each frame. The dot one relayouts the dot row on a spring that overshoots, so the width bounces past its mark. Fix: Dots: fixed-size dots, with the active pill drawn by `transform: scaleX()` from a centred origin. Poll: show the rows at full height and fade plus `translateY(-6px)` them in, moving the content below with a FLIP. Or open instantly, as M-TEAM-17 does for Show more.
- **MOT-18 Lane spinners stop turning under Reduce Motion** [P1] Assign / Proposing / live pill under iOS Reduce Motion. File:  - `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-read.css:388` (`ch-spinner-turn .7s infinite`) with `:1101-1105` (1ms) and the base floor `iteration-count: 1` - `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:1237` (`spin .7s infinite`) with `:1683-1689` - `src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:41,378` (`.is-reduce .live__spin { animation: none }`) Wrong: The floor exempts only `.ch-spinner` (`base.css:312-318`). These lane spinners get one 1ms turn and then sit still, which reads as stuck. The specs say "spinner still", but OQ-18 / H19 is the newer owner rule. Fix: Use `ui/Spinner.tsx` (`.ch-spinner`) in all three places, or add the class so the floor's exemption applies.
- **MOT-19 Team Course trend draws and staggers its dots, including on first paint from a deep link** [P1] S-TEAM.course, phone and desktop; `?station=course`. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/team-motion.ts:131-144` (`useTrendDraw`: line 900ms `cubic-bezier(.3,.7,.2,1)` literal; dots `delay: 120 + i * 55`; labels `delay: 760`); `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamBoard.tsx:161` (`initialStation`) Wrong: The effect runs on mount of `CourseCard`. A board opened with `station=course` therefore draws the line and staggers 11 dots on first paint, for about 1090ms. Even on a tap it is a stagger (banned in D-64), with a literal easing and literal delays. Fix: Draw the trend at rest. If any motion is kept on the Course tap, use one 260 fade of the card (M-TEAM-12) and no per-dot delay.
- **MOT-20 Ask's Undo ring empties at once under Reduce Motion while Undo is still live** [P1] S-ASK.saved / published receipt, Reduce Motion. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:118` (`ch-ak-drain 8s linear forwards`); base floor `base.css:308-309` Wrong: The spec says the ring is "a timer, not motion" and stays the same under Reduce Motion (coachhelm 07 M-UNDO-ring). The floor shortens the drain to 1ms, so the ring shows empty and implies the window has closed, while the server still accepts Undo for 8s. Fix: Mark the ring `.ch-spinner`-style exempt: `animation-duration: 8s !important` under reduce. Or draw it from the same JS clock as the server window.
- **MOT-21 Team station pulse ring scales the uncapped halo by 1.7, over the header and labels** [P1] S-TEAM.hole station select, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:285-300` (`.pulse` `scale(1)→scale(1.7)` over `2.4s` × 2, `transform-box: fill-box`); radius from `TeamHole.tsx` (`10 + |v|×22`, see AUDITOR_BRIEF §1) Wrong: At v = −3.9 the halo radius is about 96, so the pulse ring reaches about 163 units: wider than the hole. It sweeps over the header figure and the labels twice per tap, and since the SVG is `overflow: visible` it can leave the card. The 2.4s duration is a literal. Fix: Clamp the halo radius (the team-lane fix) and clamp the pulse to `min(1.7, (R+12)/R)`. Use `--ch-dur-live`-class tokens.
- **MOT-22 Framer and WAAPI fades pass `reduced` as "duration 0" instead of the 180ms fade, and Animations off still fades** [P2] Reduce Motion and Animations off, phone and desktop. File:  - `chTween(…, reduced)` (`lib/motion.ts:41-43` returns `duration: 0` when reduced): `ui/Menu.tsx:184`, `screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:1453`, `:1524`, `screens/messages/seen-by.tsx:77,282`, `screens/messages/polls.tsx:177-178`, `screens/roster/RosterPeek.tsx:89`, `screens/home/LatestRound.tsx:77` (`chSwap`), `ui/Toast.tsx:390`, `shell/OfflineBanner.tsx:65`, `shell/TeamSwitch.tsx:135` - The reverse case, an Animations-off fade: `screens/coachhelm/team/team-motion.ts:122`, `screens/coachhelm/file/PlayerFile.tsx:175`, `shell/quick-chat/useQcMotion.ts:26-29`, `screens/messages/hold-lift.tsx:65` Wrong: Under Reduce Motion, menus, popovers, toasts and the round pager appear in one frame instead of fading over 180ms. Under Animations off, the second group still fades 180ms because it reads the boolean `useChReducedMotion()` (OS reduce **or** Animations off). Fix: Replace `chTween(x, reduced)` with `reduced ? chFade(mode) : chTween(x)` using `useChMotionMode()`, as `shell/AccountSheet.tsx:83,163` and `shell/Bell.tsx:168` already do.
- **MOT-23 Stats window crossfade clones the whole sheet DOM on every window tap** [P2] S-STATS / S-TEAMSTATS window change, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/shs-motion.tsx:63-110` (`getSnapshotBeforeUpdate` → `this.el.cloneNode(true)` of `.ch-shs-sheet`, appended `position:absolute; inset:0`, 260ms literal `XFADE_MS`) Wrong:  - A deep clone of every card (SVG charts included) is built and painted over the live page during the same frames as the `useGlide` re-renders (MOT-07). That doubles the layers being composited on a phone. - The ghost covers the new content at its own height, so a shorter new window shows ghost content past its end for 260ms. - Under Reduce Motion the inline 180ms is overridden to 1ms (MOT-01). Fix: Crossfade only the figure blocks, keyed by window (a `Swap` with `resize="instant"` on each figure), not a clone of the sheet. Use `CH_DUR.base` and not a literal.
- **MOT-24 The segmented thumb slides by a non-uniform scale and jumps under Reduce Motion** [P2] any segment change with unequal widths ("Coach view" vs "As Eli sees it"), phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/lib/slide-plate.ts:62-68` (`translate(dx,dy) scale(w0/w1, h0/h1)` on `.ch-seg__pill`); reduced path `:51` (`return` with no animation) Wrong:  - Scaling the pill by width ratio stretches its corner radius and its `--ch-seg-on-shadow` during the move. A 1.6× ratio draws visibly oval ends mid-slide; the iOS thumb keeps its radius. - Under Reduce Motion the thumb jumps, with no 180ms crossfade. - Stats asks for 360ms `--ch-ease-spring` (M-STATS-07); the control gives 417 settle. Fix: Animate `translate` plus an inner `clip-path: inset(… round var(--ch-radius))` (or `width` on a `will-change: transform` pseudo replaced by two end caps), so the radius is constant. Under reduced, crossfade the old and new pill over `CH_FADE_MS`.
- **MOT-25 Literal durations and easings across Ask, the file, Quick chat, Read and Messages** [P2] listed below, all phone and desktop. File:  - `coachhelm-ask-v2.css:17,23,27,31,113` (`0.26s`, `0.24s`, `0.62s`) - `coachhelm-file.css:113` (`0.26s`), `:172` (`0.18s`), `:245` (`0.18s`, `var(--ch-hf-ease)`) - `quick-chat.css:25,350` (`opacity 0.2s` with **no easing**, `0.26s`), `:126` (`0.2s ease`), `:295-296` (`0.28s` / `0.11s`) - `shell/quick-chat/useQcMotion.ts:12` (`90`), `:36` (scrim 260 where M-SCRIM is 240 linear), `:51` (`220`), `:55` (scrim close 220 sheet where M-SCRIM is 200 linear) - `screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:1981` (`260`, `"cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)"`) - `screens/coachhelm/team/team-motion.ts:8` (`EASE` string) - `coachhelm-read.css:393` (assigned tick 280, where M-READ-09 is 360) Wrong: Literals don't follow Animations off (MOT-16) or the D-64 scale. Several also differ from their own spec rows (Quick chat scrim, Read tick). Fix: Use `var(--ch-dur-*)` and `var(--ch-ease*)` in CSS, and `CH_DUR` / `CH_EASE` / `CH_FADE_MS` in TS. Add `--ch-dur-scrim-in: 240ms` and `--ch-dur-scrim-out: 200ms` if Quick chat's M-SCRIM values stand.
- **MOT-26 Spring curves are played at durations they weren't sampled for** [P2] listed below. File:  - `coachhelm-ask-v2.css:27` (`0.26s` + `--ch-ease-spring-smooth`, sampled for 462) - `coachhelm-file.css:172` (`0.18s` + spring-smooth) - `coachhelm-team.css:440` (`--ch-dur-reveal` 520 + spring-smooth 462), `:1168`, `:1389` - `team-motion.ts:107` (520 with `chSpringCurve('smooth').linear`, whose natural length is `c.ms`) - `messages.css:4764` (`--ch-dur-reveal` 520 + `--ch-ease-spring-lift`, sampled for 471) Wrong: A `linear()` spring is a sampled position-over-time curve. Squeezing 462ms of physics into 180 or 260ms makes it snap. Stretching it to 520 makes it drag, and the motion no longer matches the springs in the rest of the app. Fix: Pair each curve with its own `--ch-dur-spring-*`, or use `c.ms` from `chSpringCurve`. Where a 260 tween is wanted, use `--ch-ease`.
- **MOT-27 Every lane writes its own skeleton shimmer; two lane skeletons skip the route hold and fade** [P2] loading states, phone and desktop. File:  - Cadences: Team `coachhelm-team.css:1423` (1.4s); Read `coachhelm-read.css:873` (1.6s pulse); Ask `coachhelm-ask-v2.css:70` (1.2s); Quick chat `quick-chat.css:111` (1.6s); File `coachhelm-file.css:253` (1.9s literal); Stats `stats-redesign.css:956` (1.9s literal); Messages `messages.css:4179` (900ms pulse, where M-MSG-31 is 1400). The shared one is `shell.css:853-866` (`--ch-dur-shimmer` 1.9s). - Missing `aria-label^='Loading'`, so `base.css:285-294` doesn't apply: `screens/coachhelm/team/TeamBoardSkeleton.tsx:33,46` and `screens/coachhelm/file/FileSkeleton.tsx:22`. Wrong: Seven different sheen speeds across one app. The Team and File skeletons show on the first frame of a navigation, with no 300ms hold and no fade, so a fast load flashes them. The Team skeleton's hole is `height: 300` (`TeamBoardSkeleton.tsx:39`) while the real hole is a width-scaled SVG (`TeamHole.tsx:35` viewBox), so the page shifts when it lands at any width other than the one measured. Fix: Use `.ch-skel` / `.ch-skel--feature` everywhere and add `aria-label="Loading …"` to both `<main>`s. Give the hole skeleton `aspect-ratio: H.w / H.h`.
- **MOT-28 Plan cards and Ask brief cards replay their entry on every mount** [P2] Ask thread with a plan card or brief, reopened or reloaded, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:61-62` (`.ch-cp-strip i { animation: ch-cp-seg 280 both }` from `scaleX(0)`); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:485` (`.qcard { animation: ch-ak-rise 417 settle }`) Wrong: The strip grows and the brief card rises whenever the thread mounts, including history and reload. That is first-paint motion on content the coach is only reading. M-ASK-11 means the strip to grow as the plan is written, once. Fix: Gate both on the `fresh` prop that `PlanCard` already receives (`PlanCard.tsx:108,139`) and on a "new this session" flag for the brief.
- **MOT-29 Ask summary pills stagger by index** [P2] S-ASK brief summary, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:496` (`animation-delay: calc(var(--ch-i) * var(--ch-dur-press) / 2.75)`, about 40ms steps) Wrong: An entrance stagger. Quick chat's 30ms content stagger is the only one sanctioned (`lib/motion.ts:10-12`). Fix: Drop the delay; all pills fade together over base.
- **MOT-30 Press tints are missing or late on Roster face cards and Player-file rows** [P2] phone, light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/roster.css:280-282` (`.ch-rs-face[data-ch-pressed] { transform: translateY(0) }`: no tint, no scale outside canopy); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:126,149,278` (`transition-delay: var(--ch-dur-press)` = 110ms) Wrong:  - A phone face card gives no visible answer to a press. Only the desktop canopy variant (`roster.css:2153-2155`) tints. - Player-file rows and cards wait 110ms before tinting, where the system and M-LNS-03 say 50ms (`--ch-dur-press-delay`). A quick tap therefore often shows nothing. Fix: `background: var(--ch-ledger-row-press)` on the phone face rule. Switch the file rules to `transition-delay: var(--ch-dur-press-delay)`.
- **MOT-31 Ask v2 and Practice plan controls have no press or hover states** [P2] brief options `.opt`, receipt link `.lk`, confirm secondary, plan blocks, `.saverow`, folder rows `.fr`; phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css` (0 `[data-ch-pressed]`, 0 `:active`, 0 `:hover`); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css` (0 pressed, 0 hover, 0 focus-visible rules in 1573 lines); `data-ch-press` only at `chat/parts/FollowUps.tsx:17`, `BriefCard.tsx:88`, `Artifact.tsx:13` Wrong: Most tappable surfaces in both lanes answer a finger with nothing until the tap completes, and desktop rows have no hover. The global `:focus-visible` ring (`base.css:110`) is the only state they get. Fix: Add `data-ch-press=".97"` to cards and keys. Add a `[data-ch-pressed]` tint (`--ch-ledger-row-press`, 50ms delay) to rows, and `@media (hover:hover)` hover tints to match `messages.css` rows.
- **MOT-32 Read desktop "More" opens a centred dialog, not a popover from the ⋯** [P2] S-READ-DESK.more, desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/read/ReadPage.tsx:184-203` (`<Modal …>` on both phone and desktop) Wrong: The spec wants a popover scaling from .96 at the ⋯ (220 in, 120 out). Desktop gets a full dialog rising from the centre, so the motion comes from the wrong origin. Fix: Use `ui/Menu.tsx` anchored to the ⋯ on desktop; keep `Modal` on phone.
- **MOT-33 Ask's desktop artifact pane pushes the chat column in one frame** [P2] S-ASKD.pane open / close, desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:134` (`.ch-ak-pane { width: 452px; flex: none }`), `:473-474` (slide in / out on the smooth spring) Wrong: The pane slides on transform, but the chat column beside it narrows by 452px on the first frame. The text reflows and jumps under the reader while the pane is still off-screen. Closing waits the full spring and then widens the column at once. Both directions use the in-spring; exits should be the 280 ease. Fix: Overlay the pane (`position: absolute` with a shadow) during the slide and commit the column width at the end. Or slide the chat column by `translateX` in step and commit once. Exit on `--ch-dur-release` / `--ch-ease`.
- **MOT-34 Team story viewer closes with no exit** [P2] S-TEAM.story close (×, Done, last timer, story action), phone. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Stories.tsx:118,163,286-291` (`onClose` → `setOpen(null)` unmounts); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:1885-1888` (rise on mount only) Wrong: The full-screen green viewer disappears in one frame. The rise is a CSS keyframe, so a close mid-rise can't reverse from where it is (M-TEAM-30). Fix: Render the viewer through `useDialogLifetime(open, { direction: 'bottom' })` (as `History.tsx` does), which gives an exit, retargeting and the RM fade.
- **MOT-35 Spec'd crossfades and opens that hard-cut** [P2] listed below. File:  - `screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:433` (`{menu && <div className="flist">…}`: folder list, M-ASK-17) - `styles/coachhelm-plan.css` has no rule for the swap name or Changed chip (M-ASK-14, 15) - `styles/messages.css:4073-4077` (desktop details column appears with no transition, M-MSG-20b) - `screens/coachhelm/read/cards.tsx:408` (an opacity .7 → 1 blink instead of the 2px gain ring over 900ms; `.flash` at `coachhelm-read.css:830-842` is unused, M-READ-10) Wrong: Each of these is a jump-cut where the spec has a timed open or crossfade. Fix: Folder list: `Swap` or a WAAPI fade + 6px rise, with a FLIP for the rows below. Messages details: slide the 300px pane by `translateX(100%)` → 0 inside a fixed track, not by animating the grid. Read: add `.flash` to the host card on assign.
- **MOT-36 Dead keyframe references in the Team CSS (ported design-page rules)** [P2] n/a (dead or broken CSS). File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css`: - `:531` `animation: fadein` (keyframe is `ch-htb-fadein`) - `:998` `animation: popin` (`ch-htb-popin`) - `:1228` `animation: draw` (`ch-htb-draw`): `.assigned path` stays at `stroke-dashoffset: 24`, an invisible tick - `:1237` `spin`, which works only if Tailwind's global `spin` keyframe is emitted - `:1331` `storyin` (`ch-htb-storyin`) Wrong: These selectors (`.fadein`, `.wk.pop`, `.assigned`, `.spin`, `.sto-anim`) aren't rendered by any Team TSX, which is why nothing visibly breaks today. As a result M-TEAM-26 / 27 (spinner, drawn tick) aren't built. The live Assign shows "Assigning…" text only (`screens/coachhelm/parts.tsx:436`). Anyone reusing these classes gets a missing tick. Fix: Delete the five dead blocks. Build the Assign state with `ui/Spinner` and a `stroke-dashoffset` tick on `--ch-dur-marks`.
- **MOT-37 Sheet exits run 260 CH_EASE where the lanes specify 280** [P2] every `ui/Modal` / `ui/FormSheet` close, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/lib/dialog-lifetime.ts:164` (`duration: CH_DUR.base * 1000, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)'` literal) Wrong: Team (M-TEAM-20), Messages (M-MSG-16c) and Ask (M-ASK-13) all set the sheet close at 280 `--ch-ease`. The shared exit is 20ms shorter and spells the curve as a literal. Fix: `CH_DUR.release * 1000` with `bezier(CH_EASE)`.
- **MOT-38 Toast exit moves with framer `y`, on the main thread** [P2] toast dismiss, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/ui/Toast.tsx:369` (`{ opacity: 0, y: 6, scale: 0.98 }`) Wrong: `lib/motion.ts:139-141` explains that framer's `x`/`y`/`scale` run on the main thread and that a transform string goes to the compositor. The toast still uses `y`/`scale`, so a busy frame (a route landing) stalls it. Under RM it is instant (MOT-22). Fix: `transform: 'translateY(6px) scale(.98)'`, as `CH_POP.exit` does.
- **MOT-39 Overshooting springs on data marks and avatars** [P2] seen-by faces arriving, roster coin hover, Team stations. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/messages/seen-by.tsx:112` (`chSpring('pop')`, bounce .35, peak about 1.068, on a server "seen" event); `src/clubhouse/styles/roster.css:2561` (`--ch-ease-spring` peak about 1.1 on the avatar, 180ms); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:270`, `:370` (stations on `--ch-ease-spring`, 520) Wrong: A face that pops past its size when someone else reads a message is motion on data, not on the user's action. `--ch-ease-spring` (`cubic-bezier(.34,1.3,.64,1)`) overshoots well past the 0.1-bounce cap in `lib/motion.ts:58-60`. Fix: Faces from the server: fade 180 at size. Roster hover: `--ch-ease`. Stations: no entrance at all (MOT-03).
- **MOT-40 Team rows scale on press, against the house rule that rows tint** [P2] bar rows, read rows, roster rows, course rows, phone. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:76-82` (`.tapp[data-ch-pressed] { transform: scale(.97); stroke-width: 1.5 }`, back over 280 `--ch-ease`) Wrong: `lib/press.ts:9-13` ("Rows, links, tabs, switches and cards do not scale … a whole row shrinking read as rubbery", owner 2026-10-01). Team rows full-width-scale by .97, about 11px at 358 wide, which goes past the 6px key rule. The spec row M-TEAM-38 asks for it, which conflicts with the owner rule. Fix: Replace with a `--ch-ledger-row-press` tint after `--ch-dur-press-delay`. Keep `.97` only on `.btn` keys and story tiles (`data-ch-press-scale=".97"`).
- **MOT-41 Popovers with an entry but no exit (class awareness, Ask picker)** [P2] class-awareness popover, Ask composer picker, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/class-awareness.css:180-189` (`ch-ca-in` 180 on mount only); `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask.css:717-735` (`ch-ask-pick-in` / `-down` on mount only) Wrong: Both fade or pop in and then vanish in one frame on dismiss. Fix: Mount them through `ui/Menu`'s `AnimatePresence` with `CH_POP`, or keep them mounted for a 180 `CH_POP.exit`.
- **MOT-42 Read scroll shade sits at a fixed `top: 110px`** [P2] S-READ phone, under the green bar, any Text Size or safe-area inset. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-read.css:122-126` (`.shade { position: sticky; top: 110px }`) Wrong: The bar's real height is `--ch-phone-topbar-h + env(safe-area-inset-top)` and changes with the inset and the large title. A literal 110 sits the shade mid-content on one device and under the bar on another. Fix: `top: calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top))`, or use the bar's own `data-scrolled` edge (`shell/large-title.tsx:105`), which every other lane uses.
- **MOT-43 Stats team "chosen leg" plate assumes 44px rows and drifts under Text Size** [P2] S-TEAMSTATS legs, phone, at larger iPhone Text Size. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats.css:4733-4746` (`translate: 0 calc(4px + i × 44px)`, "nothing is measured") Wrong: Rows grow past 44px when Text Size raises the 15 or 17 type, or when a label wraps. The plate then slides to the wrong row, about 8-12px off per row from row 2 down. Accurate at the default size only. Fix: Slide the plate with `useSlidePlate` (`lib/slide-plate.ts`), which measures the chosen row.
- **MOT-44 Classes import scan travels a fixed 130px over a box of any height** [P2] ImportSchedule "reading" state, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/classes.css:1015-1031` (`translateY(-34px → 130px)` over `--ch-dur-shimmer`, infinite; `background: #efe6d2` above it has no dark override) Wrong: The sweep stops 130px down whatever the page height, so on a tall schedule it never reaches the bottom. It loops forever while reading, with no off-screen pause. Fix: Translate by `100cqh` of a container (`container-type: size`), or use `.ch-skel`'s sheen over the page thumbnail.
- **MOT-45 Quick chat step band keeps `will-change: transform` on for as long as a step runs** [P2] S-RUN.steps active step, phone and desktop. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:90` (`.step.active .step__l::after { … animation: ch-qc-band 1.9s infinite; will-change: transform }`) Wrong: An infinite band on the active step plus a permanent compositor layer, while the 2.4s key wheel (`:31`) turns in the bar at the same time. Neither pauses when the panel is closed or scrolled off. Two loops plus a promoted layer for the whole run cost battery on a phone. Fix: Drop `will-change` (a transform animation promotes the layer on its own). Pause both loops with `animation-play-state: paused` when the panel is hidden (`.ch-qcs:not(.is-open)`).

### Design fidelity and features: Team view, Root cause, Coach vs player, Brief (41)
- **FID-TEAM-1 No Try again anywhere on the Team view in production** [P1] every failed part (Working on, Course, Spot, players/roster), phone|desktop, light|dark. File: routes/coachhelm.tsx:85 (`<TeamBoard … />` has no `onRetry`); team/Cards.tsx WorkingCard and team/CourseCard.tsx gate the button on `onRetry`; team/TeamBoard.tsx:288-289 renders only `failedHole` when players or roster fail Wrong: design draws "Couldn't load this part." with a Try again button on each failed card and on the board error. Code never passes `onRetry`, so the button never renders, and a players/roster failure leaves the whole body as a single failed hole with no way out but a reload. Fix: pass `onRetry={() => router.refresh()}` from the route (same as `ui/RefreshNotice` usage in CoachBoard), and render `ui/States` ErrorState with Try again in the players/roster failure branch
- **FID-RC-1 Read summary card (S-READ-BOARD) only exists in the preview** [P1] coach board / Team view / player board, phone|desktop. File: read/ReadSummaryCard.tsx (used only at preview/PreviewCoachHelmRead.tsx:21) Wrong: the package specifies a read summary card on the host board that links into the read page. No production screen renders it. TeamBoard, CoachBoard and YourWeek never import it, so the read page is reachable only from the Team read sheet or a deep link. Fix: render `ReadSummaryCard` in CoachBoard's reads list and YourWeek's reads card behind `isReadEnabled()`, with `href` from the existing read href helper
- **FID-RC-2 S-READ-BOARD actions row and dismissed state not built** [P1] S-READ-BOARD.actions, S-READ-BOARD.dismissed, phone|desktop. File: read/ReadSummaryCard.tsx (no actions or dismissed branch) Wrong: design gives the card an Assign + More row and a dismissed variant with Undo. Code renders a link card only. Fix: add a footer using `ui/Button` (secondary "Assign") and a `ui/Menu` trigger; wire to `useFocusActions` assign and dismiss/undo
- **FID-RC-3 Cleared, contradicted and under-review read states missing** [P1] read page, phone|desktop. File: read/ReadPage.tsx (no lifecycle branch); read data shape has no lifecycle field Wrong: design draws three lifecycle states with their own headline chip and copy. Code has no reference to any of them; a cleared read falls through to CH-13861 "no longer open". Fix: add a `lifecycle` field to the read shape, branch in ReadPage, reuse `ui/Badge` for the chip and `ui/States` for the body
- **FID-CVP-1 S-LIST push from the board rows into the player file not built** [P1] Team view players, Coach board players, phone|desktop. File: none on team/TeamBoard.tsx or CoachBoard.tsx; only file/PlayerFile.tsx:344 rail rows carry `data-ch-hf-open` Wrong: design says every player row on the board pushes into the player file. On the Team view the file is reachable only via the PlayerPanel link "Open {first}'s page" (team/panels.tsx:240); on the Coach board not at all. Fix: wrap board player rows in a `Link` to `playerFileHref(id)` when `fileOn`, reusing the rail row markup
- **FID-TEAM-2 Switch reads "Board / Ask" once you are on Ask** [P1] Ask view entered from the Team view, phone|desktop, light|dark. Switch to Ask and the same segment relabels itself "Board". The control changes its words under the coach's finger. Fix: pass `boardLabel={isTeamBoardEnabled() ? 'Team' : 'Board'}` from the route into Ask and AskSkeleton (and CoachHelmSkeleton.tsx:71)
- **FID-TEAM-3 Desktop context line hidden; scope text and switch not in the glass bar** [P1] Team view header, desktop, light|dark. File: styles/shell.css:4387-4391 (`@media (min-width:821px){ .ch-large-title__context{display:none} }`); team/TeamBoard.tsx:369-373 Wrong: design desktop puts "Varsity · 5 players · this season" as scope text in the glass top bar with the switch at 180×34 beside it (redesign/coachhelm-team.html :1028). Code puts the context line under LargeTitle, then the global rule hides it, so desktop shows no scope at all, and the switch sits in the deskhead. Fix: scope the global hide away from `.ch-htb` (`.ch-htb .ch-large-title__context{display:block}`) or move the scope string into the shell top bar slot; owner decision on switch placement (see report)

Code shows only the long sentence. Fix: render `<b>{r.held} of {r.checked}</b>` before the sentence; the CSS is already there
- **FID-TEAM-5 Read sheet says "Assigning… / Assigned as {first}'s focus"; design says propose** [P1] Team read sheet / panel, propose action, phone|desktop. - **FID-TEAM-6 Story viewer does not pause on keyboard focus and has no focus trap** [P1] Story viewer, phone, light|dark. File: team/Stories.tsx StoryViewer (pause wired to pointer hold only) Wrong: auto-advancing content pauses only on press-and-hold. Keyboard or switch users cannot stop it, and Tab leaves the viewer while it keeps advancing. Fix: pause on `focusin`/resume on `focusout`, add a Pause button, trap focus as `ui/Modal` does

### Design fidelity and features: Classes, Roster, Home, Stats (17)
- **FID-STATS-1 Every Stats card is radius 16 with 12pt top padding and an 8pt head gap; design is 18 / 14 / 10** [P1] My stats and Team stats, every summary and detail card, phone, light and dark. File: `styles/stats-redesign.css:136-145` (`.ch-shs-card`), `:146` (`.ch-shs-card.is-flushb`), `:152-159` (`.ch-shs-ah`) Wrong: `.ch-shs-card` is `border-radius: var(--ch-radius-lg)` (16px) and `padding: 12px 16px 16px`. Design is radius 18 and padding 14 16 16. `.is-flushb` is `12px 0 4px`; design `.card.air.flushb` is `14px 0 4px`. `.ch-shs-ah` is `gap: 4px 8px; margin-bottom: 8px`; design is `gap: 4px 10px; margin-bottom: 10px`. Each card is 2px tighter at the top, the title sits 2px closer to the body and the corners are 2px tighter, on every card on both pages. Fix: Add `--ch-radius-card-lg: 18px` next to `--ch-radius-card` in `styles/tokens.css:531`, or set 18px in the lane's scope. Then use `padding: 14px 16px 16px`, `.is-flushb { padding: 14px 0 4px }`, and `.ch-shs-ah { gap: 4px 10px; margin-bottom: 10px }`. Leave the skeleton card head at 12 (`stats-phone.css:629`).
- **FID-STATS-2 "What changed" sentence has wrong grammar and raw metric labels** [P1] My stats, What changed card and Scoring detail "against" section, phone, light and dark, player and coach. File: `screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:438-447` (change sentence builder); subjects from `lib/data/stats-figures.ts:166-170` Wrong: The verb is chosen by `l.subject.startsWith('Putts') || l.subject.endsWith('s') ? 'are' : 'is'`, and the subject is the raw figure label. The subjects are 'Scoring avg', 'Greens in regulation', 'Fairways hit' and 'Putts per round', so the card prints "Greens in regulation is up 5.6 pts", "Fairways hit is down ..." and "Scoring avg is down 1.2", with the clipped label "avg" in a sentence. The tail is cut from the first line's words with `/than (.*)$/`, which gives ", against previous 10." with no article. The code comment at :438 also shows "the previous 10". The design writes full sentences: "Your scoring average is X lower than the N rounds before. Greens are up ...". Fix: Give each figure a sentence noun and number in `stats-figures.ts` (for example `{ noun: 'greens in regulation', plural: true }`, `{ noun: 'your scoring average', plural: false }`) and choose the verb from that. Write the tail from the window count ("than the 10 rounds before") instead of a regex over another line's words.
- **FID-STATS-3 Goals detail has no hero, and the coach empty state points to a section that isn't there** [P1] My stats > Goals and focus areas detail (`#sd-goals`), phone, light and dark, player and coach. File: `screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:1099-1162` (`GoalsDetail`), `:1106` (meta), `:1134` (Focus areas meta), `:1136` (coach empty body) Wrong: The design opens the detail with `dHero(m, 'Scoring average · goal is under 75 this season', <pill On track | X to go>)`. The code goes straight to the "Goals this season" card with no `DetailHero`, unlike every other Stats detail. The Goals card meta is `plural(n, 'goal')` and the Focus areas meta is "N active · N waiting on ...". The design has no meta on Goals and "From your coach" on Focus areas. The coach's empty Focus areas body says "Add one from a weak section above.", but nothing is above it on this page: the detail holds only the two cards. Fix: Render `<DetailHero figure={...} caption={`${goal metric} · goal is ${target}`} chips={<ShsPill tone={onTrack ? 'gain' : 'flat'}>...</ShsPill>} />` from the first active goal (the same component as the SG detail, `:937`). Use meta "From your coach" on Focus areas for the player. Change the coach empty copy so it points to the summary's sections ("Add one from a leg on the Stats page."), or add the focus action here.
- **FID-STATS-4 Try again shows no skeleton and has no busy state you can see; it stays dead if the refresh brings back the same data (extends STATS-7)** [P1] My stats failed (CH-5201) and Team stats failed (CH-4201), phone, light and dark. File: `screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:324-354` (`FailedCard`); `screens/stats/StatsTeamPhoneShs.tsx:239-266` (`TeamFailedCard`); `screens/stats/StatsPlayer.tsx:234,255` (`onRetry={() => router.refresh()}`) Wrong: A tap sets `busy` and calls `router.refresh()`. The failed card stays on screen, and the only change is `aria-disabled`, which has no visual. No skeleton is drawn. `busy` is cleared only by `useEffect(() => setBusy(false), [seen])`, so if the refresh returns props that are referentially equal, or the RSC request fails, the button stays disabled with no feedback. The criterion says Try again shows the skeleton, then the page lands with no animation. Fix: Wrap the refresh in `useTransition` (`startTransition(() => router.refresh())`). While `isPending`, render `ShsSkeleton` (it already exists for loading) in place of the sheet. Clear busy when the transition ends, not when the data identity changes. Use the ui `Button` `loading` state per STATS-7.
- **FID-STATS-5 Back from a round opened in Stats goes to the Rounds list (player) or team Stats (coach)** [P1] My stats > Rounds detail, SG detail round trend > a round > Back, phone, player and coach. File: `shell/nav.ts:364-365` (round page `top`); `shell/phone-chrome.tsx:401-405` (`parent ? router.push(parent)`); round links `screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:1032` (`rebuiltHref('/golf/dashboard/rounds/<id>')`) Wrong: The round page's top bar has a fixed parent. For a player it reads "‹ Rounds" and pushes `/golf/dashboard/rounds`. For a coach it reads "‹ Stats" and pushes `/golf/dashboard/stats` without `?player=` or the `#sd-rounds` hash. A player who opens a round from Stats lands on a different tab's list. A coach who opens one from a player's Stats lands on team Stats and loses the player, the window and the scroll position. Fix: When the round is opened from Stats, pass a `from` param (or use `history.back()` when `document.referrer` / the client history entry is the Stats URL). Have `nav.ts` read it: back label "My stats" or "Player stats", parent = the full Stats URL with `?player=`, the window and the `#sd-*` hash.
- **FID-STATS-6 Section names are weight 600, wdth 96 and 28pt below the card above; design is 700, wdth 92 and 30pt** [P2] My stats and Team stats section names (Overview, Your game, Rounds, Development; Team game, Players), phone, light and dark. File: `styles/stats-redesign.css:125-131` (`.ch-shs-sech`) Wrong: `font: var(--ch-type-22-strong)` is 600 22/28. Design is 700 22/28. `font-variation-settings: 'wdth' 96`; design is wdth 92. `margin: 16px 2px 0`; design is `18px 2px 0`, so the name sits 28pt below the card above instead of 30. Fix: `font: 700 calc(22 * var(--ch-type-k))/calc(28 * var(--ch-type-k)) var(--ch-font-sans); font-variation-settings: 'wdth' 92; margin: 18px 2px 0;`. 04:139 proposed a new token. The `--ch-type-section` that exists (`tokens.css:358`) is 600 20/25, which is another value, so add `--ch-type-section-lg` instead of reusing it.
- **FID-STATS-7 All condensed figures use wdth 96; the spec sets wdth 92 everywhere** [P2] Stats feature figure, summary and cell figures, Home leg figures and greeting, phone, light and dark. File: `styles/stats-redesign.css` (8 rules with `'wdth' 96`, for example `:128`, `:2036`, `:2131-2134` `.ch-shs-leg__v`; 0 rules with 92) Wrong: Every condensed setting in the lane is `'wdth' 96`, which matches the house `--ch-wdth-display` (`tokens.css:348`). Every design row (hero 40, summary 34, tile 28, cell 24, section 22, greeting 22) specifies wdth 92, so figures render visibly wider than the design. Wide figures are what push "−5.7"-style values toward the chevron at large Text Size. Fix: Add `--ch-wdth-figure: 'wdth' 92` to `tokens.css` and use it in those 8 rules. If wdth 96 was a deliberate house choice, record it in DECISIONS and update 06.
- **FID-STATS-8 Window switch bar, filter button and sheet bottom are each off by 2 to 8px** [P2] My stats and Team stats, sticky switch and page bottom, phone, light and dark. File: `styles/stats-redesign.css:41-47` (`.ch-shs .ch-shs-ctl`), `:90-102` (`.ch-shs-ctl .ch-sf__open`), `:119-123` (`.ch-shs-sheet`) Wrong: The switch bar padding is `2px edge 8px`; design is `2px 16px 10px`. The filter button is `border-radius: var(--ch-radius-md)` (12px) on a flat `--ch-well-bg` with no shadow; design is radius 14 on the segment well `--seg` with `--seg-sh`, matching the segmented control beside it. The sheet's bottom padding is 48px; design is 56. Fix: `.ch-shs-ctl { padding: 2px var(--ch-page-x-phone) 10px }`; `.ch-sf__open { border-radius: 14px; background: <the WindowSwitch track background>; box-shadow: <its inset shadow> }` (reuse the WindowSwitch track's tokens so the two read as one control); `.ch-shs-sheet { padding-bottom: 56px }`.
- **FID-STATS-9 First run (no rounds) still shows the window switch and filter** [P2] My stats with no rounds (first-run card), phone, player and coach. File: `screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:223-229` (switch rendered for every state), `:236` (`FirstRunCard`) Wrong: The design's `S.data==='none'` branch draws the bar, then the sheet with the empty card and Development. It has no `winCtl`. The code renders `.ch-shs-ctl` unconditionally, so a player with no rounds sees "Last 10 / Season / ..." segments and a filter that change nothing. Fix: Wrap the `.ch-shs-ctl` block in `{!firstRun && (...)}`. Keep it for `noRounds` under a filter, where switching the window can bring rounds back.
- **FID-STATS-10 Scoring chart legend reads "Par" without the par value** [P2] My stats Scoring feature card legend, phone, light and dark. File: `screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:428-430` (legend `<i className="lp" />Par`) Wrong: The legend says "Par". The design says "Par ${P}" ("Par 72"), so the dashed line is labelled with its value. Fix: `Par {par}` when `par != null` (the same `par` already passed to `Ribbon` at `:823`). Hide the legend item when par is null.
- **FID-STATS-11 Coach player Stats has no "Player stats ⌄" switcher, and Team always goes back to team Stats** [P2] Stats `?player=`, coach, phone. File: `screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:201` (`PhoneTop title="Player stats" back={{ label: 'Team', ... }}`); `screens/stats/StatsPlayer.tsx:232,253` (`onBackToTeam={() => go(null, data.filter)}`) Wrong: The bar is a plain title with a "‹ Team" back. The criterion says the bar reads "Player stats ⌄", a switcher to move between players. `onBackToTeam` always pushes team Stats, so a coach who came from Roster's profile, a Home "team's game" name or an Ask link is sent to team Stats, not back where they came from. Fix: Give `PhoneTop` a title menu (the same pattern as the PhoneTop switcher used where `start` is set) listing the roster's active players. Make Back use `router.back()` when the previous entry is in the app, and fall back to team Stats only on a cold load.
- **FID-STATS-12 Team "The round" meta and Team scoring hero caption drop the comparison and window words** [P2] Team stats summary (The round) and Team scoring detail (`#sd-team-score`), coach, phone. File: `screens/stats/StatsTeamPhoneShs.tsx:340` (`meta={windowDays(data)}`), `:371` (`caption={['Team scoring average', toPar(data)]...}`) Wrong: The round meta is always the window ("Last 10 days"). The design shows "vs the N before" when there is a previous window, and the window only when there isn't. The hero caption reads "Team scoring average · +2.1 to par". The design adds the window: "Team scoring average · +2.1 to par · Last 10 round days". Fix: `meta={data.prev ? `vs the ${data.prevDays} before` : windowDays(data)}` (use the previous-window count the delta chip already reads). Append `windowDays(data)` to the caption array.
- **FID-STATS-13 Team stats reads Text Size once and never updates** [P2] Team stats, big-text layout, coach, phone. File: `screens/stats/StatsTeamPhoneShs.tsx:44-46` (`bigText()`), `:124` (`useEffect(() => setBig(bigText()), [])`) Wrong: The player page uses `useBigText()` with a `MutationObserver` (`StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:89-99`), so changing Text Size while the page is open moves it to the big layout. The team page reads `--ch-type-scale` once on mount, so it stays in the wrong layout until it is reloaded. Fix: Export `useBigText` from `StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx` (or move it to `ui/`) and use it in `StatsTeamPhoneShs.tsx` in place of `bigText()` + `useEffect`.
- **FID-STATS-14 Your game card paddings are 2px short in four places** [P2] Player Home > Your game, phone, light and dark. File: `styles/stats-redesign.css:2049-2056` (`.ch-shs-yg__h`), `:2087-2097` (`.ch-shs-yg__none`), `:2098` (`.ch-shs-leg`), `:2222-2226` (`.ch-shs-leg__n`), `:2227-2232` (`.ch-shs-yg__foot`) Wrong: The header is `12px 16px 12px`; design card head is 14 at top. The leg is `12px 16px 16px`; design `.cb` is `14px 16px 16px`. The leg note margin-top is 8px; design `.leg .cnt` is 10px. The footnote is `12px 16px 12px`; design `.cn` is `12px 16px 14px`. The empty body is `12px 16px 16px` with a 4px gap and the body at line-height 20. The design is `.cb` 14 16 16 plus `.cb-empty` 6 0 4, and the body at 15/21. Fix: Set these values: head padding-top 14, leg padding `14px 16px 16px`, note margin-top 10, foot padding-bottom 14, empty body `padding: 14px 16px 16px` with an inner `padding: 6px 0 4px`, and span `line-height: calc(21 * var(--ch-type-k))`.
- **FID-STATS-15 Your game: few-rounds note lacks its lead, failed hides the empty body, and the pill adds "SG"** [P2] Player Home > Your game in the early, no-rounds + cache-failed and normal states, phone. File: `screens/home/PlayerGame.tsx:461-466` (footnote), `:473-486` (`cacheError` with `!any`), `:508` (`{formatSigned(g.sg)} SG`) Wrong: The early-read footnote starts "Each figure shows with an early-read tag ...". The design's `few` note starts "From 2 rounds." (`From N rounds.`), like the Stats early note. With no rounds and a cache error, the code shows the CH-2216 notice and returns `null` for the body. The criterion says the empty body wins. The leg pill reads "−0.9 SG". The design pill is the figure alone, and the meaning is in the header ("Strokes gained vs Tour") and the aria-label, which `:504` already carries. Fix: Prefix the early footnote with `From ${plural(rounds, 'round')}.` in bold. When `!any`, always render the CH-2311 body and drop the notice. Render `<ShsPill tone={tone}>{formatSigned(g.sg)}</ShsPill>`.
- **FID-STATS-16 Player Home green top has no avatar or next-event line, and Your game sits far below it (extends CRH-19)** [P2] Player Home, top and first sections, phone, light and dark. File: `screens/home/PlayerHomePhone.tsx:52` and the section order after it (Up next, actions, asks, live, since, week, today and latest round before `YourGameCard`); `styles/stats-redesign.css:2012-2039` (`.ch-shs-hero-top`) Wrong: The design's `.who` row is a 40pt avatar, the greeting "Good afternoon, Jonah" at 600 22/1.2, −0.015em, wdth 92, and the next-event line "Practice at 3:30 · Lonnie Poole range". The code draws a date line and the greeting at `--ch-type-22-strong` (22/28), wdth 96, −0.01em, with no avatar and no event line. 05 places S-HOMEGAME "under the greeting". The code renders it after up to eight other sections, so on a busy day it is off the first screen. Fix: Render `<Avatar size="md">` and a second line from the next event `PlayerHomePhone` already loads for Up next (`{title} at {time} · {place}`). Use `line-height: 1.2; letter-spacing: -0.015em; wdth 92`. Move `<YourGameCard>` directly under the hero, or record the order in DECISIONS if Up next is meant to lead.
- **FID-CRH-1 Player Home's bar only changes when the Stats flag is also on (extends CRH-19)** [P2] Player Home, phone top bar, light and dark, crh on with stats-redesign off. File: `styles/shell.css:4396` (`.ch-root[data-ch-crh='on'][data-phone-hero]:has(.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'], [data-ch-hero-flat]) .ch-topbar`); `screens/home/PlayerHomePhone.tsx:52` (`data-ch-hero-flat={shs ? '' : undefined}`) Wrong: The `--ch-frame` bar applies when a `.ch-hm` has `data-ch-crh='on'` (coach Home only) or when `[data-ch-hero-flat]` is present. Player Home sets the latter only when `shs` is true. With the lane's flag on and the Stats flag off, the player's top bar keeps the old radial hero while the coach's and every other crh tab's bar is flat. The lane README says the player Home's bar changes with the lane. Fix: Set `data-ch-crh={crh ? 'on' : undefined}` on the player's `main.ch-hm` (pass `crh` the way `HomePhone` gets it), so the `:has(.ch-hm[data-ch-crh='on'])` branch matches. Keep `data-ch-hero-flat` for the green hero under Stats.

### Design fidelity and features: Ask, Quick chat (44)
- **FID-ASK-1 Every Ask create flow is a generic facts card: no draft visual, no Worked out pills, no date tile** [P0] Ask confirm card, every flow (event, travel, qualifier, lineup, goal, note, block, break, term, availability, recruit), phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx (the whole body renders `dl` facts; `.inf` only for `f.source`) Wrong: The design draws a bespoke visual for each flow: - E-WK week grid with the new day ticked (event) - E-TRAVEL route and room pairs - E-QUAL rounds and seats - E-LINEUP seats, E-LOCKP lock screen - E-GOAL stat target, E-NOTE note card, E-TASK row - E-EVD draft well on a 48px date tile and 1fr text grid - E-INF a "Worked out" pill row for every Read-filled value The code renders none of them. Every flow is a list of labels and values, and a value shows its source only through `.inf` on `f.source`. `preview/fixtures-ask-flows.ts` shows the same generic card, so the preview hides the gap. Fix: Add one visual slot per `proposal.visual.kind`. The server already sends `visual` for cal, qual and msg (v3chat/create-tools.ts:120, :164, :363); add kinds for the others. Reuse the Quick chat visuals (src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/visuals/visuals.tsx EventVisual, QualifierVisual) where the drawing is the same, and render Worked out pills from `facts[].source === 'read'` with the existing `.inf` pill.
- **FID-ASK-2 Announcement, poll, task and drill flows promised for Ask are not built; board is text only** [P0] Ask create flows f_annc, f_poll, f_task, f_drill, f_board, both, both themes. File: src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/create-tools.ts:386-387 (CREATE_FLOWS, LINEUP_FLOWS); src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/ask-draft-tools.ts:27, :53-62 Wrong:  - What exists: CREATE_FLOWS = message, event, trip, qualifier, goal, note, block, break, term, avail, recruit. - Announcement: no v2 flow; only the legacy `create_team_announcement`, which has no hold card. - Poll: none. ask-draft-tools.ts:27 notes it waits on held 20261028140800. - Task: the legacy `create_task` only. - Drill: nothing at all, and no fallback copy. Grep for "Drill bank comes" finds nothing in src. The README says the drill flow "is built but stays off"; it is not. - Board: `draft_board` sends a text-note handoff; there's no PNG in the composer. - The preview fixtures render all of these flows (fixtures-ask-flows.ts f_annc, f_poll, f_drill), so the preview claims a feature the server can't produce. Fix:  - Add `announcementFlow` and `taskFlow` as FlowDefs calling `createEnrichedAnnouncement` and `createTask`, behind `proposeGated`. - Add the drill flow's §5.14 fallback answer, "Drill bank comes with the practice builder", plus an offer to add a plan block. - Keep poll off until 140800 is applied, and mark f_poll and f_drill in the fixtures as "not live". - Board: render it to a PNG `PendingAttachment` (rounds/card/draw-card.ts:313 pattern).
- **FID-ASK-3 No server writes `data-artifact`, so artifact cards, pane, versions and why popover never appear** [P0] Ask artifact card and pane (player report, lineup, round recap), both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/artifacts.ts:22 (reads `data-artifact`); src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/agent-tools.ts:721 (writes only `data-visual-artifact`); src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/restore.ts:35 (restore list has no `data-artifact`) Wrong: The client groups `data-artifact` parts into versioned artifacts, but nothing on the server emits that part type, and restore wouldn't keep it if anything did. ASK-1 covers the JSON pane body. This goes further: the whole card, pane, push, versions and why-popover family (E-ACARD, E-APANEL, ask/05 artifact.*) can't be reached in any session. It works only from fixtures. Fix: Add an `artifact` tool (player_report, lineup, round_recap) that writes `{type:'data-artifact', id, data:{artifactId, version, kind, ...}}`, add `'data-artifact'` to restore.ts:35, and render the body with the existing visual blocks instead of the JSON pre at chat/Ask.tsx:400.
- **FID-ASK-4 Artifact pane footer has only "Edit in builder"; Save draft and Publish are missing** [P1] Artifact pane footer, desktop, and artifact card actions, phone. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Artifact.tsx:203-213 (Actions) Wrong: The design footer is Edit in builder, Save draft… and Publish… (coachhelm-ask.html pane footer). The two sheets behind them have 9 save states (E-SAVE, E-FPICK) and 6 publish states. The code has only Edit in builder, so no save or publish states exist for artifacts. Fix: Add the Save draft… and Publish… `Button`s. Save opens a folder picker on `saveTextDocument` with `playerVisible:false` (TASK_FIELDS §5.15). Publish goes through a FlowDef on the hold, like publishFlow (agent-tools.ts:740).
- **FID-ASK-5 The Ask tab's message flow sends as the coach instead of handing off to the composer** [P1] Ask message flow, both. File: src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/create-tools.ts:355-375 (messageFlow); src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/agent-tools.ts:731 (registered for every askV2 chat, Ask tab included) Wrong: messageFlow calls `sendGolfMessage` after the hold, and it is registered for the Ask tab as well as Quick chat. TASK_FIELDS §3 (last paragraph) and §5.4 say "the Ask tab's message drafts land in the Messages composer and the coach presses Send there". Only Quick chat's card is the coach's Send. In the Ask tab, CoachHelm can now send a message the coach never typed into Messages. Fix: Pass the surface into `agentTools` (the Quick chat session already has its own hook instance). Register messageFlow only for Quick chat. In the Ask tab, register a `draft_message` handoff that builds `messagesPrefillHref` in the browser, like `draft_board` (ask-draft-tools.ts:53-62).
- **FID-ASK-6 Player Ask can't be reached from the player's navigation** [P1] Player CoachHelm tabs, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/coachhelm-views-shape.ts:16 (PLAYER_HELM_HREF); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/views/PlayerHelmTabs.tsx Wrong: Player tabs list only board, standing and deep-dive. `view=ask` for players is served (routes/coachhelm.tsx:327), but only by typing the URL. On that page Ask.tsx:343 renders tabs only when `!player`, so a player who lands there has no way back except the browser. Fix: Add `ask` to PLAYER_HELM_HREF when `isPlayerAskEnabled()`, and render PlayerHelmTabs on the player Ask page.
- **FID-ASK-7 "Ask about this" opens a blank Ask: the `q` it sends is never read** [P1] Team panels and Root cause, "Ask about this" into Ask, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/ask-links.ts (askAboutHref, askAboutReadHref build `&q=`); src/clubhouse/routes/coachhelm.tsx:240 (params have no `q`); src/app/golf/(dashboard)/dashboard/coachhelm/page.tsx:199 Wrong: Callers at team/panels.tsx:166 and read/ReadPage.tsx:125 pass the question in `q`. The Clubhouse route never reads `q`; only the Fairway AskSurface.tsx:140 does. The coach taps "Ask about this" and gets an empty composer with no question and no context chip. Fix: Read `q` in page.tsx and routes/coachhelm.tsx and pass it to `AskComposer` as the seeded text (the `fresh` and `draftKey` path at Composer.tsx:165). Keep `insight` as the context chip.
- **FID-ASK-8 The event landing surface says "Wednesday" for every event** [P1] Confirm card landing surface, create_event, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:40 (`create_event: { app: 'Calendar', where: 'Wednesday' }`), rendered at :139-143 Wrong: The "where it lands" line is a hard-coded constant, not the event's date. A Friday event says "Calendar · Wednesday". Fix: Build `where` from `proposal.visual.date` (create-tools.ts:120 already sends it), formatted in the team zone, for example "Fri, Nov 14". Do the same for the other LANDS entries.
- **FID-ASK-9 The landing surface draws only the new row, with no real neighbouring rows** [P1] Confirm card E-HS, every flow, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:139-160 Wrong: E-HS shows the new row slotting in between the real rows already in that tab (that day's events, that player's goals). The code draws the one new row alone, so there's nothing to slot in between. The Quick chat calendar visual does draw `.blk.old` neighbours (qc.css:139); Ask doesn't. Fix: Have each FlowDef's `plan()` return up to 2 neighbouring rows from its own read (the event flow already reads the day). Render them as the Quick chat `.blk.old` rows.
- **FID-ASK-10 Ask's held receipt has no Open, and its failed receipt has no Try again** [P1] Receipt held and failed, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:249-268 (held: Undo only), :271-281 (failed: text only) Wrong: The design held receipt is Open plus Undo, and Open commits early through POST `?now=1` (TASK_FIELDS §3.4). The Quick chat TaskCard has it (TaskCard.tsx:204-227); Ask doesn't. The failed receipt has no retry, so the coach has to retype the request. QC-3 covers this for Quick chat only. Fix: Reuse TaskCard's Open handler (POST `/api/coachhelm/v3/chat/actions/[key]?now=1`, then navigate to `receipt.created[0].href`). Add a `Button variant="secondary"` "Try again" that resends the proposal.
- **FID-ASK-11 Ask's Told row is a text line, with no coins or seen dots** [P1] Receipt told, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:302-313 Wrong: The design (E-TOLD) shows a row of 28px avatar coins in delivery order with seen dots and "+N". The code renders "Told N by push" as text. Fix: Render coins from `receipt.notifications` recipients using `Avatar size="xs"` stacked at -6px (Quick chat's `.coin` pattern, qc.css:117-118), plus the `.coin.more` overflow.
- **FID-ASK-12 Player out state: the composer is removed, and the card has the wrong copy and parts** [P1] Player Ask, out of allowance, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Ask.tsx:379-391; src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Allowance.tsx:21-33 Wrong:  - Design (coachhelm-ask.html:2122, E-OUT): the composer stays visible but paused, with the placeholder "Ask is paused until Monday". The out card sits in the thread and holds: - title "You've used this week's Ask" - a reason paragraph - a meter row "Resets Mon" - a keep row "Your past answers and charts still open…" - "Request more", which pops in about 900ms later - Code: PlayerOut replaces the composer dock entirely. Its title is "Ask is paused until Monday" and its second line is "Need more before Monday?". It has no meter row, no keep row and no delayed reveal. Fix: Keep `AskComposer` mounted with `blocked` and that placeholder. Move OutCard into the thread with the design's copy and the meter, keep and request rows, and reveal Request more after `--ch-dur-*` 900ms (or at once under Reduce Motion).
- **FID-ASK-13 The moment chip flips to "Added" only after the 8s hold, not at the receipt** [P1] Confirm card header chip, moments cal, fold, focus and pub, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:107 Wrong: The chip shows "Added" only when `completed`, after the hold ends. The design flips it the moment the receipt appears, while the ring drains. For 8 seconds the coach sees a receipt under a chip that still reads as a proposal. Fix: Flip the chip on `held || completed`, and return it on undone.
- **FID-ASK-14 Player "Request more" sends at once, with no confirm, no ring and no told row** [P1] Player request more, both.
