File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Allowance.tsx:36-66; src/app/golf/actions/coachhelm-ask-credits.ts:36 Wrong:  - Design: a confirm card (To your coach, From you, an optional note, an "On confirm" line, then Send request), followed by a receipt with the draining ring, then told, undone and cancelled states. - Code: the tap inserts `ask_credit_requests` straight away with an 8s release (credits.ts:36). The UI shows "Request ready" with a plain Undo `Button` and no ring, and has no told or cancelled states. Fix: Render the request as a ConfirmCard proposal (same E-CONF and E-RCPT parts) whose confirm calls the existing action. Use the shared receipt ring.
- **FID-ASK-15 The allowance meter stays green when low** [P1] Player allowance low, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:220 (`.ch-ak-meter i.is-low` changes only width), :213 (fill `--ch-green-600`) Wrong: The design's low fill is amber `--ch-chart-loss-fill`; the code's stays green-600. `.ch-ak-meter.is-low` (:226) recolours only the text to `--ch-loss`, so the text warns while the bar says fine. Fix: `.ch-ak-meter i { background: var(--ch-chart-gain-fill) }` and `.ch-ak-meter i.is-low { background: var(--ch-chart-loss-fill) }`.
- **FID-ASK-16 Miss pattern, putt profile and season compare are missing half their drawing** [P1] Answer visuals, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/visual/blocks.tsx (MissPattern, PuttProfile, SeasonCompare) Wrong:  - MissPattern draws direction wedges only. ASK_REDESIGN §3.4 adds dots by distance left. - PuttProfile has no season tick on each make-rate bar. - SeasonCompare renders two number buttons. The design draws this season's line against last season's band. - In each case the chart drops the comparison the answer depends on. Fix: Add the dot layer (r 3, `--ch-chart-*` series token) to MissPattern, a 2px tick at the season value to PuttProfile, and a line plus band SVG for SeasonCompare. Reuse the Stats sparkline band pattern, not new primitives.
- **FID-QC-1 Every Quick chat task except event, message, qualifier and plan renders as an unfinished generic card** [P1] Quick chat task card for trip, goal, note, block, break, term, availability, recruit, lineup and lock, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:27-31 (WORDS has only cal, msg, qual), :64 (generic fallback icon and "CoachHelm"), :165-174 (`dl.facts`) Wrong: Quick chat uses the same chat hook and tool set as Ask (QuickChatSession.tsx:42, `useCoachHelmChat`), so every CREATE_FLOW can start from it. Only cal, msg, qual and plan have a visual and copy. Every other flow shows a check-mark glyph, the sub "CoachHelm", the chip "Done", the busy text "Working…" and a bare facts list, and its told line reads "N players told." (:58). The design has none of these, and they look unfinished. Fix: Either limit Quick chat's tools to the four designed tasks and hand the rest to Ask with "Open in Ask", or design and add WORDS entries and visuals for each flow, shared with FID-ASK-1. Get an owner call on which.
- **FID-QC-2 A partial receipt shows as a full success** [P1] Receipt partial, both. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:40-47 (phaseOf) Wrong: `phaseOf` maps `partial` to `done`. When some recipients failed, the card shows the green check and "Added". 05 S-RCPT draws an amber partial receipt that names who wasn't reached. Fix: Add a `partial` phase with the `.rcpt__g` amber variant (`--ch-qc-a-loss-tint` family, amber token) and the line built from failed `notifications`.
- **FID-QC-3 The Told row is text only, though the coin CSS is there** [P1] Receipt told, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:245-249; src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:117-122, :233-234 Wrong: The design lights up avatar coins in delivery order, plus "+N". The code comment at TaskCard says "Can't match as drawn" and renders the text line only, while `.told .coins .coin` and `.coin.more` styles exist unused. Fix: Render up to 4 `.coin` elements from recipients (class year colour classes jr, so, fr already exist), then `.coin.more` "+N". Light them in order with the motion lane's stagger.
- **FID-QC-4 Done steps keep their present-tense label and drop the fact** [P1] Run steps, done, both. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:385-395 Wrong: A done row renders `s.label` ("Checking Wednesday"). The design's done row is past tense plus one fact ("Checked Wednesday · free after 3"). Ask's Steps.tsx uses `s.done ?? s.label`; Quick chat doesn't. Fix: Render `s.done ?? s.label`, then `s.detail` in `--ch-qc-a-ink-2` after a middle dot.
- **FID-QC-5 The composer has no + or mic button, though both are styled** [P1] Panel composer, both. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:303-328; src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:317-319, :333-334 Wrong: 06 S-PANEL.composer is + (36), field, mic (36), send (36). The code renders the field and send only. `.cmp__plus` and `.cmp__mic` have size, hit-area and icon rules but no elements. Fix: Add `.cmp__plus` (opening the same add menu as Ask's Composer.tsx:368) and `.cmp__mic` (hidden where `webkitSpeechRecognition` is absent). Or, if mic is out of scope, take it out of the spec and the CSS.
- **FID-QC-6 The qualifier told line is hard-coded, and its deadline is always empty** [P1] Task qualifier, receipt and visual, both. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:56 (`got a push and an email.`); src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/create-tools.ts:164 (`deadline: null`) Wrong: The told line claims push and email whatever channels actually went out. TASK_FIELDS §2.5 says the receipt claims only what was read back. The qualifier visual's deadline field is always null, so the drawn deadline row in S-TASK.qual never shows. Fix: Build the line from `notifications[].channel` for every kind. Pass the qualifier's registration deadline from the input or read into `visual.deadline`.
- **FID-ASK-17 Read chip popover has no "Open in Stats" link** [P2] Citation popover, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ReadChip.tsx (no link in the popover) Wrong: E-CITE requires each read's popover to end with "Open in Stats", deep-linking to the stat the number came from. The popover shows the read only. Fix: Add a `Button variant="link"` "Open in Stats" pointing to the Stats route for the read's metric and player.
- **FID-ASK-18 Brief card: no header icon tile, Skip and Next footer drifts, and folded "Change answers" never resends** [P2] Practice plan brief, every question and folded, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/BriefCard.tsx Wrong:  - The design header has a 30px icon tile; the code has none. - The design footer is Back, Skip and Next on every question. The code has "Skip, just build" on Q1 and Back on later questions. - Folded "Change answers" reopens the card, but changed answers aren't sent back, so the plan keeps the old answers. Fix: Add the tile (the confirm card's 30px tile class), use the three-button footer, and on re-fold send the changed answers as a new user turn.
- **FID-ASK-19 The v2 thread still shows the old "Reading your program" placeholder before the first step** [P2] Answer, awaiting, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Thread.tsx:299-309 Wrong: The v2 path renders the legacy CoachMark plus "Reading your program" block. The design's first frame is the E-STEPS live row. Fix: When `v2`, render `Steps` with a single live placeholder step instead of the legacy awaiting block.
- **FID-ASK-20 The tool card skeleton swaps for the chart with no crossfade** [P2] Tool card, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ToolCard.tsx Wrong: ToolCard renders a skeleton only. When data arrives, a different component mounts in its place, so the change is a hard swap. E-SKEL specifies a crossfade at the same height. Timing is the motion lane's call; this is the missing shared container. Fix: Keep one container at a fixed height and crossfade the skeleton into the chart with `--ch-dur-*` (CH_FADE_MS under Reduce Motion).
- **FID-ASK-21 The confirm header falls back to raw action text for goal, note, lineup and task** [P2] Confirm card header, those flows, both. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx (HEAD map) Wrong: HEAD has no entries for goal, note, lineup, lock or task. Those cards show the server's `action` string as the title, not the designed "New goal for Ava" style header with an icon tile. Fix: Add HEAD entries (icon, sub, title template) for each FlowDef name in CREATE_FLOWS and LINEUP_FLOWS.
- **FID-ASK-22 Answer card and steps spacing is off by 2px throughout** [P2] Answer card, steps, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:15, :18, :26, :32 Wrong:  - Turn gap: 20 (design E-THREAD 22). - `.msg-a` padding 16 16 12 (design 16 18 14); radius xl 20 (22); gap 12 (14). - Step gap: 8 (10). - Step dot: `--ch-green-600` (design accent). Fix: Use the 06 values exactly, as spacing tokens or literals in this file. Set the dot to `--ch-accent`.
- **FID-ASK-23 Confirm card geometry drifts on nine properties** [P2] Confirm card, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:86, :87, :91, :93, :98, :102, :106, :119 Wrong:  - Header padding 12 16 8, gap 8 (design 14 16 10, gap 10). - Icon tile radius 8 (9). - Draft well margin 12 16 6 (14 16 6). - Fields gap 4 (5). - Button row padding 12 16 12 (12 16 14). - Capsule padding 8 16 (9 18). - Receipt gap 8 (10). - Told row margin 0 16 12 (0 16 14); padding 8 12 (10 12); radius 12 (14). Fix: Set each to the 06 value.
- **FID-ASK-24 Citation popover, sources and follow-up chip spacing drifts** [P2] Read chip popover, sources, chips, user bubble, skeleton, both. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:44, :59, :60, :65, :69, :71 Wrong:  - Popover padding 12 12, radius 12 (design 12 14, radius 14). - Sources list padding 4 12, radius 12 (4 14, 12). - Source row padding 8 0, gap 8 (10 0, 10). - Chip padding 8 12 (8 14). - Skeleton gap 8 (9). - User bubble padding 8 16 (9 15). Fix: Set each to the 06 value.
- **FID-ASK-25 Artifact card, pane and brief spacing drifts** [P2] Artifact card and pane, brief card, both. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:126, :133, :135, :139, :140, :175, :182, :185, :194 Wrong:  - Artifact card padding 12 12 12 12 (design 12 12 12 14). - Open padding 6 8 (6 10). - Pane header 12 16 8 (12 12 12 18). - Pane body 16 (14 18 18). - Pane footer 12 16 (12 18). - Brief header padding 12 16 12, gap 8 (13 16 11, gap 10). - Question h5 17 (18). - Option radius 12, padding 12 12 (radius 14, padding 12 13). - Summary pills 4 12 (5 11). Fix: Set each to the 06 value.
- **FID-ASK-26 Dark accent, card and bubble tokens don't match the Ask palette** [P2] Every Ask surface, dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/tokens.css:1083 (`--ch-accent` #23704a), :1082 (`--ch-text-on-accent` #f4eedd), :1022 (`--ch-green-600` #6bbb8c), :918 (`--ch-bubble-theirs-bg` #232925), :919 (`--ch-bubble-mine-fill` #1f6b45); light :181 (`--ch-text-on-accent` #ffffff) Wrong:  - Dark accent: design #7CC79B with #0E120F ink; code #23704a with #f4eedd ink. - Dark accent text: design #7CC79B; code #6bbb8c. - Dark answer card: design #191E1A; code #232925. - Dark user bubble: design gradient #1F4D35 to #173D2A; code flat #1f6b45. - Light on-accent ink: design #FFFEFB; code #ffffff. These tokens are shared, so check the Messages and Practice lanes before changing them. ASK-24 covers only the undefined `--ch-bubble-mine-ink`. Fix: Add Ask-scoped tokens (`--ch-ask-accent`, `--ch-ask-card`, `--ch-ask-mine-a`/`-b`) under `html[data-fw-theme='dark'] [data-ui='clubhouse']`, rather than moving the global accent. Get a call from the owner if the global accent should move.
- **FID-ASK-27 Home: the greeting is 28 not 26, gaps are 2px short, and player focus rows are always amber** [P2] Ask home, coach and player, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:268, :280, :292, :326; src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Home.tsx:120 Wrong:  - Greeting `--ch-type-28-strong` (design 26). - Home gap 12 (14). - Pulse gap 8 (10). - `.pulse .is-warn` is a hard-coded `#edb867`, not a token. - Player focus rows hard-code `is-warn` at Home.tsx:120, so the design's green second row ("on track") is always amber. The DOT map at :96 exists but isn't used there. Fix: Use `--ch-type-26-strong` and gaps 14 and 10. Set `.is-warn { background: var(--ch-chart-loss-fill) }`. Map the focus row's trend through `DOT`.
- **FID-ASK-28 The request-more email names the team, not the player who asked** [P2] Player request more, server, n/a. File: src/app/golf/actions/coachhelm-ask-credits.ts:55 (`subject: \`Ask: more requested (${teamName})\``) Wrong: The alert says only which team asked, and the body is the optional note or a generic line. The coach can't tell which player wants more without opening another screen. The design receipt says "Sent to Coach Rivera… from Ava". Fix: Include the player's display name from the request row in the subject and body.
- **FID-ASK-29 Meter and out card geometry** [P2] Player allowance meter, out card, both. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:197-229 Wrong:  - Meter radius 16 (design 18); padding 12 16 (13 15); gap 8 (7). - Bar 4px tall at radius 999 (6px, radius 3). - Out card radius 16 (18); padding 16 (18); title 16 (18). Fix: Set each to the 06 value.
- **FID-ASK-30 The Knows panel error is styled as the allowance meter** [P2] Knows panel error, desktop. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/KnowsPanel.tsx:76 (`className="ch-ak-meter is-low"`) Wrong: The fact add or remove error borrows the meter's panel class, so it renders as a 16-radius padded meter box with loss text, not an inline error. It also gives no way to retry. Fix: Use the shared inline error (`ch-field__error` or a `Notice`-style row) under the list.
- **FID-QC-7 The live pill is 2px off on radius and padding, with the wrong type step** [P2] Live pill, both, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:374 (`.ch-qck .live`) Wrong:  - Radius `--ch-radius-lg` 16 (spec 18). - Padding 0 12 0 8 (spec 0 12 0 10). - Font `--ch-type-14-strong` (spec 13.5). - Background `#06231a` and spinner `#f4eedd`/`#7cc79b` (:377-381) are hard-coded hex, not tokens. QC-11 covers the 2px vertical offset only. Fix: radius 18, padding-left 10, `--ch-type-13-strong` with 13.5 via the k-scale. Move the colours to `--ch-qc-a-*` tokens.
- **FID-QC-8 The panel sheet body is 12px inset, not 14** [P2] Panel body, both. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:332 (`.qc__body` padding 12 12 16) Wrong: The spec is 14 14 16 inside the 6px inset sheet. The code is 12 12 16. Three rule sets assign `.qc__body` padding (:62, :289, :332) and `.qc` geometry (:50-51, :327-329, :339-340), and only the last wins, so the early values are dead code that misleads anyone editing. Fix: Set :332 to 14 14 16, and delete the overridden early rules :50-51, :62 padding and :289.
- **FID-QC-9 The header padding, gap and mark tile don't match the spec** [P2] Panel header, both. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:52, :55, :57 Wrong:  - Header padding 12 8 8 16, gap 8 (spec 18 14 12 20, gap 12). - A 30px avatar mark (spec: a 36px mark tile at radius 12). - Context line gap 4 (6). - Header buttons 36 (34). Fix: Set each to the spec value, and use the 36 tile with `--ch-radius-md`.
- **FID-QC-10 The request bubble and answer text are a half step too large** [P2] Request bubble, answer text, both. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:71, :72 Wrong:  - Bubble padding 8 12 (spec 9 14). - Bubble radius 20 20 6 20 (spec 19 19 6 19). - Bubble type 16 (spec 15.5). - Answer text 16 (spec 15.5 on a 22.5 line). Fix: Use the spec values; 15.5 comes from the k-scale (`--ch-qc-k`).
- **FID-QC-11 Steps, step summary and task header spacing is 2px short** [P2] Steps, steps summary, task card, both. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:74, :92, :95, :96, :101, :104 Wrong:  - Step gap 8 (spec 10). - Summary padding 4 8 (5 10 5 8). - Task header padding 12 12 8, gap 8 (14 14 10, gap 10). - Icon tile radius 8 (10). - Body padding 0 12 12 (0 14 12). - Fact gap 8 (10). Fix: Set each to the spec value.
- **FID-QC-12 "Stopped" bleeds into earlier finished turns** [P2] Run stopped, a session with several turns, both. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:49 (one `stopped` for the session), :385, :407 Wrong: `stopped` is one boolean for the whole session and is passed to every AssistantTurn. Once set, the last step of every finished earlier turn renders with the `stopped` class (:385). Every earlier turn with no blocks (a task-only turn) also shows "Stopped. Nothing was saved." (:407), even though it finished and saved. Fix: Store the stopped turn's id (`stoppedId`) and compare per turn.
- **FID-QC-13 Coins are 24px with 11px type, and the plan segment ink is hard-coded** [P2] Coins, plan bar, both, dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:117, :168, :172 Wrong: The coins are 24px with `--ch-type-11-strong`; the spec is 9.5 × k type on a 24 coin, and QC-21 already notes 11px coins. The plan segment ink is `color: #fff` (:168). Its dark override `.ch-qcs :root[data-theme="dark"] .pseg` (:172) can never match (extends QC-2), so on dark the white ink stays on the light dark-mode segment fills. Fix: Use `--ch-qc-a-seg-ink`, defined light #fff and dark #0e120f under `html[data-fw-theme='dark'] .ch-qcs`.
- **FID-QC-14 The key's accent colours are hard-coded hex** [P2] Key and anything using `--ch-qc-a-accent-text` outside the panel, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:347-348 (`--ch-qc-a-accent-text: #155a39`, dark `#7cc79b`, champ-ink `#725b2c` / `#cdbb8e`) Wrong: The key scope redefines the accent text and tint as literals instead of pointing at tokens.css. They will drift from the panel scope's values and from FID-ASK-26's fix. Fix: Point them at `--ch-green-700`/`--ch-accent-text` and `--ch-champagne-*` tokens.

### Design fidelity and features: Practice, Documents, Messages (39)
- **FID-PRAC-1 Practice tab, builder, drill bank, groups, templates, print studio, Run view and Calendar Plan row do not exist** [P0] all Practice routes, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/app/golf/(dashboard)/dashboard/practice/ (only `format/` and `plan/[planId]/` exist); src/clubhouse/shell/nav.ts:35-36 (CH_NAV_COACH has no Practice entry); src/clubhouse/screens/practice/model.ts (2-line re-export, no screens) Wrong: 02-PLAN N-00 §0.2 specifies nine routes: /practice, drills, drills/[id], groups, templates, plan/[id], plan/[id]/edit (Blocks, Groups, Drills, Review), print/[id] and makeup. It also specifies items 4.1 to 4.17: Share, six starter templates, the Run view, the Calendar Plan row, the team chat plan card and Seen by. 05-SCREENS draws the whole S-BLD.* and S-BLDD.* families. The code has one of the nine routes (plan/[id]) plus Plan format. No builder exists, no Practice tab is in the nav, and `CH_PRACTICE_PAGES` (nav.ts:214) matches routes that cannot be reached. A plan can only be made by asking CoachHelm. Fix: Build N-00 as planned, after applying the held N-00 migrations 010000 to 010800. Put the routes under app/.../practice/, add Practice to CH_NAV_COACH, and build the builder from the existing PracticePlanSheet model plus ui/ Sheet, Segmented and List rows. Until then, take the routes out of CH_PRACTICE_PAGES and stop the copy promising "the builder".
- **FID-PRAC-2 A plan in Documents cannot be opened** [P0] Team Hub › Documents › Practice plans, plan row tap, phone and desktop, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/data/hub.ts:905 (`ext = d.file_url == null ? 'plan' : …`); src/clubhouse/screens/hub/parts.tsx:680-790 (row → `writes.openDocument`); src/clubhouse/data/writes.ts:105-108 (getPreviewUrl) Wrong: A plan row has no `file_url`, so Open goes to getPreviewUrl, which fails with "This file has no stored copy to open." The comment at hub.ts:904 says plan rows open the plan page, but ChHubFile carries no planId, so nothing can route there. S-DOC.sheet-coach is reached from this row in the design. Fix: Add `planId` to ChHubFile from the golf_documents row (or the practice plan join). In parts.tsx, route a plan row to `/golf/dashboard/practice/plan/{planId}` with `router.push` instead of openDocument.
- **FID-PRAC-3 The plan format is saved but never used** [P1] Plan format set and see; Ask draft; plan sheet. Both platforms, both themes.. File: src/app/golf/actions/coachhelm-plan-format.ts:43 (writes `sections`); src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/practice-reads.ts (no format read); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/plan-sheet-model.ts (fixed section order) Wrong: S-FMT.set says "Plans from Ask and the builder now use it." No code reads `golf_practice_plan_formats.sections` when drafting or rendering a sheet. The sheet always uses its own fixed sections. The design's sheet foot line "In your format · Fall practice sheet" is never shown. "It learns as you go" (S-FMT.found, COACHHELM_PRACTICE_PLAN.md) has no code. Fix: Read the team's format in practice-reads.ts and pass `sections` to the draft prompt. Make plan-sheet-model.ts order and rename sections from it. Add the foot line. Until then, change the receipt copy so it does not claim plans use the format.
- **FID-PRAC-4 Undoing a new format deletes the old one** [P1] Plan format, set → Undo, both platforms. File: src/app/golf/actions/coachhelm-plan-format.ts:43 (`upsert … { onConflict: 'team_id' }`), :50 (`delete().eq('id', id)`) Wrong: Setting a format overwrites the team's only row. Undo then deletes that row. A coach who had format A, set format B and pressed Undo ends up with no format at all, not format A. S-FMT.undone shows the prior format kept. Fix: Before the upsert, return the prior row (name, sections, source_document_id) in the receipt's undo payload, and have undoPlanFormat upsert it back. Delete the row only when there was no prior row.
- **FID-PRAC-5 Format upload is filed in "Practice plans" and each retry leaves a document behind** [P1] Plan format, upload sheet → "Try another", both platforms. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/format/PlanFormat.tsx:75 (`createGolfDocument({ … player_visible: false, folder: 'Practice plans' })`) Wrong: The uploaded sample sheet is stored as a golf_document in the "Practice plans" folder. Held migration 20261026090300 adds a check that only plans may sit in that folder, so this insert will fail once that migration is applied. Until then, every "Try another" adds another file row to the coach's Practice plans list. Nothing removes the rows. Fix: File the sample under a non-plan folder (for example "Plan formats") or store it as a format-only upload. Delete the previous sample document when the coach picks another.
- **FID-PRAC-6 No Share PDF anywhere, and no Print in Ask's full plan** [P1] Plan page bar; Ask full plan; Plan format "see" dock; player plan bar. Both platforms.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanPageActions.tsx (Print only); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:682-740 (portal has no Print or Share) Wrong: The design draws Share PDF in S-ASK.share-pdf, in the S-FMT.see dock and in the S-PLY.plan bar. A grep finds no "Share PDF" in src/. The Ask full-plan portal has neither Print nor Share. Its only way out is opening the plan page. Fix: Add a Share PDF action to PlanPageActions and the portal footer that renders PrintPlan to a PDF (or uses `navigator.share` with a generated file), with a Print fallback. Use the ui/ Button secondary pair the design shows.
- **FID-PRAC-7 The player can't open the full plan, print it, or reach it from Documents** [P1] Player plan page; player Documents; phone, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/data/practice-plan.ts:97 (`documents: 'Team Hub › Practice plans'`), :98 (`planHref: null`) Wrong: `planHref` is always null, so PlayerPractice never shows "Open the plan" (S-PLY.plan) and the player has no Print (S-DOC.print-player). The Documents line is a fixed string, not a link or a real path. Fix: Set `planHref` to the plan page with a player sheet mode (PracticePlanSheet with the coach-only parts hidden), and add Print there. Make the Documents line a real link to the plan's Documents row once FID-PRAC-2 is fixed.
- **FID-PRAC-8 Publish: team chat post has no link, Told row never shows, no Seen by** [P1] Ask publish → published receipt; Team chat; Documents shared row. Both platforms.. File: src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/practice-draft.ts:170 (`${i.title} is published. ${i.when}.`), :171 (summary "Published. N people told.", no `notifications`); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:284 (`r.notifications.filter…`) Wrong: Default 4 says the team chat post is plain text with a link to the plan. The post has no link. The run returns no notifications array, so the receipt's Told row (the list of who was told) never renders. Seen by for plans (N-00 4.17, S-DOC.list-shared) does not exist. Fix: Add the plan URL to the post text. Return `notifications` from publishPracticePlan (recipients plus status) through run(). Add a Seen by line from the existing practice-plans recordOpen data, using the seen-by.tsx SeenFaces pattern.
- **FID-PRAC-9 Publish failed says "Nothing was saved." with no Try again, and the published Documents strip is missing** [P1] Ask publish failed and published receipts, both platforms. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:279 (`Nothing was saved.`); LANDS map (no `publish_practice_plan` entry) Wrong: For a failed publish the spec says "Nothing was sent." with a Try again button. The code reuses the save copy and has no retry. The design's published state shows a Documents › Practice plans strip. With no LANDS entry it never renders. Fix: Use kind-specific failed copy ("Nothing was sent.") and add a Try again button that re-runs the flow. Add a LANDS entry for publish_practice_plan pointing to Documents › Practice plans.
- **FID-PRAC-10 Plan format on phone draws two green bars (extends PP-16)** [P1] Plan format, phone ≤820, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/format/PlanFormat.tsx:118-124 (`.fbar.is-page`, Link to /golf/dashboard/calendar "Practice"); src/clubhouse/shell/nav.ts:369-370 (PUSHED_BELOW stand-in) Wrong: The shell's stand-in bar draws because the page renders no PhoneTop. The page then draws its own `.fbar` below it, so there are two stacked bars with two Backs. The page's own Back goes to Calendar, labelled "Practice". The design draws one bar. Fix: Render the page's bar as the shell's PhoneTop (so `pageTop` suppresses the stand-in), or drop `.fbar` on phone. Point Back to where the coach came from (Ask, via `from`).
- **FID-PRAC-11 The random drill kind is ochre in light and dark, not plum (corrects PP-17)** [P1] Plan sheet rotation, kind legend and segments, both platforms, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:103 (`--ch-pl-cpv-random: #8a6a2f`), :119 (`#c9a35d`), used at :178, :232, :391 Wrong: 04 §2 gives random as plum, #6E5487 light and #B49ACB dark. The code uses ochre in both themes. Ochre reads as the amber loss colour, which breaks "amber for losses only" (06 Fixes). Fix: Set `--ch-pl-cpv-random` to #6E5487 in light and #B49ACB in dark (or add `--ch-kind-random` in tokens.css and use it).
- **FID-PRAC-12 Plan format and plan page check different flags** [P1] /practice/format and /practice/plan/[id] routing. File: src/app/golf/(dashboard)/dashboard/practice/format/page.tsx:7 (`isPracticePlansEnabled` only); plan/[planId]/page.tsx:7 (`isPracticeTabEnabled` only) Wrong: 02-PLAN §0.1(12) says to check both flags. With coachhelm_practice_plans_v1 on and golf_practice_plans off, a coach can publish from Ask and players get a notification whose plan page redirects to Calendar. With the flags the other way round, Plan format redirects while plans still open. Fix: Gate both routes, and the publish flow, on `isPracticePlansEnabled() && isPracticeTabEnabled()`, or have publish refuse when the tab flag is off.
- **FID-PRAC-13 Plan format's error page sends "home" to a route that doesn't exist** [P1] Plan format error state, both platforms. File: src/app/golf/(dashboard)/dashboard/practice/format/error.tsx:20 (`homePath="/golf/dashboard/practice"`) Wrong: /golf/dashboard/practice has no page.tsx (see FID-PRAC-1), so the error page's way out lands on a 404. Fix: Set homePath to /golf/dashboard/coachhelm or /golf/dashboard/calendar until the Practice tab exists.
- **FID-PRAC-14 Documents plan rows look and act like files** [P1] Team Hub › Documents › Practice plans rows (coach), both platforms, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/data/hub.ts:905 (ext 'plan' → type "PLAN"); src/clubhouse/screens/hub/parts.tsx:771 (Download icon), :777 ("Delete file" → `onDelete` → deleteDocument) Wrong: The design rows read "Draft · Only coaches · Thu, Nov 5" and "Shared with players · Thu, Nov 5". The code shows the generic type "PLAN" and a Download icon on something that can't be downloaded. "Delete file" calls deleteDocument rather than deletePracticePlan, so it removes the document row and leaves the plan (and any published notices) behind. Fix: Give plan rows a state line from plan status and date, a chevron instead of Download, and a "Delete plan" item that calls the deletePracticePlan action in actions/practice-plans.ts with the planId from FID-PRAC-2.
- **FID-MSG-1 Pins sync is built but held; pins live on one device** [P1] Inbox pinned card, phone and desktop. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/use-conversation-pins.ts:97, :119 (localStorage path); supabase/migrations/HELD.md (20261031150000_golf_conversation_pins) Wrong: pin-sync/ promises pins that follow the user across devices. With the migration held, the hook falls back to localStorage, so a pin made on a phone is missing on desktop. The UI gives no sign of this. Fix: Apply 20261031150000 (owner decision). Until then, the feature is device-only and should be listed that way.
- **FID-PRAC-15 Sheet headings drop the player count and total time** [P2] Plan sheet Groups and Schedule headings, all surfaces. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/PracticePlanSheet.tsx (Groups and Schedule `.pp-h`) Wrong: The design has "Groups · 9 players" and "Schedule · 3 h" (redesign/coachhelm-practice.html:1314-1315). The code shows bare "Groups" and "Schedule". Fix: Append the count and total from plan-sheet-model.ts in a `.pp-h span` muted span.
- **FID-PRAC-16 Sheet foot copy is wrong and the page count is hard-coded** [P2] Plan sheet foot, all surfaces, print. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/PracticePlanSheet.tsx:122 (`Page 1 of 1`) Wrong: The design is "Draft · not published" or "Published Wed, Nov 4, 9:14 PM", then "In your format · Fall practice sheet", then the page. The code shows "Draft · Only coaches" or "Published" with no time, no format line, and "Page 1 of 1" even on a two-page print. Fix: Use the design copy with the published timestamp. Add the format line (FID-PRAC-3). Drop the page stamp on screen, and in print use a CSS counter in the print footer.
- **FID-PRAC-17 Bring uses "×", Notes draws 3 lines, empty Bring shows a bare heading** [P2] Plan sheet Bring and Notes, all surfaces. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/plan-sheet-model.ts:89 (`${b.item} × ${b.qty}`); PracticePlanSheet.tsx (Notes lines, Bring section) Wrong: The design is "Alignment sticks · 6". The code is "Alignment sticks × 6". Notes has 4 ruled lines in the design and 3 in the code. With nothing to bring, the code still draws the "Bring" heading over nothing. Fix: Use ` · ${qty}`, draw 4 note lines, and omit the Bring section when the list is empty.
- **FID-PRAC-18 Why lines don't mark losses in amber** [P2] Plan sheet `.pp-why` lines, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/PracticePlanSheet.tsx (pp-why); src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/practice-draft.ts:72-94 (planCardMeta evidence `String(m.value)`) Wrong: The design marks the losing stat in each Why line with the amber `.neg` style and U+2212. The code prints `String(m.value)` with no `.neg` span and a hyphen minus. Fix: Format the value with the shared stat formatter (U+2212) and wrap negative values in `<span class="neg">` with the amber loss token.
- **FID-PRAC-19 Print margin and page size differ from spec; no repeating header** [P2] Print, coach, desktop. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PrintPlan.tsx:14 (`@page { size: letter portrait; margin: 12mm; }`) Wrong: The spec is A4 or Letter at 16mm margins, with the plan title and date repeated at the top of each page. The code forces Letter, uses 12mm and has no running header. Fix: Use `@page { margin: 16mm }` with no forced size, and add a print-only header that repeats via `position: running()` or a table-header group.
- **FID-PRAC-20 Full plan sheet metrics drift on almost every rule** [P2] Plan sheet (plan page, Ask full plan, print), phone and desktop, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:1-82 vs redesign/coachhelm-practice.html:904-974 - What's wrong (design → code): | Element | Design | Code | |---|---|---| | paper padding / radius | 22 20 14 20, r5 | 20 20 12, r4 | | pp-top gap | 10 | 8 | | pp-mark radius / tracking | r9, .02em | r8, .01em | | pp-org span | 12.5/16.3 | type-13 | | pp-date padding / radius | 5 0 6, r9 | 4 0 6, r8 | | pp-date span | 10.5 600 .08em | type-11-strong .01em | | pp-title | 30/31.5 | type-28 | | pp-focus | 15.5/22 | 16 | | pp-meta div padding | 9 10 | 8 8 | | dd | 13.5 600, margin 3 | 14-strong, margin 2 | | pp-h | 13.5 | 14 | | pp-g padding / gap / radius | 8 8 9 8, gap 7, r10 | 8, gap 6, r8 | | gh | gap 5, 12.5 | gap 6, 14 | | gd | 700 | 400 (second rule) | | li | 13.5 | 14 | | drow gap / padding | gap 10, padding 10 0 | 8, 8 | | pn | 14.5 | 15 | | pd | 13.5 | 14 | | span.all padding / radius | 5 9, r7 | 4 8, r6 | | who span padding | 3 8 3 3 | 2 8 2 2 | | pp-why | 12.5/17.5, margin-top 7 | 13, 6 | | rot cells at phone (container query) | 32×28 | 40×30 | | rot .h | 11.5 | 12 | | pp-two gap | 18 | 16 | | pp-check gap / size | 7, 13.5 | 6, 14 | | pp-foot | padding-top 10, 11.5 | 8, 12 | Wrong:  Fix: Port the design values. The paper is a fixed document scale, so use its exact px values rather than the nearest type step. Add the `@container (max-width: …)` rule for 32×28 rotation cells. Remove the second `.gd` rule that resets weight to 400.
- **FID-PRAC-21 Plan format rows, hints and receipt drift** [P2] Plan format empty, set and learn states, both platforms, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/format/PlanFormat.tsx (set receipt icon, Undo) and its CSS (`.cur`, `.fhint`, `.lrn .ft`, `.okc`) - What's wrong (design → code): - `.cur` padding / gap: 14 16, gap 10 → 12 16, gap 8. - `.fhint` padding: 6 16 14 → 0 16 12. - `.lrn .ft` padding: 2 16 14 → 2 16 12. - `.okc` colours: gain-tint #E8EEE8 with gain #14613D, 22 round → accent-tint with green-600. - Set receipt check: 14 icon → CircleCheck 18. - Undo: 18 undo icon with gap 6 → no icon. Wrong:  Fix: Port the values. Use the gain tokens (`--ch-gain`, `--ch-gain-tint`) and ui/ Icon size 14 and 18 (Undo2).
- **FID-PRAC-22 Format "found" view shows 3 of 5 sections and no summary line; no More key** [P2] Plan format found state, both platforms. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/format/PlanFormat.tsx (found tags; bar) Wrong: The code always shows three tags (Header, Groups, Schedule), whatever the read found. The design lists every section read and the line "1 page · 5 sections". The design bar has a More key; the code bar does not. Fix: Render one tag per `sections` entry from readPlanFormat, add the summary line, and add the More key with the ui/ menu.
- **FID-PRAC-23 Player plan stops and chips drift** [P2] Player plan page, phone, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/player/PlayerPractice.tsx; src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:1347-1420 - What's wrong (design → code): - Stop padding / gap: 11 16, gap 10 → 12 16, gap 8. - now / next chip: 2 7, r7, gain-tint / gain and flat-tint #EFEDE7 → 2 6, r6, accent-tint / green-600 and well-bg-flat. - "In Documents" card: the title with the meta pushed right (`margin-left:auto`), no icon, meta not a link → a tile icon with stacked text. Wrong:  Fix: Port the values and use the gain tokens. Rebuild the card as a single ui/ List row with trailing meta.
- **FID-PRAC-24 Group faces are 24 px and leave out the viewer** [P2] Player plan group faces; Ask publish confirm coins. Both platforms.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/player/PlayerPractice.tsx:58 (`size="xs"`); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/ConfirmCard.tsx:118 (`size="xs"`) Wrong: The design faces are 26 and include the viewer (`avs(['Eli','Jonah','Owen'])`). The code uses Avatar xs (24) and lists only the others. There is no 26 Avatar size in ui/ (xs 24, sm 28). Fix: Add a 26 size to ui/Avatar (or a `.ch-av-26` modifier), use it in both places, and include the viewer marked "You".
- **FID-PRAC-25 Plan page loading and error aren't Clubhouse screens** [P2] Plan page and Plan format loading and error, both platforms, both themes. File: src/app/golf/(dashboard)/dashboard/practice/plan/[planId]/loading.tsx (`<SkelRows rows={8}/>`); plan/[planId]/error.tsx and format/error.tsx (Fairway RouteErrorBoundary, "Failed to load Plan format… Please try again.") Wrong: The loading state is eight generic rows, not a sheet-shaped skeleton. The error pages use Fairway's boundary and copy, which Clubhouse's error rule replaces. Fix: Use a paper-shaped skeleton (title, meta grid, rows) from the Clubhouse skeleton parts, and the Clubhouse route error component with plain copy.
- **FID-PRAC-26 Drill bank is capped at 20 and library drills all read as "skill"** [P2] Ask draft, drill choices. File: src/lib/coachhelm/v3/chat/practice-reads.ts:24 (`limit(20)`), :27 (`limit(20)`), :31 (library rows have no `kind`) Wrong: A team with more than 20 drills, or a library with more, silently loses the rest. Library drills carry no kind, so they default to 'skill' and colour the rotation wrongly. Fix: Filter by area and minutes before limiting (or page), and map the library `category` to a kind.
- **FID-PRAC-27 "?from=coachhelm" is passed and ignored** [P2] Ask full plan → Open plan page → Back, phone. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:395 (`?from=coachhelm`); src/clubhouse/routes/practice.tsx (no read of `from`) Wrong: The plan page never reads `from`, so Back uses the shell default rather than returning to the Ask thread. Fix: Read `from` in PracticePlanRoute and set the PhoneTop back target to the CoachHelm thread.
- **FID-PRAC-28 Unnamed groups fall back to "Player"** [P2] Plan sheet Groups, all surfaces. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/plan-sheet-model.ts (group name fallback `'Player'`) Wrong: The design names groups "Group 1", "Group 2" and so on. A group without a name renders as "Player", which reads as a person. Fix: Fall back to `Group ${index + 1}`.
- **FID-MSG-2 Pinned card padding, radius and gaps drift** [P2] Inbox pinned card, phone and desktop, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:4241-4330 - What's wrong (spec → code): - Header padding: 14 16 10 → 12 16 8. - Pins padding / radius / gap / grid gap: 10 4 9, r18, gap 7 → 8 4 8, r16, gap 6, grid gap 6. - Bubblet radius: r11 → r12. Wrong:  Fix: Port the values. Use `--ch-radius-lg` plus 2 (or a local 18) as the spec draws it.
- **FID-MSG-3 Pin faces: direct 64 with 22 initials, group 62** [P2] Inbox pinned card faces, phone. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:339 (`size="xl"` for direct pins); GroupMark at :489 (62) Wrong: The spec is one 62 face with 23.5/600 initials. Direct pins render at 64 with 22 initials, so direct and group pins sit at different sizes in one row. Fix: Add a 62 Avatar size (or a pin modifier) with 23.5/600 initials, and use it for both kinds.
- **FID-MSG-4 Group pins show a last-message bubblet** [P2] Inbox pinned card, group pin, phone. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:328 (`const said = c.lastSenderId !== api.viewer.userId ? c.lastText : null;`, no `!c.group` check) Wrong: MSG-LIST-07 says the bubblet is for direct conversations only. Group pins show it too, with no sender name, so it reads as if the group spoke. Fix: Only set `said` when `!c.group`.
- **FID-MSG-5 HelloCard face is 64, spec 72** [P2] Fresh conversation (HelloCard), both platforms. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/HelloCard.tsx:13 (`size="xl"`) Wrong: MSG-FRESH-01 draws a 72 face. Avatar xl is 64 with 22 initials. Fix: Add a 72 size to ui/Avatar (or a HelloCard modifier) with initials scaled to match.
- **FID-MSG-6 New message suggestion faces are 36, spec 32** [P2] New message, phone, suggestion list. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:1435 (`size="md"` under `api.redesign`) Wrong: The spec draws 32 faces in the suggestion rows. Avatar md is 36. There is no 32 size (sm 28, md 36). Fix: Add a 32 size to ui/Avatar and use it here.
- **FID-MSG-7 "Players you haven't messaged" lists coaches too** [P2] New message, desktop, empty search. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/NewMessagePane.tsx:34 (`api.directory.filter(… !direct.has(p.userId))`), :73 (subtitle) Wrong: The subtitle says "Players", but the list is the whole directory minus existing direct conversations, so coaches appear under it. Fix: Filter to `p.role === 'player'` under that subtitle, or change it to "People you haven't messaged".
- **FID-MSG-8 "Message the team" does the same as "Start a conversation" on phone** [P2] Messages first run, coach, phone vs desktop. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:319 (`onTeam={… ? onNew : undefined}`); MessagesView.tsx:3595 (opens the group dialog) Wrong: On phone, both buttons open the same blank New message. On desktop, "Message the team" opens the group dialog. The two platforms behave differently. Fix: On phone, open PhoneNewMessage in group mode with the team preselected, matching desktop.
- **FID-MSG-9 Attach tiles use the wrong fills; the attach tokens don't exist** [P2] Composer + attach sheet, both platforms, both themes. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:4798-4810 (Photos `var(--ch-bubble-mine-fill)`, File and Poll on well with a green glyph); src/clubhouse/styles/tokens.css (no `--ch-attach-*`) Wrong: Ruling 15 and 06 tokens (lines 52-54) give `--ch-attach-photos` #2F7D4C, `--ch-attach-file` #7F612A and `--ch-attach-poll` #155A39, each with an ivory glyph. The code has one green fill and two well tiles. Fix: Add the three tokens (light and dark) to tokens.css and use them with an ivory glyph.
- **FID-MSG-10 Switch off-ring is #8e8a80, spec #8F8B81** [P2] Mute and settings switches, off state, light. File: src/clubhouse/styles/tokens.css:683 (`--ch-switch-off-ring: #8e8a80`) Wrong: The spec is #8F8B81. The code is one step off on each channel. Fix: Set the token to #8F8B81 and check the dark value at :930 against the spec.
- **FID-MSG-11 First-run illustration has hard-coded colours** [P2] Messages first run illustration, dark. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesFirstRun.tsx:26-28 (`#fdfcf8`, `#d6c08f`) Wrong: The flag and highlights use literal hex. They don't change in dark mode, so the ivory strokes glare on the dark card. Fix: Use `var(--ch-ivory)` / `var(--ch-card-bg)` and the gold token via `currentColor` or CSS variables on the SVG.

### iPhone: shell, Classes, Roster, Home, Stats (36)
- **MOB-SHELL-1 Modal and FormSheet keyboard rules never run in Safari, so Save sits under the keyboard** [P1] any phone modal or FormSheet with a text field (ClassForm, GoalSheet, StatsFilter, roster edit sheets); all widths; any Text Size; light and dark; keyboard up. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/controls.css:617-625` (`body.keyboard-open .ch-modal`), `controls.css:1554-1583` (FormSheet `--keyboard-height` rules), fed by `src/components/providers/CapacitorProvider.tsx:204-228` Wrong: this extends ASK-2. ASK-2 found that the web fallback `innerHeight - vv.height - vv.offsetTop` reads 0 on Safari 26. A second consequence is new here: every CSS rule keyed on `body.keyboard-open` or `--keyboard-height` is dead in Safari and in standalone mode. Only `PhoneScreen keyboardAware` (`useSafariKeyboardFit`) adapts. The phone modal is a 92dvh bottom sheet (`controls.css:526-560`), so its footer Save sits about 300-340px under the keyboard on a 390×844 phone. The person must dismiss the keyboard to reach Save. ClassForm also has a hidden submit (`ClassForm.tsx:167-168`), so Return saves silently instead. Fix: move the sheet onto the existing `useSafariKeyboardFit` (`src/clubhouse/shell/PhoneScreen.tsx`). Either export it and call it from `ui/Modal` and `ui/FormSheet` when phone, or have it set `--keyboard-height` and `body.keyboard-open` from `visualViewport` (`innerHeight - vv.height`, ignoring `offsetTop`) so the existing CSS starts working.
- **MOB-SHELL-2 The new-version bar on phone sits under the home indicator and covers the Resume pill and composers** [P1] any phone page after a deploy; all widths; any Text Size; light and dark; keyboard down (and up: it then sits under the keyboard). File: `src/components/providers/StaleDeploymentRecoveryScript.tsx:48` (inline style string) Wrong: this extends QA-11, which covered this banner on desktop only. The inline style is `position:fixed;bottom:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:9999;padding:12px 16px;font-size:14px`. On phone: - (a) There is no `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`, so the 34px home indicator area overlaps the button in standalone and on Face ID phones. - (b) z-index 9999 puts it over the PhoneDock Resume round pill (`.ch-dock`, `shell.css:2778-2790`) and over the chat or note composers. - (c) The Reload button has 8px padding with 14px text, so it is about 33px tall, under 44pt. - (d) The raw 14px ignores iPhone Text Size. - (e) There is no dismiss, so a coach mid-round cannot get the Resume pill back without reloading. Fix: inside `[data-ui='clubhouse']`, render the notice through the shell (an `.ch-offline`-style bar under the PhoneBar, or a toast above `--ch-dock-h`) instead of the inline fixed bar. At minimum add `padding-bottom: calc(12px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`, a `min-height: 44px` button, `font-size: 0.875rem`, and a dismiss X.
- **MOB-SHELL-3 The PhoneBar back label is never shortened and runs into the centred title** [P1] any pushed PhoneScreen (Roster > player, Classes > class, Stats drill-downs); 320 at default Text Size; 375-390 from XL; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/ui/PhoneBar.tsx` (back renders the full `backLabel`), `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:1447` (`.ch-pbar__back`), `shell.css:1479` (`.ch-pbar__title` absolute, centred, `max-width:52%`) Wrong: the title is centred over the whole bar, so its left edge sits at (100% − 52%)/2 = 24% of the width. At 320 that is about 77px. The back control starts at 16px padding plus a chevron, so a "Roster" label ends at about 86px. That is a 9px overlap with the title's text box. At 375 and 390 the same happens from XL Text Size, once a "Classes" or "Statistics" label passes about 90px. There is no truncation, so the two strings draw over each other. They are not clipped. Fix: give `.ch-pbar__back` a `max-width: calc(24% - 16px)` with `overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap`. Also measure the label with ResizeObserver in `PhoneBar.tsx` and fall back to "Back", then to the chevron with `aria-label`, when it would cross the title box.
- **MOB-SHELL-4 Toasts land on top of the Resume round pill** [P1] any phone page with an in-progress round (dock visible) after a save or delete toast; all widths; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:1295-1299` (`.ch-toasts{left:12px;right:12px;bottom:calc(12px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))}`), dock at `shell.css:2778-2790` Wrong: the toast stack's bottom edge is 12 + 34 = 46px from the screen bottom on Face ID phones. The dock spans about 34-82px. The toast z-index is 100, above the dock, so every toast sits over Resume for its lifetime. A tap meant for Resume hits the toast or is swallowed. The `--ch-dock-h` spacer exists (it is used by the dock-spacer in `ClubhouseFrame.tsx`) but toasts ignore it. Fix: `.ch-root[data-phone] .ch-toasts{bottom:calc(12px + var(--ch-dock-h, 0px) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))}`, and set edges to `var(--ch-page-x-phone)` (see MOB-SHELL-15).
- **MOB-SHELL-5 The offline banner sticks at the desktop top-bar height, under or below the phone bar** [P1] any phone page while offline; all widths; standalone and Safari; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:1842-1856` (`.ch-offline{position:sticky;top:var(--ch-topbar-h)}`), `tokens.css:441` (`--ch-phone-topbar-h:50px`) Wrong: `--ch-topbar-h` is the 56px desktop token, with no phone override. The phone bar is 50px plus `env(safe-area-inset-top)` (`.ch-pbar`, `shell.css:1438`): - In standalone on a Dynamic Island phone, that is about 109px. The banner sticks at 56px, so it is hidden under the green bar while scrolling and the person never sees that they are offline. - In a Safari tab (top inset 0), the bar is 50px and the banner sticks 6px below it, leaving a gap that content scrolls through. Fix: `.ch-root[data-phone] .ch-offline{top:calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top))}`. Better still, render it inside the sticky bar container so it rides with the bar.
- **MOB-SHELL-6 Text Size jumps after hydration because the type scale is not set before paint** [P1] every page on first load or reload, any Text Size above Large; all widths; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/lib/dynamic-type.ts` (`useLayoutEffect(..., [])`), `src/clubhouse/shell/ClubhouseFrame.tsx:250` (`useChDynamicType()`), `src/components/golf/theme/ThemeScript.tsx` (BOOT sets only `--fw-type-scale`) Wrong: `--ch-type-scale` is written only after React hydrates. Server HTML and the first paint use scale 1. At XXL (21/17 = 1.235) every rem-scaled line then grows by about 24% after hydration. Lists reflow, scroll position shifts, and in standalone this is visible on every cold launch. The BOOT script already reads `-apple-system-body` for `--fw-type-scale`, but the Clubhouse tree ignores that variable. Fix: in `ThemeScript.tsx` BOOT, also set `--ch-type-scale` on `<html>`, using the same 23/17 cap as `dynamic-type.ts`. Keep the hook for later changes (see MOB-SHELL-11).
- **MOB-SHELL-7 Overscroll and status-area colour differ from the green bar and the page** [P1] every phone page; top rubber band and bottom overscroll; Safari (status tint on ≤18) and standalone; light (and dark, with dark tokens). File: `src/clubhouse/shell/ClubhouseFrame.tsx:307-353` (`usePhoneEdges`, :329 `html.style.backgroundColor = hero ? green : page`, :331 theme-color), `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:2441-2525` (green bar), `tokens.css` (`--ch-bg-page #ede9df` phone, `--ch-workspace #f5f0e4`, `--ch-grouped-bg #f2f0e9`) Wrong: the edges are painted `--ch-bg-page` (#ede9df ivory), while the bar is `--ch-frame` (#0a331f green): - Pulling down shows an ivory strip above the green bar, which reads as a web page, not an app. - Safari ≤18 tints the status area ivory above a green bar. - At the bottom, the overscroll is #ede9df under a page that is #f5f0e4 (app canvas) or #f2f0e9 (CRH pages), so a visible seam appears on every bounce. Fix: in `usePhoneEdges`, set theme-color to `--ch-frame`. Give `.ch-topbar` and `.ch-pbar` a green upward extension (`box-shadow: 0 -100vh 0 100vh var(--ch-frame)` or a `::before` of `height:100vh; bottom:100%`). Set the html background to the canvas colour actually under the page (`--ch-workspace` or `--ch-grouped-bg` per route).
- **MOB-SHELL-8 Landscape phones get the desktop sidebar shell** [P1] any page at 844, 852 or 932 wide (iPhone 13-16 and Pro Max landscape); any Text Size; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/lib/use-phone.ts` (`CH_PHONE_QUERY='(max-width: 820px)'`), `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:3-11` (`.ch-app` desktop grid; the comment at :6-8 says this is intentional) Wrong: turning the phone to landscape crosses 820px. The layout switches to the sidebar plus an inner `.ch-canvas` scroller with about 300px of usable height (390 minus the Safari bars). The phone tree unmounts, so pushed PhoneScreens, sheet state and scroll position are lost on rotate. Inner-scroller momentum and status-bar tap-to-top stop working. The manifest asks for `portrait-primary`, but iOS ignores that for web apps. Fix: make the phone query `(max-width: 820px), (max-height: 500px) and (pointer: coarse)` so landscape phones stay in the phone tree with landscape safe-area padding. **Decision for owner** (see summary).
- **MOB-SHELL-9 The home-screen icon renders on black because it is a transparent 512px logo** [P1] Add to Home Screen, the iOS home screen and the app switcher. File: `src/app/layout.tsx:64` (`icons.apple: '/Helm-Logo-New-Main.png'`), `public/manifest.json` (icons) Wrong: the apple-touch-icon is a 512px RGBA PNG, about 75% transparent. iOS fills transparency with black, so the icon is a small mark on a black tile. It is the Helm mark rather than the GolfHelm app mark, and there is no 180×180 asset. The manifest icons are about 76% transparent too. Fix: add `public/apple-touch-icon.png` (180×180, opaque, the brand green or ivory filling the square, the mark inset about 20%) and point `icons.apple` at it. Add opaque `purpose: "any"` and `"maskable"` icons to the manifest.
- **MOB-SHELL-10 The Resume pill's Discard X is a 36px target 4px from Resume** [P1] the PhoneDock with a round in progress; all widths; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:2845-2850` (`.ch-resume__x` 36×36), `.ch-resume` gap 4px; `src/clubhouse/shell/Dock.tsx` Wrong: Discard is destructive. It is 36pt, under 44pt, and sits 4px from the Resume link, so a thumb aimed at Resume's right edge can hit Discard. Fix: make `.ch-resume__x` 44×44, or keep the 36px circle with a `::after{inset:-4px}` hit area. Raise the gap to 8px. Confirm Discard with an action sheet if it does not already confirm.
- **MOB-CRH-1 The roster player profile note box is in a screen that ignores the keyboard** [P1] Roster > player profile (coach), coach note at the bottom; all widths; keyboard up. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/roster/RosterPhoneCrh.tsx:274-296` (`PhoneScreen` without `keyboardAware`), `RosterProfileCrh.tsx:178` (`<CoachNote>`), `RosterPeek.tsx:452` (textarea) Wrong: the textarea is the last thing on a long scroll. Without `keyboardAware`, focusing it leaves the caret under the keyboard. In Safari 26 the scroller does not shrink, so the save action and the text being typed are hidden (see MOB-SHELL-1). Fix: pass `keyboardAware` to that `PhoneScreen`, and `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})` the note on focus.
- **MOB-CRH-2 Home first-run quick-add chips are 32px with 6px gaps** [P1] Home empty state (coach and player, first run); all widths; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/home.css:1143-1162` (`.ch-hm-none__q` gap 6px, `.ch-hm-none__chip` height 32px) Wrong: these are the first controls a new user taps. They are 32pt tall and 6px apart, and they are missing from the `::after` hit-area list that other small chips use. Fix: add `.ch-hm-none__chip` to the existing `::after{inset:-6px 0}` reach list, or set `min-height:44px`, and raise the gap to 8px.
- **MOB-STATS-1 The goal sheet's number pad has no minus key, so negative strokes-gained targets cannot be typed** [P1] Stats > Set goal (coach and player); default metric `sg_putting`; keyboard up. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/GoalSheet.tsx:188-200` (`type="number" inputMode="decimal"`) Wrong: the iOS decimal pad has digits and a decimal point, but no minus key and no Return key. Strokes-gained targets are often negative (for example −0.5 putting), so they cannot be entered. With no Return key and the dead keyboard CSS (MOB-SHELL-1), the person cannot dismiss the pad to reach Save without tapping outside. Fix: for signed metrics use `type="text" inputMode="text" pattern="-?[0-9]*[.,]?[0-9]*"`, or add a ± toggle segment beside the field (an existing `ui/` Segmented). Add `enterKeyHint="done"`.
- **MOB-STATS-2 The big-text layout misses XXL, so the window switch overflows under the filter at 320** [P2] Stats (player), 320 wide at XXL; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/StatsPlayerPhoneShs.tsx:89-99` (`useBigText` `>= 1.24`), control row :222-229, `stats-redesign.css:49-58` Wrong: XXL is 21/17 = 1.235, below the 1.24 threshold, so the stacked layout does not engage. The five-column grid (`1fr 1fr 1fr 1.3fr 1.6fr`) beside the filter button overflows at 320, and the last segment runs under the filter. Fix: set the threshold to `>= 1.2`, or switch on measured overflow (ResizeObserver) rather than on the scale.
- **MOB-STATS-3 The window switch fades its last segment even when that segment is selected** [P2] Stats window switch with Custom selected, or the big-text layout; all widths; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats-redesign.css:59-65` (`.is-custom` mask `linear-gradient(90deg,#000 85%,transparent)`), :216-222 (`.is-big`, same mask) Wrong: the scroll-hint mask is always on, even when nothing scrolls. The last 15% of the control, which is the selected "Custom" segment, is faded toward transparent, so the selected state reads as disabled. In dark mode the label drops below 4.5:1 at its right end. Fix: apply the mask only while `scrollLeft + clientWidth < scrollWidth`, toggled by a class from a scroll listener, the same as other strips in `ui/`.
- **MOB-STATS-4 Starting a scroll on the scoring chart flashes a readout and fires a haptic** [P2] Stats scoring chart (coach and player); all widths. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/stats/ScoringFeatureChart.tsx:153-167` (pointerdown captures, picks and calls `hapticScrub('start')`), `stats.css` `.ch-shs-chart{touch-action:pan-y}` (`stats-redesign.css:321-325`) Wrong: a vertical page scroll that starts on the chart picks a point and buzzes before the browser sends `pointercancel`. Every scroll past the chart flickers a value and vibrates. Fix: do not pick a point or fire the haptic on pointerdown. Start the scrub after more than 8px of horizontal movement or a 250ms hold, and ignore it if `pointercancel` arrives first.
- **MOB-STATS-5 The leg-bars "open" link keeps its hover underline after a tap** [P2] Stats leg bars (coach and player); all widths. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/stats.css:7642` (`.ch-lb__open:hover`), used in `LegBars.tsx:114` Wrong: the hover rule is not guarded, so iOS keeps the underline on the last tapped link until the next tap elsewhere. Fix: wrap the rule in `@media (hover:hover)`.
- **MOB-SHELL-11 Smaller Text Sizes are ignored and Text Size is never re-read** [P2] every page at xSmall, Small or Medium; also after changing Text Size with the app open. File: `src/clubhouse/lib/dynamic-type.ts` (`if (... size <= DEFAULT_BODY_PX) return null;`, `useLayoutEffect(..., [])`) Wrong: sizes at or below 17px return null, so someone who chose Small (15px) still gets 17px text. The hook runs once with no listener. Changing Text Size in Control Centre and returning to the standalone app keeps the old scale until a reload. Fix: allow scales below 1, with a floor such as 14/17. Re-run on `visibilitychange` (to visible) and on `resize`, which iOS fires when Text Size changes.
- **MOB-SHELL-12 Bar side padding stays at 16px when the page edge is 20px (≥414)** [P2] Plus and Pro Max widths (414, 428, 430, 440) on top-level pages. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:1242` (`[data-phone='page']`), `:1258` (`[data-phone='start']`, forced `padding-left:16px`), `:1438` (`.ch-pbar` padding `... 8px 6px 16px`); `tokens.css:1444-1447` (page edge 20px at ≥414) Wrong: the bar title or back button sits at 16px while the content below starts at 20px, a 4px misalignment on every large phone. Fix: replace the literal 16px with `var(--ch-page-x-phone)` in all three rules.
- **MOB-SHELL-13 The route loading bar draws under the status bar in standalone** [P2] route changes in standalone mode. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:2069-2073` (`.ch-lp__bar{position:fixed;inset:0 0 auto 0;height:2px}`) Wrong: with `black-translucent`, y=0 is behind the Dynamic Island or notch, so the 2px progress bar is mostly hidden behind the clock and island. Fix: `top: env(safe-area-inset-top)`, or move the bar to the bottom edge of the PhoneBar.
- **MOB-SHELL-14 The bell count badge has an ivory ring on the green bar and fixed 11px text** [P2] PhoneBar bell with unread items; light and dark. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:1618-1630` (`.ch-bell__count` `box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--ch-bg-page)`, `font:600 11px/1`) Wrong: the cut-out ring was made for an ivory bar. On the green bar it draws an ivory halo around the badge. The 11px font does not follow Text Size. Fix: ring colour `var(--ch-phone-bar-bg)` on phone; font `600 calc(11 * var(--ch-type-k))/1`, or the existing caption token.
- **MOB-SHELL-15 The dock and toasts use 12px side edges** [P2] phone pages with the dock or a toast; all widths. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:2778-2790` (`.ch-dock{left:12px;right:12px}`), `:1295-1299` (`.ch-toasts`) Wrong: both floating surfaces sit 12px from the screen edge, while content sits at 16 or 20px. The Resume pill is visibly wider than every card above it. Fix: `left/right: var(--ch-page-x-phone)`.
- **MOB-SHELL-16 Bell stack pills and drag handle are small, with 2px between hit areas** [P2] bell stack (notification drop) on phone. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:3512-3600` (`.ch-bst-pill` 28px tall; `--x` 28px wide with `::before inset:-8px -2px`; tools gap 6px; `.ch-bst__drag` 56×24) Wrong: the pill dismiss X has a hit area of about 32×44, and adjacent pills' hit areas are only 2px apart. The drag handle's target is 24px tall. Fix: make the X `::before` inset `-8px` on all sides with a tools gap of 8px or more. Give `.ch-bst__drag` a 44px-tall hit area (`::before inset:-10px 0`).
- **MOB-SHELL-17 There is no pull to refresh in the home-screen web app** [P2] standalone mode, all list pages. File: `src/clubhouse/shell/PullToRefresh.tsx` (gated on `inIOSApp`) Wrong: Safari tabs have the toolbar reload. Standalone has none, and pull to refresh runs only in the Capacitor app. A home-screen user cannot refresh Home, Roster or Stats without closing the app. Fix: enable `PullToRefresh` when `matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches || navigator.standalone`.
- **MOB-SHELL-18 iOS scroll indicators are hidden in every scroller** [P2] all inner scrollers (sheets, section strip, tables, the landscape canvas). File: `src/clubhouse/styles/base.css` (`scrollbar-width:none` and `::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}` for all elements) Wrong: on iOS this also removes the thin overlay indicator that tells the person a sheet or list scrolls and how far. Fix: limit the hiding to horizontal chip strips (`.ch-strip`, segmented rows). Leave vertical scrollers alone.
- **MOB-SHELL-19 No launch image, and the manifest colours belong to another palette** [P2] cold launch from the home screen. File: `src/app/layout.tsx:67-70` (`appleWebApp` without `startupImage`), `public/manifest.json` (`theme_color #16A34A`, `background_color #FFFEFA`), `ThemeScript.tsx` (pre-paint theme-color `#f2e6d2` light) Wrong: launch shows a white screen, then BOOT's #f2e6d2, then `usePhoneEdges`' #ede9df, then the green bar. That is four colours in the first second. The manifest's bright #16A34A is not a Clubhouse token. Fix: set the manifest `theme_color` to `--ch-frame` (#0a331f) and `background_color` to the canvas. Set BOOT theme-color to the frame. Add `startupImage` entries, or at least a matching `background_color`.
- **MOB-SHELL-20 The refresh-flag and hero edge colours differ from the surfaces they extend** [P2] CRH pages with the refresh flag on; Home hero top. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:4360-4365` (phone page background `--ch-grouped-bg`), `:4387-4400` (CRH hero bar `--ch-frame`); `ClubhouseFrame.tsx:329-331` (edges `--ch-bg-page`, hero `--ch-green-800`) Wrong: on the hero, the edge is #0b3a25 while the bar is #0a331f, a visible step in the overscroll. On flagged pages, the bottom bounce is #ede9df under #f2f0e9. This is a narrower case of MOB-SHELL-7. Fix: make `usePhoneEdges` read one token per state (`--ch-frame` for the hero, the route's page background otherwise) instead of hard-coding `--ch-green-800` and `--ch-bg-page`.
- **MOB-SHELL-21 The pushed-screen scroll lock can make Safari's toolbar jump** [P2] Safari tab (not standalone) when a PhoneScreen pushes after scrolling. File: `src/clubhouse/shell/PhoneScreen.tsx` (`useOverlayScrollLock`, body `position:fixed`) Wrong: fixing the body while Safari's compact toolbar is collapsed makes Safari expand the toolbar and resize the viewport during the push animation, so the pushed screen's bottom jumps by about 50px. Fix: lock with `overflow:hidden` on the scroller plus `overscroll-behavior:contain` on the pushed screen, rather than body `position:fixed`.
- **MOB-SHELL-22 The search field autocorrects and capitalises names** [P2] Roster and TeamRoster search; keyboard up. File: `src/clubhouse/ui/SearchField.tsx` (no `autoCorrect`, `autoCapitalize` or `spellCheck`), used in `roster/Roster.tsx:692` and `TeamRoster.tsx:132` Wrong: iOS capitalises the first letter and autocorrects surnames ("Nguyen" becomes "Nguyen's", "Ko" becomes "Ok"), which breaks the filter. Fix: `autoCorrect="off" autoCapitalize="none" spellCheck={false} enterKeyHint="search"` on the input in `SearchField.tsx`.
- **MOB-CRH-3 The roster sheet scroller uses a fixed 20px edge on small phones** [P2] roster profile sheet; widths below 414. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/roster.css:1404-1410` (`.ch-rsm-scroll{padding:24px 20px}`) Wrong: the edge is 20px at 320-390, where the rule is 16px, so it misaligns with the bar and the page edge. Fix: `padding: 24px var(--ch-page-x-phone)`.
- **MOB-CRH-4 The Classes sticky day header is the wrong colour and not full width** [P2] Classes (phone, CRH flag on), while scrolling. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/classes.css:2149-2157` (`.ch-cl.is-phone .ch-cl-tthead` sticky, `background:var(--ch-workspace)`), `:2733-2736` (CRH page `--ch-grouped-bg` with a page-x padded container) Wrong: the sticky band is #f5f0e4 on a #f2f0e9 page and stops at the padding, so rows scroll visibly past its left and right edges while it is pinned. Fix: set the background to `var(--ch-grouped-bg)` on the CRH page, with `margin-inline: calc(-1 * var(--ch-page-x-phone)); padding-inline: var(--ch-page-x-phone)`.
- **MOB-CRH-5 The Home team scoring figure is a fixed 44px** [P2] Home team form card (coach); any Text Size. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/home.css:1414` (`.ch-hm-form__top b{font:500 44px/0.9}`) Wrong: this is raw px, so the main number on Home stays the same size at every Text Size while the text around it scales. At XXXL the label is larger than the figure's visual weight suggests. Fix: `font: 500 calc(44 * var(--ch-type-k))/0.9`, capped with `min()` if needed.
- **MOB-CRH-6 The Home score row is fixed at 46px and clips at XXXL** [P2] Home score rows; XXXL. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/home.css:1593-1600` (`.ch-hm-score{height:46px}`) Wrong: at XXXL (23/17) the two-line content needs about 50px, so descenders clip. Fix: `min-height: 46px; height: auto`.
- **MOB-CRH-7 Home's clash warning explains itself only on hover** [P2] Player Home and coach Home (flag off), Today list, overlapping events. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/home/HomePhone.tsx:318-320` (triangle with `title="Overlaps another event"` plus sr-only text) Wrong: on touch there is no hover, so a sighted user sees an unexplained triangle. Fix: show a short visible label ("Clash") next to the icon, as the CRH coach Home already does, or make the icon open a popover on tap.
- **MOB-CRH-8 Return in ClassForm saves the whole form instead of moving to the next field** [P2] Classes > New/Edit class; keyboard up. File: `src/clubhouse/screens/classes/ClassForm.tsx:167-168` (hidden submit); text inputs without `enterKeyHint` Wrong: the iOS keyboard shows "return", and pressing it in the first field submits a half-filled class. Fix: add `enterKeyHint="next"` and an Enter handler that focuses the next field on all inputs except the last, which gets `enterKeyHint="done"`.
- **MOB-CRH-9 Short CRH pages show a two-tone band below the content** [P2] CRH pages (Classes, Roster, Home) with little content. File: `src/clubhouse/styles/home.css:4515` and `classes.css:2733` (page `--ch-grouped-bg`), app background `--ch-workspace` (`shell.css` phone `.ch-app` and `.ch-canvas`) Wrong: the page container ends where the content ends, so below it the #f5f0e4 app background shows under a #f2f0e9 page, a faint horizontal seam. Fix: give the CRH page container `min-height: 100%`, or set `.ch-canvas` to `--ch-grouped-bg` when the CRH flag is on.

### iPhone: Team view, Root cause, Coach vs player (29)
- **MOB-TEAM-1 Team and file read sheets lose their height cap: tall sheets run off the bottom of the screen and can't scroll** [P0] Team view on phone. Read, player, course and gone sheets ("Details"). Player file and Your week "This read" sheets. 320 and 375 at default size; every width at XXL. Light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-team.css:1805-1809 (`.ch-htb-sheet .ch-modal__panel{max-height:86%}`); controls.css:525-536 (phone `.ch-modal__panel{max-height:var(--ch-modal-max-h)}` = 92dvh); controls.css:393-403 (`.ch-modal{overflow:visible}`); used by TeamBoard.tsx:380, PlayerFile.tsx:380, YourWeek.tsx:109 Wrong:  - The rule has specificity (0,2,0) and loads later, so it replaces the phone's 92dvh cap with `86%`. - The dialog has no definite height, only `max-height`. A percentage max-height against an auto-height parent computes to `none`. - The panel therefore grows to its full content height. The dialog is `overflow:visible`, so the panel spills below the viewport. `.ch-modal__body{overflow:auto}` never gets a bounded box, so it never scrolls. - The read sheet is long (head, lede, evidence badges, Comparison, ReadEvidence cards, drill, correction chips, Assign / Ask actions, "Open the full read"; panels.tsx ReadPanel). At 375 it is taller than the screen even at the default size. The actions and the "full read" link are pushed past the home indicator and can't be reached. Dragging scrolls the page behind or does nothing. Fix: Delete `max-height:86%` from `.ch-htb-sheet .ch-modal__panel`, or set `--ch-modal-max-h:86dvh` on `.ch-htb-sheet` so the shared cap and the flex column stay in charge. Check at 320x568 with XXL that "Assign" and "Open the full read" can be scrolled to.
- **MOB-CVP-1 Set-intent note is 15px, so Safari zooms on focus, the keyboard is never detected, and Save sits under the keyboard (extends CVP-13)** [P1] Player file, set-intent sheet (IntentSheet), the "Only you see this" note. iPhone Safari and standalone, every width.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:285 (`.ch-hf .note{font:var(--ch-type-15)}`); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/IntentSheet.tsx:103 (textarea), :60 (Modal with `footer`); src/components/providers/CapacitorProvider.tsx:216 (`viewport.scale > 1.01 ? 0 : ...`); controls.css (`body.keyboard-open .ch-modal{margin-bottom:var(--keyboard-height)}`) Wrong:  - At default Text Size the font is 15px. iOS zooms any field under 16px when it gets focus. - The keyboard fallback reports a height of 0 whenever `visualViewport.scale > 1.01`, on purpose. A focus zoom is exactly that case. - So `keyboard-open` is never set, and the sheet never lifts. The "Save plan" footer stays behind the keyboard, and the page stays zoomed after the keyboard closes. - The textarea also has no `enterkeyhint`. - CVP-13 covers the hand-drawn field and chips, not the zoom or the keyboard. Fix: `font-size:max(16px, calc(15 * var(--ch-type-k)))` on `.note`, or use the FormSheet TextArea, which should carry the same floor. Add `enterkeyhint="done"`. Check with the keyboard up at 320x568 that the footer is visible.
- **MOB-CVP-2 `.ch-hf` paints the sheet's `<dialog>`: square page-coloured corners behind the rounded sheet and a 48px band under it** [P1] Player file read sheet, Your week read sheet, and the set-intent sheet, on phone. Light (#f2f0e9) and dark (#0e120f).. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:57 (`.ch-hf{background:var(--ch-hf-a-page);padding-bottom:48px}`); PlayerFile.tsx:380, YourWeek.tsx:109 (`className="ch-htb-sheet ch-htb ch-hf ch-hf-sheet"`); IntentSheet.tsx:60 (`className="ch-hf ch-hf-sheet"`); controls.css:393-403 (`.ch-modal{padding:0;background:transparent}`) Wrong:  - The Modal's className lands on the dialog element. `.ch-hf` (0,1,0) loads after `.ch-modal` (0,1,0), so it replaces the dialog's transparent background and zero padding. - The dialog becomes a page-coloured rectangle. Its square top corners show behind the sheet's rounded corners, which ruins the sheet silhouette over the scrim. - The panel is pushed up by a 48px page-coloured band at the bottom, so the sheet appears to float above the home indicator. Fix: Scope the page rule to the page root (`main.ch-hf{...}` or `.ch-hf:not(.ch-modal)`), or pass a separate sheet class (for example `ch-hf-sheet` alone) and put the `--ch-hf-*` tokens on it without background or padding.
- **MOB-TEAM-2 Story viewer press-and-hold selects text and opens the loupe; vertical drags scroll the board behind and cancel the hold** [P1] Team view, story viewer, at every phone width, in Safari and standalone.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Stories.tsx:140-149 (`onPointerDown/Up/Cancel` → `held`), :168 (`.sto-b` div `onClick={next}`); coachhelm-team.css:1244, :1826 (`.story`), :1303-1325 (`.sto-b h3/p`); base.css (touch-callout only on button, a and role elements) Wrong:  - Pause is a long press on the whole viewer. iOS treats a long press on text as a selection: the h3 and p highlight, the loupe and the Copy / Look Up callout appear, and the selection stays when the finger lifts. - Nothing sets `user-select:none`, `-webkit-touch-callout:none` or `touch-action` on `.story`. - The viewer is `position:fixed` with no body scroll lock. A small vertical movement during a hold scrolls the Team board under the viewer and fires `pointercancel`, so the pause ends early. - There is no swipe-down to dismiss, the gesture every iOS story viewer has. Fix: On `.ch-htb .story`, set `user-select:none; -webkit-user-select:none; -webkit-touch-callout:none; touch-action:none` (or `pan-x`) and `overscroll-behavior:contain`. Lock the document scroll while the viewer is open, as ui Modal does. Add a downward-drag dismiss that follows the finger and respects Reduce Motion.
- **MOB-TEAM-3 Story body has no overflow path: at 320x568 or XXL the Back / Next footer is pushed off the screen** [P1] Story viewer at 320x568 (SE) at default size and at every width at XXL; worst on stories with the 18-hole grid.. File: coachhelm-team.css:1303-1310 (`.sto-b{flex:1;justify-content:center;gap:20px}`), :1311-1318 (h3 `--ch-type-28-strong`, p `--ch-type-16`), :1326 (`.sto-f`); :1244 (`.story` padding 48 top, 32 bottom) Wrong:  - The body is a centred flex column with no `min-height:0` and no `overflow`. - At k=1.353 the title is about 38px per line and the body about 22px. With the progress bar, the source row and the grid, the content is taller than 568 − 80px of padding. - Content centred with `justify-content:center` overflows at both ends: the top of the title goes under the progress bar and the `.sto-f` buttons fall below the bottom edge. Nothing scrolls. Fix: `.sto-b{min-height:0;overflow-y:auto;justify-content:safe center}`, and keep `.sto-f` as a non-shrinking footer. Check at 320x568 and XXL.
- **MOB-TEAM-4 Spot and Player bar rows are 30px tap targets with 12px gaps** [P1] Team view on phone, the Spot card rows and the player sheet bars ("tap a row to open the player").. File: coachhelm-team.css:397-406 (`.ch-htb .br{min-height:30px}`, row gap 12); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/SpotCard.tsx:30, :38 (`.br tapp` button) Wrong: Each row is a button about 30px tall, with 12px of dead space between rows. That is under 44pt, and a thumb landing in a gap does nothing. TEAM-12 covers the stations, not these rows. Fix: `min-height:44px` on `.br.tapp`, or a `::after{inset:-7px 0}` hit area with the row gap set to 0 and the rows padded instead.
- **MOB-TEAM-5 Spot row names truncate after about 7 characters** [P1] Spot card on phone at 375 and 390. Worse at 320 and at XXL.. File: coachhelm-team.css:400-406 (`.br{grid-template-columns:calc(92*var(--ch-type-k)) minmax(0,1fr) 52px}`), :407-413 and :1762 (`.bn{white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}`); SpotCard.tsx:22-27 (24px Coin + first name); team/parts.tsx (Coin small = Avatar xs 24) Wrong:  - The name column is 92px at k=1. Taking the 24px coin and 6px gap leaves 62px for a 13–14px first name. - "Christopher" and "Alessandra" show as "Christo…" and "Alessan…". - At XXL the column grows to 124px but the type grows too, so about 8 characters still fit. The spare width in the 1fr bar column is never given to the name. Fix: Make the name column `minmax(calc(92*k), max-content)` capped at about 40%, or drop the coin on phone. At XXL, put the name on its own line above the bar.
- **MOB-TEAM-6 At 320 the hole SVG shrinks to 0.81: station labels about 10.5px, coin initials about 9px, coins about 18px (extends TEAM-6)** [P1] Team view, Hole card, 320 wide (SE 1st gen and Zoomed display), every Text Size.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamHole.tsx:35 (`viewBox 0 0 330 400`), :92 (coin r=11), :100 (`+N` coin); hole-geometry.ts (HOLE.v w330); coachhelm-team.css:301-309 (`.stn .sl/.sv`), :317-322 (`.scn text` type-11) Wrong:  - The figure box at 320 is about 268px (320 − 2×16 edge − card padding), so the 330-unit viewBox scales by about 0.81. - Text in the SVG uses `--ch-type-*` in user units, so it shrinks with the drawing: type-13 labels render at about 10.5px and type-11 coin initials at about 8.9px. Both are under the app's 12px floor. - Coins become about 18px circles. The `+N` coin is the only way to open a crowded station. - TEAM-6 covers the opposite direction (labels growing with Text Size). This is the shrink at the narrowest width. Fix: Divide SVG text sizes by the rendered scale (measure the SVG width with a ResizeObserver and set `--hole-s`), or move the labels into HTML over the SVG. Give each coin and the `+N` coin a transparent r=22 hit circle.
- **MOB-TEAM-7 Phone landscape (844x390) gets the desktop Team layout, squeezed into about 486px** [P1] Team view, iPhone landscape 844x390 (and 852 and 932 Pro Max).. File: src/clubhouse/lib/use-phone.ts (`(max-width: 820px)`); TeamBoard.tsx:158; coachhelm-team.css:1592-1605 (`.feat .top` flex, chips `width:min(500px,64%)`), :1722 (`@media (max-width:1179px)` single column 720); shell.css:4334 (208 sidebar, 28 gutter), :8 (`.ch-app` safe-area padding) Wrong:  - 844 − 2×47 safe area − 208 sidebar − 2×28 gutter leaves a card about 486px wide. - The station chips get `min(500px,64%)` ≈ 311px for 5 chips, about 57px each, so "Short game" and "Approach" wrap. - The figure column left beside the chips is about 141px, so the 3-line caption wraps to about 6 lines. - Only about 300px of height is left under Safari's landscape bars, so the sidebar and the bar take most of the screen. Fix: Decide the phone layout on `(max-width:820px), (max-height:500px) and (pointer:coarse)` in `useChPhone`, or add a landscape-phone breakpoint. Owner decision; see the report.
