- **MOB-TEAM-8 Desktop inspector in landscape ignores the right and bottom safe areas and has no scrim** [P1] Team view, iPhone landscape 844x390 (desktop layout): the inspector opened from a station, row or story.. File: coachhelm-team.css:1645-1658, :1813-1817 (`.insp{position:fixed;top:58px;right:0;bottom:0;width:420px}`); src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Inspector.tsx (non-modal aside, Esc closes) Wrong:  - `right:0` and `bottom:0` put the panel under the Dynamic Island or sensor housing (rotated to the right) and the home indicator. Its close button and last row sit about 47px into the cut-out. - At 420 of 844px it covers half the screen. With no scrim and no tap-outside to close, a touch user can only close it with the X. Fix: `right:env(safe-area-inset-right);bottom:0;padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` and `width:min(420px, 50vw)`. Add a transparent scrim on `(pointer:coarse)`, or use the phone sheet when MOB-TEAM-7 moves landscape to the phone layout.
- **MOB-RC-1 Phone landscape (844x390) gives the Read page's middle column about 216px (extends RC-7)** [P1] Read page (root cause), iPhone landscape 844x390, coach view.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-read.css:1080-1100 (desk grid `250px 1fr 320px`, at ≤1179 `250px 1fr`), :1218-1258 (deskbar); ReadPage.tsx:142-156 (deskbar buttons); use-phone.ts (820 breakpoint) Wrong:  - Main area: 844 − 94 safe area − 208 sidebar − 8 ≈ 534. Minus 48 padding, the 250 rail and a 20 gap leaves about 216px for the read. - The deskbar ("Ask about this read" + "Message {first}" + ⋯, about 380px, no wrap) overflows it by about 164px. RC-7 measured 90px at 1180; this is about twice that. - The 44px hero, the ladder SVG and the headline wrap to one or two words a line. Fix: Same breakpoint change as MOB-TEAM-7. Also drive the grid from a container query on the main area (`@container (max-width:700px){grid-template-columns:1fr}`) so it doesn't depend on window width.
- **MOB-CVP-3 Phone landscape (844x390): the desktop file header leaves the player's name about 50px** [P1] Player file, iPhone landscape 844x390 (desktop layout).. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:293 (`.dhead .lt` nowrap ellipsis), :301-304 (`.aseg` 300), :295 (`.ib` 36); PlayerFile.tsx:326-391 (`.dhead` = Avatar xl 64 + `h1.lt` + `.aseg` + `.ib`) Wrong: In a main area of about 486px, the 64px avatar, the 300px lens control, the 36px ⋯ and the gaps leave about 50px for the h1. The name ellipsizes to two or three letters ("Ch…"). It is the page title. Fix: `.dhead{flex-wrap:wrap}` with `.aseg{flex:1 1 100%}` under a container query on the main width (about 640px), or use the phone layout per MOB-TEAM-7.
- **MOB-TEAM-9 Coach Brief "Open" buttons are 30px tall** [P1] Coach Brief on phone (`?view=brief`), Open reads and Working on rows.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/views/CoachBrief.tsx:65 (`<Button variant="secondary" size="sm" ... className="ch-hmb-row__a">Open</Button>`); src/clubhouse/styles/ui.css:36-43 (`.ch-btn--sm{height:30px}`); coachhelm-brief.css:99-110 (phone row) Wrong: The sm button has a fixed 30px height and no extended hit area. On phone it is the only action in each row, so each row's one target is 30×~58px. The Plan screen adds a `::after` hit area only under `.ch-cpv` (coachhelm-plan.css:1556). Fix: Make the whole `.ch-hmb-row` a link on phone, with the chevron as its affordance, or add `.ch-hmb-row__a::after{content:'';position:absolute;inset:-7px -4px}`.
- **MOB-TEAM-10 Read sheet correction chips are 36px; the 44px hit area exists only in the file's sheet** [P2] Team view read sheet on phone ("Is this right?" chips and "Change").. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm.css:1833-1930 (`.ch-hl-cx__c{all:unset;min-height:36px;padding:0 16px}`, gap 8; `.ch-hl-cx__chg{min-height:36px;padding:0 4px}`); coachhelm-file.css:99-102 (`.ch-hf.ch-hf-sheet ... ::after{inset:-4px 0}`, file sheet only) Wrong: The same correction chips get an extra 8px only when inside `.ch-hf.ch-hf-sheet`. In the Team read sheet they are 36px with 8px gaps, and "Change" is about 36×55 with 4px of padding. RC-12 covers link chips on the Read page, not these. Fix: Move the `::after` hit area onto `.ch-hl-cx__c` and `.ch-hl-cx__chg` themselves, and drop the file-only override.
- **MOB-RC-2 Read page correction chips and "Change" are 36px** [P2] Read page on phone, the correction row.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-read.css:947-1000 (`.ch-hr .cx button{min-height:36px}`, `.cx .chg`) Wrong: 36px chips with 8px gaps, and a "Change" text link narrower than 44, in the row where a coach marks a read wrong. RC-12 covers the link chips higher up, not this row. Fix: `min-height:44px`, or a `::after{inset:-4px 0}` hit area, on `.cx button` and `.cx .chg`. Share it with MOB-TEAM-10.
- **MOB-TEAM-11 "Open the full read" and "Open {first}'s page" links are about 32px tall** [P2] Team view phone sheets (read and player), the closing link.. File: coachhelm-team.css:1206-1213 (`.ch-htb .lk{display:inline-flex;padding:6px 0;font:var(--ch-type-15-strong)}`); team/panels.tsx (ReadPanel `Link.lk`, PlayerPanel :240) Wrong: 15px text with 6px of padding gives a target about 32px tall and only as wide as the text. It is the main way forward from the sheet. Fix: `.ins .lk{min-height:44px;width:100%}`, or render it as the ui list row used elsewhere in sheets.
- **MOB-TEAM-12 Story tiles snap flush against the card edge and chain overscroll to the page** [P2] Team view, story tiles strip on phone.. File: coachhelm-team.css:136-146 (`.tiles{overflow-x:auto;scroll-snap-type:x mandatory;padding:2px 16px 16px}`), :147-150 (`.tile{scroll-snap-align:start}`) Wrong:  - With no `scroll-padding-inline`, a snap to `start` puts the tile's left edge at the scroller's edge, not at the 16px gutter. After any swipe, the strip sits 16px out of line with the cards above and below. - With no `overscroll-behavior-x:contain`, a hard swipe at the end bounces the strip and can start Safari's back-swipe at the left edge. Fix: `scroll-padding-inline:var(--ch-tb-edge); overscroll-behavior-x:contain` on `.tiles`.
- **MOB-TEAM-13 Course sheet rows: minimum column widths exceed the sheet at 320, so the value clips** [P2] Team view, Course sheet, 320 wide, and 375 at XXL.. File: coachhelm-team.css:745-753 (`.cp{grid-template-columns:auto minmax(64px,1fr) minmax(64px,36%) auto}`), :801-804 (`.v{min-width:62px}`) Wrong: The fixed minimums (hole label, 64, 64, 62 and gaps) add up to about 238px. The row has about 224px inside the sheet at 320. The last column, the value, overflows the sheet's right edge. Fix: Drop the second `minmax` to `minmax(0,36%)` and `.v{min-width:auto}`, or stack the value under the bar below a 340px container width.
- **MOB-TEAM-14 Story action scrolls the Working card under the phone top bar** [P2] Team view on phone, a story's "See what they're working on" action.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamBoard.tsx:189-195 (`scrollIntoView({block:'start'})` on `[data-ch-team-card="work"]`); coachhelm-team.css (no `scroll-margin-top` on `[data-ch-team-card]`); tokens.css:441 (`--ch-phone-topbar-h:50px`) Wrong: The window scrolls the card's top to y=0. The bar is 50px plus a 47–62px top safe area, so the card's header lands about 100px under the glass bar. Fix: `[data-ch-team-card]{scroll-margin-top:calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top) + 8px)}`.
- **MOB-TEAM-15 Coach Brief section links land under the phone bar: scroll-margin is 72px, the bar is about 100px** [P2] Coach Brief on phone, opened with `?section=` (week, patterns, working, record, goals).. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-brief.css:114-116 (`.ch-hbr-sec{scroll-margin-top:72px}`); tokens.css:441 (`--ch-phone-topbar-h:50px`) Wrong: 72px was sized for the desktop sticky head. On phone the bar is 50px plus a 47–62px top safe area (97–112px), so each section's heading is 25–40px under the bar after the jump. Fix: `scroll-margin-top:calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top) + 12px)` under the phone breakpoint.
- **MOB-TEAM-16 Coach Brief goal player names are 13px inline links about 18px tall** [P2] Coach Brief on phone, Goals section, each player name.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-brief.css:132-137 (`.ch-hbr-goals__name{justify-self:start;font:var(--ch-type-13-medium)}`); CoachBrief.tsx:76 Wrong: The name is the only link to the player, but it is an 18px-tall text link as wide as the name. There is a hover state but no press state, so a tap shows nothing. Fix: `display:inline-flex;min-height:44px;align-items:center` (or a `::after` hit area), plus an `:active` tint.
- **MOB-TEAM-17 Chosen station resets to the biggest leak after going back from a player or read** [P2] Team view on phone: pick a station, open a row's full page, then swipe back.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamBoard.tsx:161 (`useState<TeamStation>(initialStation ?? leak)`) Wrong: The station is component state. A back navigation remounts the board, so the chip and the hole snap back to the biggest leak and the coach loses their place. Native back keeps it. Fix: Use `useChSessionState('team.station', ...)` or write it to the URL with `replaceState`.
- **MOB-TEAM-18 The "+N" coin has a click handler but no role, focus or label, and is about 18–22px** [P2] Team view hole, a station with more players than slots.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/TeamHole.tsx:100 (`g.scn.is-more` with onClick inside an `aria-hidden` group) Wrong: The coin opens the station's list, but it sits in an `aria-hidden` group with no role or tabIndex. VoiceOver can't reach it, and its target is a 22-unit circle (about 18px at 320). FID-TEAM-13 covers what the coin shows, not how it is reached. Fix: Remove the onClick and rely on the station's `g.stn role=button`, or make it a focusable `role="button"` with an `aria-label` and an r=22 transparent hit circle.
- **MOB-TEAM-19 Story tap-to-advance region has no `touch-action:manipulation`** [P2] Story viewer, tapping the body to advance quickly.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/team/Stories.tsx:168 (`<div className="sto-b" onClick={next}>`); base.css (`touch-action:manipulation` only on button, a, [role=button] and similar) Wrong: The advance target is a plain div, so the base rule doesn't reach it. Two quick taps can be read as a double-tap zoom, which zooms the viewer and skips nothing. Fix: Covered if MOB-TEAM-2 sets `touch-action` on `.story`. Otherwise add `touch-action:manipulation` to `.sto-b`.
- **MOB-CVP-4 Deep link to a file section scrolls it under the bar and the sticky lens control** [P2] Player file on phone, opened with a section target (from Brief, a story or a notification).. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/file/PlayerFile.tsx:165-170 (`el?.scrollIntoView?.({block:'start',behavior:'auto'})`); coachhelm-file.css:62 (`.sticky-seg`) Wrong: There is no `scroll-margin-top` on the targets. The target's top goes to y=0, under the 50px bar plus the top safe area and the sticky lens Segmented below it, about 150px in all. The card the link pointed to opens with its header hidden. Fix: Give the file's section cards `scroll-margin-top:calc(var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + env(safe-area-inset-top) + <seg height> + 8px)`, or expose the sticky height as a variable and use it.
- **MOB-CVP-5 Long read titles in the file's read rows are cut to one line on phone** [P2] Player file and Your week read list on phone, 375 and below, and at XXL.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:289-291 (`.r__t b` and `.r__t small` nowrap ellipsis, unless `.l2`) Wrong: Read titles are full sentences ("Wedge distance control from 80 to 120 yards"). On a 343px row with a badge and chevron, about 26 characters fit, so the part that says what the read is about is cut. Only rows with `.l2` get two lines. Fix: Apply the `.l2` clamp (`-webkit-line-clamp:2`) to every `.r__t b` under the phone breakpoint.
- **MOB-CVP-6 Card header meta truncates to a few characters at 320 and XXL** [P2] Player file cards on phone, header right side ("Last 10 rounds" and similar).. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:287-288 (`.card__h h4{flex:none;max-width:70%}`; `small` nowrap ellipsis) Wrong: The title takes up to 70% and doesn't shrink, so the `small` gets what's left. At 320 with XXL that is about 70px, so "Last 10 rounds" becomes "Last 1…", which reads as a different number. Fix: `.card__h{flex-wrap:wrap}` and remove the ellipsis from `small`, or move the meta to its own line below the phone container width.
- **MOB-CVP-7 File correction buttons and "Change" are 36px outside the sheet** [P2] Player file page on phone (not the sheet): the "Is this right?" row on read cards.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-file.css:245 (`.corr button{min-height:36px}`), :250 (`.cx__chg` 36) Wrong: The 44px hit area (:99-102) applies only inside `.ch-hf-sheet`. On the page itself the same controls are 36px. CVP-4 covers their tokens, not their size. Fix: Move the `::after{inset:-4px 0}` hit area to `.ch-hf .corr button` and `.ch-hf .cx__chg`.
- **MOB-CVP-8 File sheets carry `.ch-htb`, so the Team view's catch-all motion rules run inside them** [P2] Player file and Your week read sheets, with Reduce Motion on.. File: PlayerFile.tsx:380 and YourWeek.tsx:109 (`className="ch-htb-sheet ch-htb ch-hf ch-hf-sheet"`); coachhelm-team.css:1683-1699 (reduced-motion block for `.ch-htb` descendants) Wrong: `.ch-htb` was added to borrow the read panel's styles, but it also applies every `.ch-htb` rule to the dialog: the Team reduced-motion overrides, the `.ch-htb .lk` link sizing and the sheet palette. A change to the Team view now changes the file's sheet as well. It is also part of why MOB-TEAM-1 reaches these sheets. Fix: Split out the read panel styles under a `.ch-readpanel` class that both screens use, and drop `ch-htb` from the file Modals.

### iPhone: Ask, Quick chat (34)
- **MOB-ASK-1 Answer bar chart track collapses to 6px at 320** [P0] Ask v1 answer with a bars figure (e.g. "Who's hitting the most greens"), 320; 375 is degraded. Any Text Size, light and dark, keyboard down. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:488 (`.ch-th--phone .ch-th-bar`, grid `70px minmax(0,1fr) 84px`, gap 8) inside `.ch-th--phone .ch-th-fig` :384 (padding 16) Wrong: The track gets 176 − 70 − 84 − 16 = 6px at 320, 61px at 375 and 76px at 390. At 320 every bar looks identical, so the chart says nothing. At XXL the 70px label column breaks long names onto 2–3 lines while the track stays at 6px. Fix: At ≤374 stack each row: label and value on one line (`grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr) auto`) with the track full width beneath (`grid-column: 1 / -1`). Use `--ch-space-4` between rows. Do the same at XXL by keying on `--ch-type-k`, or with a container query on `.ch-th-fig` (`@container (max-width: 260px)`).
- **MOB-ASK-2 SG anatomy diverging track is about 12px at 320** [P0] Ask v1 strokes-gained anatomy answer, 320 (67px at 375). Default Text Size, light and dark. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:1545 (`.ch-th--phone .ch-th-va`: label 88, value 44, gap 8, padding 12 16) and :1607 (grid) Wrong: 208 card width − 32 padding − 88 − 44 − 16 gaps leaves about 12px of track, 6px on each side of zero. A −0.8 putting bar and a +0.3 driving bar differ by about 2px. The chart cannot be read at 320. Fix: Under a container query (`.ch-th-va` container width < 280), put the label row above the track (`grid-template-areas: "label value" "track track"`). Keep `--ch-th-va-value: 44px`.
- **MOB-QC-1 Landscape iPhone opens the 640px desktop panel and focuses its field off screen (extends QC-16)** [P0] Quick chat opened from the top-right key, landscape 844x390 and 932x430 (desktop branch), keyboard up. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:87 (`if (!phone) field.current?.focus(...)`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:340 (`.ch-qcs.is-desk .qc { top: 62px; height: 640px }`) Wrong: QC-16 noted the 640px height. The new problem is that on a landscape phone the composer sits at about y=702 in a 390px viewport (62 + 640). The desktop branch calls `focus()` on open, so iOS raises the keyboard (about 200px in landscape) for a field that is 300+px off screen. Safari then scrolls the layout viewport to reach the fixed field, which shifts the page behind. The user sees a header, a keyboard, and no field. Fix: Gate the focus with `matchMedia('(pointer: fine)')`, as Composer.tsx already does with `finePointer`. Set the desktop panel height to `min(640px, calc(100dvh - var(--ch-qc-qc-top) - 14px))`.
- **MOB-QC-2 Phone panel has no minimum height, so with the keyboard up in mini landscape the composer is clipped** [P0] Quick chat, iPhone 12/13 mini landscape 812x375 (phone layout), keyboard up. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:72–73 (`Math.min(700, kb - bar - 6 - 8)`); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:339 (`.qc` fixed, overflow hidden) Wrong: `kb` is about 375 − 200 (keyboard + QuickType bar) ≈ 175 and `bar` is about 55, so the height is about 114px. The header (~68) plus the composer (~56) need 124, so the composer is clipped by `overflow:hidden` and the body is 0. Nothing clamps the height from below and nothing reflows for this case. Fix: Clamp with `Math.max(header + composer, …)`. When the space is below that, collapse the header to its 44px close row and let the body scroll to 0, or present the panel full-screen (`top: env(safe-area-inset-top)`) while the keyboard is up.
- **MOB-ASK-3 Blue "send" return key inserts a newline on iPhone** [P1] Ask composer, every phone width, keyboard up. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Composer.tsx:408 (`enterKeyHint="send"`) and :340 (`if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && finePointer)`) Wrong: iOS draws a blue "send" return key, but with a coarse pointer the handler ignores Enter, so tapping it adds a line break. The key's label promises one thing and does another. This is the most common action in the lane. Fix: Either keep `enterKeyHint="send"` and submit on Enter for coarse pointers too (a newline stays reachable by paste), or set `enterKeyHint="enter"` on coarse pointers so the key reads "return". The first matches Messages.
- **MOB-ASK-4 Plan sheet inputs are 15px, so iOS zooms the page on focus** [P1] Ask, practice plan card sheet: "Ask about this block", "Ask about this station" and the folder-name field. All widths, default Text Size (zoom happens below a computed 16px). File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:474 (`.ch-cpv .fr .inp`, `--ch-type-15`), :686–694 (`.ch-cpv-sheet .ask-in input`, `--ch-type-15`); TSX src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:469, 603, 646 Wrong: The computed font size is 15px at default Text Size, so Safari zooms the page about 1.07x on focus and does not zoom back out after blur. The sheet then sits off-centre with horizontal pan. Fix: Use `--ch-type-17` (the body style) for these inputs, or `max(16px, var(--ch-type-15))` if the look must stay.
- **MOB-QC-3 Quick chat "New folder" field is 15px, so iOS zooms inside the glass panel** [P1] Quick chat, plan task, new folder row, all phone widths, keyboard up. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:262 (`.ch-qcs .fnew input`, `--ch-type-15-medium`); TSX src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/visuals/PlanTask.tsx:187 Wrong: Same 15px zoom, but inside a fixed panel. After the zoom the panel's `left:10px; right:10px` no longer matches the visual viewport, so the panel is cut on the right until the user pinches back. The field also autofocuses via `ref={(el) => el?.focus()}`, so the zoom happens without a tap. Fix: `font: var(--ch-type-17-medium)` (or `max(16px, …)`). Also add `enterKeyHint="done"` and `autoCapitalize="words"`.
- **MOB-QC-4 Auto-scroll fires on every streamed token, smooth, with no stick-to-bottom check** [P1] Quick chat body while an answer streams, all phone widths, with the user scrolled up to reread. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:140–143 (`useEffect … el.scrollTo({ top: el.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth' })` on `[chat.messages]`) Wrong: `chat.messages` changes on every token, so each token starts a new 300–400ms smooth scroll that cancels the last one. iOS shows this as a constant stutter, and a user who scrolls up to reread is pulled back down about every 30ms. Momentum scrolling is cut off by each programmatic scroll. Fix: Track `atBottom = scrollHeight - scrollTop - clientHeight < 24` in a scroll listener. Scroll only when `atBottom`, use `behavior:'auto'` during a stream and `'smooth'` only for a new user message. Show a "jump to latest" pill otherwise (the existing Ask pattern requested in ASK-15).
- **MOB-QC-5 Swipe-to-close on the panel header never fires on touch** [P1] Quick chat on phone, swiping up on the header to close, all widths. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:196–205 (`onPointerDown`/`onPointerUp` swipe), spread on `.qc__h` (:213) and the grab (:341) Wrong: The header has no `touch-action: none`, so iOS turns the vertical drag into a pan, sends `pointercancel`, and never sends `pointerup`. `swipeFrom` is never compared, so the swipe never closes the panel. Only a tap-and-release with no movement reaches `onPointerUp`. Fix: Set `touch-action: none` on `.qc__h` and the grab. Handle `onPointerCancel` by resetting. Use `setPointerCapture` on pointerdown and track `pointermove` so the panel follows the finger, as `ui/Modal`'s sheet drag does.
- **MOB-QC-6 Grab handle swipe zone is 36x5px and sits in the home-indicator gesture zone** [P1] Quick chat on phone, bottom of the panel, all widths, portrait and standalone. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:341 (`<i className="ch-grab" …>`, no `ch-grab--hit`); src/clubhouse/styles/controls.css:1524 (`.ch-grab--hit::after`, the shared hit area) Wrong: The handle is only the visible 36x5 bar, because it does not use the shared `ch-grab--hit` hit area. The panel bottom is 8px above the visual viewport bottom (see MOB-QC-7), so an upward swipe there is taken by iOS's home gesture first. Fix: Add `ch-grab--hit`, make the zone a full-width 21pt strip (`.qc__sheet::after`), and lift the panel clear of the indicator (MOB-QC-7).
- **MOB-QC-7 Phone panel ignores safe-area-inset-bottom and ends in the home-indicator zone** [P1] Quick chat on phone, keyboard down, Face ID iPhones (375/390/430), Safari with the toolbar collapsed and standalone mode. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:72–73 (`kb - bar - 6 - 8`, no inset); src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:339 Wrong: The panel bottom is 8px above `visualViewport` bottom. In standalone on a 13 mini (812 high, inset 34) the panel ends at y≈804, so the composer's lower 26px and its send button sit under the home indicator. In Safari, when the toolbar collapses, `visualViewport.height` grows but nothing re-runs `place()` until resize, so the panel is short or long by about 50px. Fix: Subtract `max(8px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))` when the keyboard is down (`vv.height ≈ innerHeight`), read through a CSS var probe. Re-run `place()` on `visualViewport` `resize`, which already covers the toolbar change.
- **MOB-ASK-5 Artifact push on phone has no edge-swipe back and no history entry** [P1] Ask, opening an artifact (plan, report) from an answer on phone, all widths. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Artifact.tsx:75–85 (plain fixed `div.ch-ak-push`, not a `PhoneScreen`) Wrong: Every other pushed phone screen uses `PhoneScreen`, which has `useEdgeSwipeBack`. This one does not. The left-edge swipe therefore hits Safari's own back gesture, which goes back past the Ask page and loses the conversation's scroll position and draft. The browser Back button (and the Android back button) does the same, because nothing pushes a history state. Fix: Render the push through `PhoneScreen`, or push `history.pushState({ artifact })` on open and close on `popstate`, plus `useEdgeSwipeBack(close)`.
- **MOB-ASK-6 Action-card Confirm button is clipped at 320 with XXL text** [P1] Ask v1 confirm card (e.g. "Move Saturday Spring Preview Tournament"), 320, Text Size XXL (k≈1.353). File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:1206 (`.ch-th--phone .ch-th-act__foot .ch-btn { flex: 1 1 0 }`) with `.ch-btn` `white-space: nowrap`, inside `.ch-th-act` :1055 (`overflow: hidden`) Wrong: At XXL, Cancel and Confirm need about 107px each with their padding, a total of 222 > 208 available. `nowrap` stops the labels from shrinking, the row overflows, and the card's `overflow:hidden` cuts about 14px off the right of Confirm, the primary action. Fix: `flex-wrap: wrap` on `.ch-th-act__foot`, with `.ch-btn { flex: 1 1 120px }`, so the buttons stack full width when they don't fit (Confirm on top, the iOS alert pattern).
- **MOB-ASK-7 Action-card fact values break mid-word at 320 with XXL text** [P1] Ask v1 confirm card facts ("Event: Saturday Spring Preview Tournament"), 320, XXL. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:1196 (`.ch-th--phone .ch-th-act__facts > div`, `96px minmax(0,1fr)`), :1126 (`overflow-wrap: anywhere`) Wrong: The value column is about 100px. At XXL "Tournament" is wider than that, and `anywhere` breaks it as "Tourname / nt". A 96px label column at XXL also wraps "Players" and "When" awkwardly. Fix: At ≤374 or XXL stack the label above the value (`grid-template-columns: 1fr`, label in `--ch-type-13` secondary). Use `overflow-wrap: break-word` instead of `anywhere`.
- **MOB-ASK-8 Ranked list name column is about 52px at 320, so names break mid-word** [P1] Ask v1 rank answer ("Who's improving most"), 320, default and XXL. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:638 (`.ch-th--phone .ch-th-rank li`, `14px minmax(0,1.4fr) minmax(36px,1fr) auto`), :573 (`overflow-wrap: anywhere`) Wrong: 208 − 14 − value (~40) − 3 gaps of 8 leaves about 130px for name plus bar, split 1.4:1, so the name gets about 76px at default and about 52px once the value grows at XXL. "Christopherson" breaks as "Christophe / rson". Fix: Below a container width of 260, give the name its own row above the bar. Otherwise use `overflow-wrap: break-word` with `text-overflow: ellipsis` on one line and the full name in `title` and `aria-label`.
- **MOB-ASK-9 Answer mark indent takes 48px of every phone answer** [P1] Ask v1, every answer, 320/375. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-thread.css:90 (`.ch-th-mark` 36x36) plus the 12px row gap; :144 (`.ch-th--phone .ch-th-card` padding 12 16) Wrong: This is the root cause of MOB-ASK-1, 2, 7 and 8. At 320 the answer card is 208px of a 288px column, so 28% of the width goes to a repeated avatar. Messages and the ask package phone stills put the avatar once above the turn, not as a left gutter on every card. Fix: At ≤414 move `.ch-th-mark` into the turn's header row (24px, `--ch-space-2` gap) and let the cards span the whole column. This gives back 48px on every answer.
- **MOB-ASK-10 Keyboard-up phone chrome leaves about 170px of thread on an iPhone SE** [P1] Ask, coach, 375x667 (SE 3), keyboard up, default Text Size; worse at XXL. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Ask.tsx:343–346 (`.ch-ak-top` with CoachHelmTabs, or AllowanceMeter for a player) and the scope line and dock below Wrong: The stack is status bar plus topbar (~70), Board|Ask tabs (8 + 44 + 8 = 60), scope line (~24), dock (~76) and keyboard with QuickType (~300). That leaves about 137–170px for the conversation, about two lines of an answer. A player's AllowanceMeter card (padding 12 16) is taller still. Nothing collapses when the composer is focused. Fix: When the composer is focused on coarse pointers (`:has(.ch-ak-dock textarea:focus)`), hide `.ch-ak-top` and the scope line (`display:none` or a transform with reduced-motion fallback), or let them scroll with the thread instead of sitting sticky.
- **MOB-ASK-11 The "+" menu blurs the composer, so the keyboard drops and comes back** [P1] Ask composer on phone, keyboard up, tapping "+". File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Composer.tsx:350–372 (the `+` Menu trigger has no `onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()}`, unlike the `@` button at :454); src/clubhouse/ui/Menu.tsx:140 (rAF focuses the first menuitem) Wrong: The tap moves focus to the trigger, then Menu focuses its first item, so the textarea blurs and the keyboard slides down (~250ms). Choosing an item calls focusEnd, which raises the keyboard again. The menu also opens in the space the keyboard leaves, then the keyboard covers it. The whole thing looks like a glitch. Fix: Add the same `onMouseDown` preventDefault to the `+` trigger, and pass a Menu prop (e.g. `focusFirst={finePointer}`) so it does not move focus to an item on coarse pointers.
- **MOB-QC-8 Receipt row leaves the receipt text about 62px at 320 and about 23px at XXL** [P1] Quick chat task receipt ("Added Wedge Distance Control (80-120 yards) to Tuesday" with Undo and Open), 320; XXL. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:192 (`.ch-qcs .rcpt`, `26px minmax(0,1fr) auto`), :199 (`.rcpt__a` gap 2), :200 (`.tbtn` padding 0 8, 15px strong) Wrong: At 320 the panel is 300 wide. After body margin and padding (36) and task padding (24) the row is 240. Icon 26, two gaps 16, Undo ~80, Open ~54 leaves about 62px for the receipt text, which wraps to 5–6 lines. At XXL the buttons grow to about 175px and the text gets about 23px, one or two letters per line. Fix: Below a container width of 300 (or at XXL), wrap `.rcpt__a` to its own row (`grid-template-areas: "icon text" ". actions"`).
- **MOB-QC-9 Phone panel sits under the notch in mini landscape** [P1] Quick chat, iPhone 12/13 mini landscape 812x375 (phone layout), notch side either way. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:339 (`.ch-qcs.is-phone .qc { left: 10px; right: 10px }`) Wrong: In landscape the side insets are about 50px, so with `left:10px` the panel's first 40px (the CoachHelm mark, the close button on the other side, the start of every line) is under the sensor housing. Fix: `left: max(10px, env(safe-area-inset-left)); right: max(10px, env(safe-area-inset-right))`.
- **MOB-QC-10 Panel stays open across navigation, so "Looking at" changes mid-task** [P1] Quick chat on phone, panel open, then Safari edge-swipe back or a link in an answer. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatProvider.tsx:31–81 (no effect closes `open` on a `pathname` change; `looking` at :39 is recomputed from the new path) Wrong: An edge swipe or tapped link changes the page behind while the panel stays open. The "Looking at" context and the quick starts (`quickStarts(pathname)`, QuickChatSession.tsx:239) silently switch to the new page in the middle of a task, so a confirm prepared for one player can show another page's context. Fix: In the provider, `useEffect(() => setOpen(false), [pathname])`, or freeze `looking` while a run or confirm is pending.
- **MOB-ASK-12 Artifact push ignores the side insets in mini landscape** [P2] Ask artifact push, 812x375 mini landscape (phone layout). File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:141 (`.ch-ak-push { inset: 0; padding-top: 48px }`), :139 (`.apb` padding 16) Wrong: The body and dock have 16px side padding with no safe-area term, so the content starts under the notch (inset about 50px). The push is full-bleed fixed, so the shell's own inset padding does not reach it. Fix: `padding-left: max(16px, env(safe-area-inset-left)); padding-right: max(16px, env(safe-area-inset-right))` on `.apb` and `.apdock`.
- **MOB-ASK-13 Phone composer placeholder wraps to two lines at 320** [P2] Ask composer, first turn, 320; 375 at XXL. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Composer.tsx:348 (phone placeholder 'Ask about a player, a stat, or the week') Wrong: The text area is about 124px at 320 (320 − 24 dock padding − about 172 for the +, @ and send buttons and padding). The 40-character placeholder wraps to two lines and the empty composer grows to two rows. QC-6 covers the Quick chat placeholder only. Fix: Use a shorter phone placeholder ("Ask CoachHelm") at ≤374, or `textarea::placeholder { white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis }` (Safari honours this on a single-row textarea).
- **MOB-ASK-14 Chat settings sheet ignores its 0.62 detent** [P2] Ask, Chat settings on phone, all widths. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Ask.tsx:336 (`<Modal detent={0.62}>`); src/clubhouse/styles/controls.css:1858 (detent rules only under `main[data-ch-crh='on']`) Wrong: The detent CSS applies only inside the CRH lane, so in Ask the sheet opens at its default height (near full height). The page behind is no longer visible, so it does not look like a medium-detent iOS sheet. Fix: Scope the detent rules to `.ch-modal[data-ch-detent]` in general, or add `main[data-ch-ask]` to the selector.
- **MOB-ASK-15 Scope line truncates the important part (the time window) at XXL** [P2] Ask v2 scope line ("Demo University Golf · all players · last 30 days"), 320/375, XXL. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:254–263 (`.ch-ak-scope` nowrap, ellipsis) Wrong: The ellipsis cuts from the end, so at 320 XXL it reads "Demo University Golf · all pla…". The window that tells the coach what the answer covers is lost first. Fix: Split into spans with the team name `min-width:0; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis` and the window `flex:none`, or allow two lines (`white-space: normal`, `-webkit-line-clamp: 2`).
- **MOB-ASK-16 Citation chip breaks the prose line spacing, and its hit area covers the lines above and below** [P2] Ask v2 answer prose with citations, all widths, worse at XXL. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-ask-v2.css:41 (`.cite` inline-flex, padding 4 8, 13px strong), :42 (`.cite::after { inset: -12px 0 }`) Wrong: The chip is about 26px tall inside 21px prose lines, so every line with a citation is about 5px taller and the paragraph spacing is uneven. The 12px vertical hit area reaches into the words of the lines above and below, so taps meant for a link or for selecting text open the citation. Fix: Use `padding: 1px 6px; line-height: 1; vertical-align: baseline` so the chip fits a 21px line, and limit `::after` to `inset: -4px -4px`. Give the citations a 44pt target as a footer row under the answer, not inline.
- **MOB-QC-11 Undo and Open are 2px apart** [P2] Quick chat receipt, all phone widths. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:199 (`.ch-qcs .rcpt__a { gap: 2px }`), :200 (`.tbtn`) Wrong: Two 44pt targets with opposite effects (Undo reverses, Open navigates away) are 2px apart, so a slightly off tap does the other action. Fix: `gap: var(--ch-space-2)` (8px), or a hairline divider between them.
- **MOB-QC-12 Tone and option chips' 44px hit areas overlap between wrapped rows** [P2] Quick chat message-tone chips and question options, wrapping to two or more rows at 320/375, any Text Size. File: src/clubhouse/styles/quick-chat.css:153–154 (`.mtone` gap 6, buttons about 30px tall), :241–242 (`.opts` gap 6, min-height 36), :334 (`::before` 44x44 hit area) Wrong: The rows are 36px (30 + 6) and 42px (36 + 6) apart, but each hit area is 44px tall, so hit areas overlap by 8px and 2px. The chip later in the DOM wins, so a tap on the bottom edge of a first-row chip picks the chip below it. Fix: Set the row gap to `calc(44px - chip height)`: `.mtone { row-gap: 14px }`, `.opts { row-gap: 8px }`. Or make the chips themselves `min-height: 44px` and drop the `::before`.
- **MOB-ASK-17 Plan sheet "Ask about…" field has no focus ring, a 28px send button and no return key behaviour** [P2] Ask, practice plan card sheet, ask field, all widths, keyboard up. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:686–694 (`.ask-in input` `outline: 0`, min-height 28), :697 (`.ask-in button` 28x28, no hit area); TSX src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:603, 646 (no `enterKeyHint`, `onKeyDown`, `autoCapitalize`) Wrong: The send button is 28x28 with no `::after` hit area, under the 44pt rule. With `outline:0` and no `:focus-visible` ring, a keyboard or VoiceOver user cannot see which field has focus. The return key reads "return" and does nothing. Fix: Use the shared composer send button (or add `::after { inset: -8px }`). Add `:focus-within` on `.ask-in` with `--ch-focus-ring`. Add `enterKeyHint="send"` and submit on Enter.
- **MOB-QC-13 VoiceOver reads streaming answers and typed titles letter by letter** [P2] Quick chat with VoiceOver on, any width. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:233 (`aria-live="polite"` on all of `.qc__inner`); src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/TaskCard.tsx:16–25, 148, 156 (`useTyped` char-by-char title in `<b aria-label={title}>`) Wrong: The live region wraps the whole conversation, so every streamed token and every frame of the typed title is a DOM mutation. VoiceOver queues partial announcements ("Mov", "Moving Sat", …) and then reads the whole region again. On a `<b>`, `aria-label` is ignored (it is not a labelled role), so the typed fragments are what gets read. Fix: Remove `aria-live` from `.qc__inner`. Add a visually hidden `role="status"` that gets the finished text once per message or step. Render the typed title `aria-hidden` with a `.ch-sr-only` full title next to it.
- **MOB-ASK-18 role="log" over the streaming thread repeats announcements, with nested live steps** [P2] Ask v1 with VoiceOver, while an answer streams. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/Thread.tsx:189 (`role="log"` on `.ch-th-col`), plus the nested `aria-live` on the step list Wrong: `role=log` is an implicit polite live region, and the streamed answer mutates inside it on every token, as does the nested steps region. VoiceOver on iOS announces fragments and can read the step list twice, once from each region. Fix: Keep `role="log"` but mark the streaming message `aria-busy="true"` until it finishes. Remove the nested `aria-live` on the steps, or move it out of the log.
- **MOB-QC-14 The panel dialog is not modal for VoiceOver** [P2] Quick chat with VoiceOver, phone. File: src/clubhouse/shell/quick-chat/QuickChatSession.tsx:212 (`role="dialog"`, no `aria-modal`, page not `inert`) Wrong: Tab is trapped (AC-QC-PNL-08), but VoiceOver swipe navigation is not limited by Tab handling. A right swipe leaves the panel and reads the page behind the scrim. The scrim is `aria-hidden` and the page is not, so the panel does not behave as a modal for VoiceOver. Fix: Add `aria-modal="true"` and set `inert` on the shell's main content while the panel is open, the same mechanism `ui/Modal` uses.
- **MOB-ASK-19 Artifact push is a dialog with no modality and no focus move** [P2] Ask artifact push with VoiceOver, phone. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/Artifact.tsx:77 (`role="dialog"`, no `aria-modal`, no initial focus) Wrong: VoiceOver focus stays on the answer button that opened the push, which is now covered. The first right swipe reads the hidden conversation behind it. Nothing returns focus to the opener when the push closes. Fix: Render through `PhoneScreen` (see MOB-ASK-5), or focus the `PhoneTop` title on mount, make the thread `inert`, and restore focus to the opener on close.
- **MOB-ASK-20 Brief question options are announced as a radio group with no radios** [P2] Ask v2 brief card questions with VoiceOver. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/chat/parts/BriefCard.tsx:64 (`role={q.multi ? 'group' : 'radiogroup'}` around buttons with `aria-pressed`) Wrong: VoiceOver says "radio group" and then reads each option as "toggle button, selected". The rotor's radio navigation finds nothing, and arrow-key roving (implied by radiogroup) is not wired. Fix: For single choice, use `role="radio"` and `aria-checked` on each button with roving tabindex, or keep `aria-pressed` and use `role="group"` for both cases.

### iPhone: Practice, Documents, Messages (40)
- **MOB-MSG-1 Opening a photo or file from Details is blocked as a pop-up in iPhone Safari** [P1] Details > Photos / Files tiles, all widths, all Text Sizes, light and dark. Keyboard down.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/files.ts:41-51 (`open`). Used by details-v2.tsx PhotoTile and the file rows (~:248-320). Wrong: `open` awaits `api.attachments(f.messageId)` (:43) and only then calls `window.open(hit.url, "_blank", …)` (:50). That is outside the tap's user activation, so Mobile Safari silently blocks the new tab, and nothing happens on tap. The repo already has the fix pattern: `announcements.tsx:129-150` opens the window inside the tap, with the comment "The tab opens inside the tap, or Safari blocks it". Hub's `TeamHub.tsx:293` does the same. Fix: inside the click, call `const w = window.open("about:blank","_blank")`. After the await, set `w.location.href = hit.url`, or call `w.close()` on a miss. Better still, reuse the announcements helper.
- **MOB-MSG-2 There are no message actions on touch at ≥821 (iPhone landscape 844/932, iPad)** [P1] thread, 844x390 and 932x430 landscape (desktop tree), any Text Size, light and dark.. File:  - src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:1162 (long press only when `onActions`), :1350-1390 (`.ch-msv-hoverbar`) - src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:5693-5699 (`@media (hover: hover)`), :4510-4516 (time on `:hover, :focus-within`) Wrong:  - The desktop tree passes no `onActions`, so the long press is off. - The v2 hoverbar (react, reply, edit, delete, copy) appears only under `hover:hover`. - iOS fires no `contextmenu` on a long press. - The legacy `.ch-ms-msg__tools` had a `hover:none` fallback (messages.css:682-686), but the v2 bar has none. - The result: on an iPhone turned sideways, a touch user cannot react, reply, edit or delete. The time label shows only through a sticky `:hover` after a tap. Fix: under `(hover: none)`, enable `useLongPress` and the HoldLift whenever `matchMedia('(pointer:coarse)')`, even in the desktop tree. Alternatively, add a `@media (hover:none)` rule that shows the hoverbar on `:focus-within` and give the bubble `tabIndex=0`.
- **MOB-MSG-3 Landscape iPhone gets the desktop composer, and rotating throws away composer state** [P1] thread, 844x390, keyboard up, rotating between portrait and landscape. All Text Sizes.. File:  - src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:2078-2086 (`!phone` Enter sends), :2178 (`enterKeyHint`), :2186-2192 (hint) - MessagesScreen.tsx (swaps the MessagesPhone and MessagesDesktop trees on `useChPhone`) Wrong:  - At 844 the composer has `phone=false`. The iOS Return key therefore sends (there is no way to type a new line), and the hint "Enter to send · Shift + Enter for a new line" is drawn on a device with no Shift+Enter. - The desktop tree has no `keyboardAware` / visualViewport fit, so in Safari the keyboard (≈160–200px of a 390px-tall viewport) covers the composer. - Crossing 820 unmounts the whole tree. Picked files, `replyToId`, `editing`, the scroll position and focus are all lost. Only the text draft survives, through `api.drafts`. Fix:  - Key the `phone` prop on `(pointer:coarse)` rather than width, so Return is a line break and the hint is hidden. - Lift files, reply and edit state into the draft store (`api.drafts`) next to the text. - Or make the phone tree's breakpoint `(max-width:820px), (max-height:500px) and (pointer:coarse)`.
- **MOB-MSG-4 About 34px of home-indicator padding stays above the keyboard in Mobile Safari (extends MSG-3)** [P1] thread and New message, 320–430 portrait, keyboard up, Safari (not Capacitor). Light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:2110-2122 (`.ch-msp-thread .ch-ms-comp`, `.ch-msp-new .ch-ms-comp`) Wrong:  - The composer pads `calc(8px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`, and the drop to 8px is only under `body.keyboard-open`, which only Capacitor sets. - In Safari, PhoneScreen fits to the visual viewport with `[data-ch-vv]` (shell.css:1577), but `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` stays 34px. So a band of about 34px of composer background sits between the pill and the keyboard. - MSG-3 found the composer jump. This is the leftover gap once the fit works. Fix: add `.ch-pscreen[data-ch-vv] .ch-ms-comp { padding-bottom: 8px; }` next to the `body.keyboard-open` rule.
- **MOB-MSG-5 The keyboard drops after every send on iPhone** [P1] thread composer, all widths, keyboard up, Safari.. File:  - src/clubhouse/screens/messages/NotifyAnyway.tsx:97 (send `<button onClick={() => onSend(false)}>`) - src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:2056 (`ta.current?.focus()` after the await) Wrong:  - The send key has no `onPointerDown` / `onMouseDown` preventDefault, so a tap blurs the textarea first and iOS lowers the keyboard. - The refocus at :2056 runs after `await` (outside the gesture), and iOS will not reopen the keyboard from a programmatic focus outside a gesture. - Each message therefore costs a tap back into the field, and the thread jumps twice as the viewport grows and shrinks. - The `+` key (ComposerPlusV2) has the same issue. Fix: add `onPointerDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()}` to the send button and the `+` trigger. Focus then never leaves the textarea, and the post-await `focus()` becomes a no-op.
- **MOB-MSG-6 A long press while typing drops the keyboard under a lift measured with the keyboard up** [P1] thread, 320–430, keyboard up, long press on a bubble.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/hold-lift.tsx:73-74 (`rbar…querySelector('button')?.focus({preventScroll:true})`) Wrong:  - On open, focus moves to the first reaction, which blurs the textarea, so iOS lowers the keyboard. - The thread then grows by the keyboard height (≈290–336px), but the lifted copy, the reaction bar and the menu stay at the rect measured before. They end up floating 300px above the bubble or off the top. - Nothing re-measures on `visualViewport` resize. Fix:  - Either `blur()` the active field and wait one `visualViewport` resize before measuring. - Or re-measure the rect on `visualViewport.resize` while the lift is open. - Or skip moving focus when `pointerType==='touch'` and the textarea is focused.
- **MOB-MSG-7 The date pill sits at a fixed 74px, under the notch bar** [P1] thread scroll, all portrait widths, all Text Sizes, light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:4518-4521 (`[data-ch-ms-v2] .ch-msv-datepill { top: 74px }`) Wrong:  - The phone bar is `env(safe-area-inset-top)` (47–59) plus `.ch-msp-who` 44, which is ≈91–103px. - At 74px the floating "Today" pill is drawn under, or half under, the translucent bar. At XXL the bar is taller still (see MOB-MSG-14). - The desktop uses 140px. There is no phone value. Fix: in the phone thread, set `top: calc(env(safe-area-inset-top) + var(--ch-phone-topbar-h) + 8px)`, or measure the bar's height into a CSS variable.
- **MOB-MSG-8 At XXL the drag-for-times stops 13–20px short, so times stay clipped** [P1] thread, drag left to show times, 320–430, largest non-AX and XXL, light and dark.. File:  - src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:571 (`useThreadDragTimes(scroller, !!api.redesign, threadReduced)`, with no `k`) - use-thread-drag-times.ts:7, :26 (`max = TIMES_DRAG_PX * k`, `k` defaults to 1) - messages.css:4490-4502 (`.ch-msv-time { right: calc(-64px * k); width: calc(52px * k) }`, -68k at 414–820) Wrong: the time column is placed by the Text Size factor `k` (1.353 at XXL → 86.6–92px), but the drag is capped at 58px because `k` is never passed. At XXL, "10:42 AM" therefore shows only about 2/3 of the way. Fix: read `--ch-type-k` (e.g. `useDynamicTypeScale()`) and pass it as the 4th argument. Use 64 or 68 to match the CSS offset at ≥414.
- **MOB-MSG-9 Pin speech bubbles overlap at 320 and 375** [P1] inbox Pins card with 3 pins, 320, 375 and 390, default Text Size and up, light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:4241-4330 (`.ch-msv-pins` 3-column grid with padding 6/8/8 and gap 6; `.ch-msv-bubblet { left:50%; max-width:92px; transform:translateX(-12%) }`; only `:nth-child(3)` is right-anchored). PIN_LIMIT is 3 (`pinned.ts:10`). Wrong:  - Pin 2's bubblet runs right from its centre into pin 3's right-anchored bubblet. - Computed overlap is about 37px at 320, 9.5px at 375 and 2px at 390. The "said" previews draw over each other, and the result is worse at larger Text Sizes. Fix:  - Below 400px, centre the bubblet (`translateX(-50%)`) and cap it at `calc(100% - 4px)` of its cell. - Or hide the bubblet below 360 and show it on press.
- **MOB-MSG-10 The poll sheet's Cancel and Send scroll away under the Safari keyboard** [P1] composer `+` > Poll, 320–430, keyboard up, Safari. All Text Sizes.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/polls.tsx:657+ (PollSheet in `Modal`); src/clubhouse/styles/controls.css:617-623 and :1555-1590 (fit only under `body.keyboard-open`) Wrong:  - Modal and FormSheet fit the keyboard only under Capacitor's `body.keyboard-open`. Safari has no visualViewport path for sheets. - With a choice field focused, iOS scrolls the layout viewport, and the sheet's header (Cancel / Send in the redesign) goes off the top. On 320, the "Add choice" row sits under the keyboard. Fix: reuse `useSafariKeyboardFit` in Modal/FormSheet. Set `--ch-vv-h` and `max-height: var(--ch-vv-h)` on the sheet, as PhoneScreen does at shell.css:1577.
- **MOB-MSG-11 Details hosts Search but isn't keyboard-aware, and Search doesn't take focus** [P1] Details > Search in conversation, 320–430, keyboard up, Safari.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:947 (`<PhoneScreen … className="ch-msp-details">`, with no `keyboardAware`); details-v2.tsx:163-176 (ConversationSearch, no autofocus) Wrong:  - The screen isn't fitted to the visual viewport, so iOS pans the page when the field focuses. The page bar scrolls off, and the results list sits under the keyboard. - Opening Search needs a second tap to place the caret. Fix: add `keyboardAware` to the Details PhoneScreen. Focus the field when the search opens (`autoFocus`, inside the opening tap).
- **MOB-MSG-12 Landscape 812 (phone tree) ignores the side safe areas** [P1] thread, inbox and composer, 812x375 landscape (iPhone X/11 Pro/12-13 mini), all Text Sizes.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css. There is no `safe-area-inset-left/right` anywhere; only bottom at :1357 and :2115, and top at :5816. Thread padding is `0 12px` at :2039, and the composer is 8px at :2115. Wrong: with `viewport-fit=cover`, the notch side has a 44px inset. Bubbles, the avatar column, the `+` key and the inbox rows run under the sensor housing. Fix: on `.ch-msp-thread .ch-ms-msgs`, `.ch-ms-comp` and the inbox list, use `padding-inline: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-left))` and `max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-right))`.
- **MOB-PRAC-1 Plan sheet times print "10:30 AM" in a 46px column and 40px rotation headers** [P1] Practice plan sheet (coach), 320–430, default Text Size and up, light (the sheet is light only).. File:  - src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/sheet/plan-sheet-model.ts:32 (`clock = formatWith(d,{hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit',timeZone})`, en-US by lib/dates.ts:114) - src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css `.drow { grid-template-columns: 46px … }` (`.pt b` type-14-strong) and `.pp-rot` (`repeat(n,40px)`, inline in PracticePlanSheet.tsx) Wrong:  - "10:30 AM" at 14px strong is ≈62px. It wraps onto 2 lines in the 46px column and overflows the 40px turn headers into the next turn. - Three places disagree: - PlayerPractice strips AM/PM (data/practice-plan.ts:82). - PlanCard's clock has none (PlanCard.tsx:88-93). - The sheet keeps it. Fix: use the same AM/PM-less clock as `data/practice-plan.ts:82` in plan-sheet-model, and show the period once in the sheet header ("Time" row :83). Size `.drow` to `calc(46px * var(--ch-type-k))`.
- **MOB-PRAC-2 Print on iPhone leaves a second plan sheet rendered under the app** [P1] plan page > Print, iPhone Safari and the standalone home-screen app.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PrintPlan.tsx (appends `div.ch-cp-print-root` to body; `await document.fonts.ready` + 2 rAFs, then `window.print()`; cleanup on `afterprint` only); src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:83-88 (`@media print` only) Wrong:  - `.ch-cp-print-root` has no screen rule hiding it, so the whole sheet is in the page under the app (scroll height grows, VoiceOver reads it twice). - `window.print()` runs after awaits, outside the tap's activation. iOS may ignore it, or show the print sheet late. - If `afterprint` never fires (often in standalone mode), the root stays for the rest of the session, and a second Print appends a second copy. Fix:  - Add `@media screen { .ch-cp-print-root { display:none } }`. - Call `window.print()` synchronously in the tap, after preloading fonts when the page loads. - Remove any existing root before appending, and also clean up on `focus` / `visibilitychange`.
- **MOB-PRAC-3 "Ask about this block/station" is covered by the Safari keyboard** [P1] plan card sheet (block / station) > Ask field, 320–430, keyboard up, Safari.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:596-606, :639-650 (`form.ask-in` input); src/clubhouse/styles/controls.css:1555-1590 (FormSheet keyboard fit only under `body.keyboard-open`) Wrong:  - The field sits at the bottom of a FormSheet, and the sheet has no Safari keyboard fit. The keyboard covers the field and Done, and iOS pans the page, so the sheet's grabber and title go off the top. - The field has no `enterKeyHint="send"`, so the Return key reads "return" for a field that submits. Fix: give FormSheet the visualViewport fit (see MOB-MSG-10). Add `enterKeyHint="send"` and `autoCapitalize="sentences"` to the input.
- **MOB-MSG-13 Thread side padding stays 12px at ≥414** [P2] thread, 414/428/430, all Text Sizes.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:2034-2040 (`.ch-msp-thread .ch-ms-msgs { padding: 0 12px }`, with no ≥414 rule) Wrong: the spec gives 12 at 390 and 16 at 430. The code holds 12 at every width, while the jump key already moves to 18 at ≥414. Fix: `@media (min-width:414px) { .ch-msp-thread .ch-ms-msgs { padding: 0 16px } }`.
- **MOB-MSG-14 The thread bar's name block overflows its fixed 44px at large Text Sizes** [P2] thread bar, 320–430, largest non-AX and XXL.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:1941-1983 (`.ch-msp-who { height: 44px }`; `b` type-15-strong, `em` type-12, lead 1.1) Wrong: at XXL the name and subtitle are ≈27 + 2 + 17.9 = 47px inside a fixed 44px box, so the subtitle is clipped or overlaps the hairline. The spec grows `.who` to 53.7px at XXL. Fix: change `height:44px` to `min-height:44px`, and let the bar grow with `--ch-type-k`.
- **MOB-MSG-15 Phone bubble column is 82% wide, the spec says 76%** [P2] thread, all phone widths.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:2052-2053 (`.ch-msp-thread .ch-ms-msg__col { max-width: 82% }`) Wrong: long bubbles reach 6% further than the design. At 320 that leaves about 40px of gutter on the opposite side instead of about 60px, so who said what is harder to scan. Fix: `max-width: 76%`.
- **MOB-MSG-16 A double tap anywhere in the bubble stack likes the message, even on links, polls and images** [P2] thread, all widths, touch.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:1222-1229 (`toggleLikeOnDouble`, 300ms), set on `.ch-ms-msg__stack` at :1323 Wrong: the handler isn't filtered by target. Two quick taps on poll choices, a link, an image or the reply quote also toggle a Like. Changing a poll vote quickly likes the poll. Fix: return early when `e.target.closest('a,button,input,label,[role=button],.ch-ms-poll,.ch-ms-quote,img')`.
- **MOB-MSG-17 New message and announcement fields lack phone keyboard hints, and Return does nothing** [P2] New message (To, group Name) and New announcement (Title), 320–430, keyboard up.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:1381-1388 (To), :1394 (Name), :1528 (announcement Title) Wrong:  - The To field has only `autoComplete="off"`. It has no `autoCorrect="off"`, `spellCheck={false}`, `autoCapitalize="none"` or `enterKeyHint`, so iOS autocorrects people's names as you type them. - Return does nothing, rather than picking the top match. - The To field isn't focused on open, so an extra tap is needed. - Name and Title have no `enterKeyHint="next"` and no `autoCapitalize="words"`/`"sentences"`. Fix:  - On the To field, add `autoCorrect="off" spellCheck={false} autoCapitalize="none" enterKeyHint="next" autoFocus`, with Enter choosing the first match. - On the Title field, add `enterKeyHint="next"` and focus the body on Enter.
- **MOB-MSG-18 The Search key leaves the keyboard over the results** [P2] inbox search and conversation search, all widths, keyboard up.. File: src/clubhouse/ui/SearchField.tsx:34-35 (`onKeyDown` handles only Escape; `enterKeyHint="search"`) Wrong:  - The Search key does nothing, so the keyboard keeps covering about half of the results and must be swiped down. - There is no `autoCorrect="off"` / `spellCheck={false}`, so searching for a name gets autocorrected. Fix: on Enter, call `e.currentTarget.blur()`. Add `autoCorrect="off" spellCheck={false}`.
- **MOB-MSG-19 A short inbox shows a band of a different colour below the list** [P2] inbox with 0–4 conversations, 320–430, light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:4201 (`.ch-msv-inbox { background: var(--ch-grouped-bg) }`); src/clubhouse/styles/shell.css:1217 (canvas `padding-bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 20px)` on `--ch-bg-page`) Wrong:  - The inbox paints grouped-bg (#f2f0e9 light, #0e120f dark) only to the end of its content. - The phone canvas below it is bg-page (#ede9df light, #111512 dark), and the band is 20px plus the inset. It is visible above the dock, and the same thing happens on the empty state. Fix: give `.ch-msv-inbox` `min-height: 100%` (flex:1 in the canvas). Or set the canvas background to grouped-bg on the Messages route.
- **MOB-MSG-20 Announcement folds keep a hover tint after a tap** [P2] inbox Announcements section, all phone widths, light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:3284 (`.ch-ms-fold:hover`, not gated) Wrong: iOS applies `:hover` on tap and keeps it until another element is tapped, so a folded announcement stays tinted green 7% as if selected. Fix: wrap the rule in `@media (hover: hover)`.
- **MOB-MSG-21 The attach menu sits under the bar on a 320 SE with the keyboard up, and tapping outside goes through** [P2] composer `+` menu, 320x568, keyboard up (visual viewport ≈ 260px), all Text Sizes.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/ComposerPlusV2.tsx:27-37 (document `pointerdown` close); src/clubhouse/styles/messages.css:4716-4790 (menu `bottom: calc(100% + 13px)`) Wrong:  - The menu opens upward from the composer with no max-height and no flip. Once the keyboard takes ≈300px, the top item (Photos) is under the thread bar, and worse at XXL. - The outside `pointerdown` closes the menu but doesn't stop the tap. A tap meant to dismiss the menu also opens a bubble or likes it. Fix:  - Set `max-height: calc(var(--ch-vv-h) - var(--ch-ms-comp-h) - 60px)` with `overflow:auto`. - In the close handler, call `preventDefault()` / swallow the next click, as SendCluster's `onClickCapture` does (NotifyAnyway.tsx:52).
- **MOB-MSG-22 The photo picker can't send videos, and "File" opens the same iOS sheet** [P2] composer `+` > Photos / File, iPhone.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/ComposerPlusV2.tsx:88 (`accept="image/*"`), :89 (File: no `accept`) Wrong:  - Photos excludes videos (`image/*`), so swing clips from the camera roll can't be picked there. - "File" with no accept shows the same Photo Library / Take Photo / Choose File sheet, so the two menu items look the same on iOS. Fix: Photos `accept="image/*,video/*"`. For File, use `accept` with document types (`.pdf,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx,.csv,.txt,application/*`) so iOS goes straight to Files.
- **MOB-MSG-23 A tapped photo leaves the app for a new Safari tab** [P2] thread image attachment, all widths.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:918-943 (ImageAttachment `<a target="_blank">` around `<img>`) Wrong:  - Tapping an image opens a new tab with a signed URL. In a standalone home-screen app that jumps out to Safari with no way back. - The double-tap-to-like on the stack (MOB-MSG-16) also opens the tab. - Pinch, swipe and close follow none of the app's patterns. Fix: replace the anchor with a button that opens a `Modal` / full-screen PhoneScreen holding the image. Keep "Open original" inside the viewer.
- **MOB-MSG-24 Opening a thread mounts 200 bubbles, each fetching signed attachment URLs** [P2] a busy team thread, on cellular.. File: src/hooks/golf/use-golf-messages.ts:783-793 (`.limit(200)`, no older paging); src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:945-1000 (Attachments fetch on mount, plus a 45-minute re-sign timer per instance) Wrong:  - All 200 messages render at once with no virtualization. Each one with attachments fires its own signing request and keeps its own timer. - On an older iPhone over LTE, the first paint and the scroll-to-end wait on this. Memory grows with images. - Older history beyond 200 can't be reached. Fix:  - Batch the signing per thread, one call for visible message ids. - Lazy-sign attachments with IntersectionObserver. - Page older messages on scroll-to-top.
- **MOB-MSG-25 The inbox row's leading swipe fights Safari's back-swipe at the left edge** [P2] inbox rows, Safari (not standalone), all widths.. File: src/clubhouse/ui/SwipeRow.tsx (pointerdown; no edge exclusion); MessagesView.tsx:420 (leading action) Wrong: a swipe that starts within ≈20px of the left edge both reveals the row's leading action and starts Safari's back gesture. The thread's own drag uses `BACK_SWIPE_EDGE 24` (use-thread-drag-times.ts), but SwipeRow doesn't. Fix: in SwipeRow, ignore a pointerdown with `clientX < 24` (shared constant with use-thread-drag-times).
- **MOB-MSG-26 Swipe-to-reply gives no feedback while dragging** [P2] thread bubble, swipe right to reply, touch.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesView.tsx:1095-1101 (`useLongPress` resolves the swipe on `pointerup` only) Wrong: the bubble doesn't follow the finger and no reply arrow appears, so the user can't tell a reply swipe is happening until they let go. Short swipes do nothing, with no hint. Fix: on `pointermove`, apply `translateX(min(dx, 64px))` and show the reply glyph once past the threshold. Spring back on release (`--ch-dur-quick`). Skip it under Reduce Motion.
- **MOB-MSG-27 The jump-to-latest key doesn't re-check when the keyboard opens or the screen resizes** [P2] thread, keyboard up or down while scrolled near the end.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/thread-v2.tsx (ThreadJumpKey: `far` is updated on `scroll` and mount only) Wrong: when the keyboard shrinks the scroller, the reader can end up far from the end with no scroll event. The jump key stays hidden, or stays shown after the keyboard closes. Fix: also recompute `far` in a ResizeObserver on the scroller. use-thread-anchor.ts already has one, so share it.
- **MOB-MSG-28 Details "Files" jumps instead of scrolling smoothly** [P2] Details, tapping the Files count, all widths.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/messages/MessagesPhone.tsx:967 (`scrollIntoView({ block: "start" })`) Wrong: there's no `behavior:"smooth"`, so the screen cuts to the files section. With the translucent bar, the section heading also lands under the bar. Fix: `scrollIntoView({ block: "start", behavior: reduced ? "auto" : "smooth" })`. Add `scroll-margin-top: calc(env(safe-area-inset-top) + var(--ch-phone-topbar-h))` on `.ch-msv-files`.
- **MOB-PRAC-4 Player plan and Plan format keep a 16px edge at ≥414 and show a background seam (extends PP-25)** [P2] player's plan view (`.ch-cpv-ply`) and Plan format (`.ch-cpv-fmt`), 414–430, light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:1082-1086 (`.ch-cpv-fmt, .ch-cpv-ply { padding:16px; background: var(--ch-grouped-bg) }`) Wrong:  - PP-25 covered the coach sheet. The player's view has the same fixed 16px edge, which should be 20 at ≥414. - It paints grouped-bg on `main` while the phone canvas below is bg-page (#ede9df / #111512), so the colour changes where `main` ends. Fix: `padding-inline: var(--ch-page-x-phone)`, and move the grouped-bg to the canvas for these routes (or `min-height:100%`).
- **MOB-PRAC-5 The player's plan view shows two titles** [P2] player > Practice, all phone widths.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/player/PlayerPractice.tsx (LargeTitle "Practice"); src/clubhouse/shell/nav.ts:369 (stand-in bar "Back · Practice plan") Wrong: the stand-in bar says "Practice plan" and the page's large title says "Practice" right under it. That's two names for one screen, and about 52px of vertical space lost at 320. Fix: drop the bar title on this route (nav.ts), or drop the LargeTitle and keep the bar. Use the same word in both places.
- **MOB-PRAC-6 "Now" and "Next" go stale on a long-open phone** [P2] player's plan view, during practice, home-screen app resumed after minutes.. File: src/clubhouse/data/practice-plan.ts:84-85 (`nowAt`/`nextAt` computed once on the server); PlayerPractice.tsx (no timer or `visibilitychange` refresh) Wrong: a player who opens the plan at 3:25 and checks again at 4:10 still sees the 3:25 block marked Now. The labels are wrong for the rest of practice unless the page is reloaded. Fix: compute Now/Next on the client from block starts. Rerun on a 60s interval and on `visibilitychange` (visible).
- **MOB-PRAC-7 Time columns overflow at XXL (`.drow` 46px, `.stop` 52px, `.blk` 54px)** [P2] coach sheet, plan blocks and player stops, 320–430, largest non-AX and XXL.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css `.drow` (46px), :181-215 `.blk` (54px), :1347-1420 `.stop` (52px) Wrong: "10:30" at XXL type-14-strong is ≈52px, and the cells are fixed px. The time wraps or runs into the block title. That is before AM/PM (MOB-PRAC-1). Fix: size the columns as `calc(<px> * var(--ch-type-k))` or `max-content`, and use `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums`.
- **MOB-PRAC-8 Bring and Notes stay two columns at 320** [P2] plan sheet, 320 (and 375 at XXL).. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css `.pp-two` (2 columns, no phone breakpoint); PracticePlanSheet.tsx Wrong: each column is ≈116px at 320. Bring lists and notes wrap one or two words per line, and long equipment names break mid-word. Fix: `@media (max-width: 380px) { .pp-two { grid-template-columns: 1fr } }`, plus the same at large Text Sizes, keyed on `--ch-type-k`.
- **MOB-PRAC-9 Plan format's "See … in it" link is small and wraps next to Undo** [P2] Plan format, after changing the format, 320–430, all Text Sizes.. File: src/clubhouse/styles/coachhelm-plan.css:1279-1292 (`.rlinks button` 44px), :1541 (`.rlinks a`, no min-height); PlanFormat.tsx (`.rlinks`) Wrong: the "See {title} in it" link has no 44pt height and no truncation. With a long plan title it wraps to 2–3 lines beside Undo, and the tap target is the text line (≈20px). Fix: give `.rlinks a` `min-height:44px; display:inline-flex; align-items:center`, plus `overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap; min-width:0`.
- **MOB-PRAC-10 Documents: desktop drop copy, a 32px ⋯ button and an 11px type tag on the phone** [P2] Practice > Documents, 320–430, light and dark.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/hub/parts.tsx:723 (drop copy), :771-783 (⋯ `.ch-hb-iconbtn`); src/clubhouse/styles/hub.css:147-158 (32x32), ~:935 (`.ch-hb-file__ic em`, type-11) Wrong:  - "Drop files to share with the team" shows on a phone, which can't drop files. - The ⋯ button is 32x32 with a 4px gap and no hit slop, so it's easy to open the file by mistake. - The "PDF" tag is 11px. Fix:  - Show "Tap to add files" under `(pointer:coarse)`. - Make the ⋯ 44x44 on the phone (or add a `::before` hit area of inset -6px). - Set the tag to type-12 or larger.
- **MOB-PRAC-11 The folder name field refocuses on every render and lacks keyboard hints** [P2] plan builder > new / rename folder, keyboard up.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/PlanCard.tsx:455-470 (callback ref calls `focus()` on every render) Wrong:  - Every re-render (each keystroke, or a sync) refocuses the field and can move the caret or scroll the sheet. - There's no `autoCapitalize="words"` and no `enterKeyHint="done"`. Fix: focus once with `useEffect(() => ref.current?.focus(), [])` or `autoFocus`. Add `autoCapitalize="words" enterKeyHint="done"`.
- **MOB-PRAC-12 Plan format's file input sits outside the modal sheet** [P2] Plan format > upload a format file, all widths.. File: src/clubhouse/screens/coachhelm/plan/format/PlanFormat.tsx (hidden `<input type=file>` rendered outside the FormSheet `<dialog>`; all three options call `input.click()`) Wrong: `showModal()` makes everything outside the dialog inert. Calling `click()` on an inert input works in current WebKit, but it's fragile, and VoiceOver can't reach the input. The upload is also tied to an element the sheet doesn't own. Fix: render the hidden input inside the FormSheet body.

### Motion system: the rules every lane follows

Each rule names the file in `src/clubhouse/` that already does it best, so fixes reuse it instead of writing new motion.

1. **Tokens only.** No literal `ms`, `s` or `cubic-bezier` in lane CSS or TSX. The scale is press 110, quick 180, base 260, release 280, reveal 520, plus the sampled springs. A sampled spring curve plays **only at the duration it was sampled for**: `--ch-ease-spring-smooth` goes with `--ch-dur-spring-smooth`, and `chSpringCurve(kind)` with its own `.ms`. Never pair a spring curve with a different duration.
   Best: `styles/tokens.css:840-889` plus `:1271-1287`, and `lib/motion.ts` (`CH_DUR`, `chSpringCurve`). Best user of them: `lib/slide-plate.ts:57-68`, whose curve and duration come from one call.
2. **Transform and opacity only.** No `width`, `height`, `left`, `top`, `margin`, `padding` or `grid-template-*` in a transition, a keyframe, WAAPI or a per-frame React render. A value change that moves a mark is a transform from its old box.
   Best: `screens/stats/LegBars.tsx:79-93`: `spanTransform` / `shiftTransform` glide bars on transform while `left`/`width` stay at the target.
3. **Enter and exit.** Every sheet, drawer, push, menu and inspector has both an enter and an exit. It animates from its origin, can be interrupted from where it is, and keeps its content while it leaves.
   - Sheets and drawers: `lib/dialog-lifetime.ts` (rise held for two frames, exit from the live transform, thrown exits keep their velocity, `retainContent`), through `ui/Modal.tsx` / `ui/FormSheet.tsx`.
   - Pushed screens: `shell/PhoneScreen.tsx` plus `lib/nav-motion.ts` (24% underlay `CH_UNDERLAY_SHIFT`, dim, edge-swipe).
   - Menus: `ui/Menu.tsx:180-184` (`CH_POP`, `transformOrigin` from the anchor).
   - Content swaps: `ui/Swap.tsx`, with `resize="instant"` (see MOT-09).
4. **Press feedback on everything tappable.** Keys up to 240 wide scale (`.ch-btn`, `[data-ch-press]`). Rows and cards tint through `[data-ch-pressed]`, landing after `--ch-dur-press-delay` (50ms) and clearing at once.
   Best: `lib/press.ts` (controller) and `styles/home.css:3240-3256` (`a.ch-hm-next[data-ch-pressed]::after`, opacity tint, 50ms delay, press duration).
5. **Reduce Motion means a 180ms opacity fade; Animations off means instant.** Pages and sheets read `useChMotionMode()` (`'full' | 'fade' | 'off'`), never the boolean `useChReducedMotion()`. Under `'fade'` the transform is dropped and opacity fades over `CH_FADE_MS`. Under `'off'` nothing moves. The spinner keeps turning under Reduce Motion (H19 / OQ-18).
   Best: `lib/dialog-lifetime.ts:136-145` (fade out where it stands under `fade`, finish at once under `off`) and `styles/shell.css:2014-2025` (view-transition crossfade). WAAPI or `!important` is needed while the base.css floor stands (MOT-01).
6. **Route and tab transitions are shell-owned.**
   - Phone drill-in: push 462 on the smooth spring, with the underlay at −24% and dimmed. Back: pop. Section tab: instant.
   - Desktop drill-in: crossfade with a 120 out / 180 in gap. Sub-view switch: dim plus rise from the dimmed opacity (`use-view-switch.ts`).
   - No lane writes its own push.
   Best: `shell/RouteFrame.tsx`, `lib/nav-motion.ts`, `styles/shell.css:1940-2025`, `screens/coachhelm/use-view-switch.ts` with `styles/coachhelm.css:1062-1080`.
7. **No first-paint motion, no staggers, no draw-ins on scroll.** Chart marks are drawn at rest. They glide only when the user changes the window, never on a refresh. Digits never roll.
   Best: `lib/motion-glide.ts` (`useGlide` returns the target on first paint). Its React-state-per-frame driver should hand off to transforms (MOT-07).
8. **Gestures carry velocity and rubber-band.**
   Best: `lib/sheet-drag.ts` (`rubberBand`, `FLICK_PX_PER_MS`, `chThrownExit`), `lib/swipe-row.ts`, `shell/PullToRefresh.tsx:20-22`.
9. **One skeleton.** `.ch-skel` with `--ch-dur-shimmer`, inside a `main[aria-busy='true'][aria-label^='Loading']` so the route hold and fade apply. Its shape is the final layout's (an aspect-ratio box for an SVG, not a fixed px height).
   Best: `styles/shell.css:853-866` plus `styles/base.css:280-294`.
10. **Scroll-linked state** comes from an IntersectionObserver sentinel, or failing that a passive, rAF-throttled listener. No per-scroll React state.
    Best existing: `shell/large-title.tsx:100-125` (passive and rAF-throttled). For the stuck state, an IO sentinel is the target.
